Method: EpiCon 通过两个独立训练的 2B 模型（记忆控制器与树式自组织器），将问题级记忆演化与持久经验库连接；控制器跨多次尝试联合优化文本引导与视觉证据并选择性纳入视觉记忆，自组织器分层整合经验并检索规则。

Result: 在覆盖四个多模态任务域的十一个基准上、使用两种测试框架与多种骨干模型评估：冻结经验库仅需一次求解尝试即提升其他系统；第二种框架使原系统宏平均分提升 2.6 分；四种宿主配置下较 No Memory 提升 1.7 至 4.9 分，并将记忆操作时间减少 67% 至 74%。

Conclusion: EpiCon 无需调整宿主模型参数即可实现跨智能体的经验共享与复用，在提升多模态任务表现的同时显著降低记忆操作开销。

Abstract: Agents can learn from past executions, but enabling different agents to reuse and build on one another's experience remains challenging. We introduce EpiCon, a shared multimodal memory framework for agent collective learning without updating host model parameters. EpiCon links question-level memory evolution to a persistent experience bank through two independently trained 2B models: a memory controller and a tree self-organizer. The controller jointly refines textual guidance and visual evidence across attempts and selectively includes visual memory. The self-organizer consolidates lessons hierarchically and retrieves experience and rules for new problems. We evaluate EpiCon on eleven benchmarks spanning four multimodal task domains, using two harnesses and multiple backbones. A frozen bank improves other systems even with a single solving attempt. A second harness raises the original system's macro-average score by 2.6 points across eleven benchmarks. Across four host configurations, EpiCon improves macro-average scores by 1.7 to 4.9 points over No Memory and reduces memory-operation time by 67\% to 74\% relative to backbone-sized memory models.

</details>


### [206] [Rollout-Marginal Distillation for Long-Horizon Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37925)
*Chenjian Gao,Zhihao Hu,Jianqi Ma,Jun Zhang,Weidong Zhang,Tianfan Xue*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Rollout-Marginal Distillation (RMD)，通过独立分块评分结合视频级 DMD，缓解自回归视频扩散中的误差累积与视觉质量退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散虽支持低延迟流式生成，但预测误差在长程 rollout 中不断累积。训练生成器基于自身 rollout 可让其接触不完美历史，然而现有视频级分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）对整段 rollout 联合评分，导致某分块的修正会受过去与未来上下文影响，为维持时间一致性而偏向匹配周围的伪影，视觉质量信号被稀释。

Method: RMD 在保留生成历史用于自回归预测的同时，将每个分块与分块教师（chunk teacher）独立评分，使质量修正不受不完美时间上下文干扰；随后再施加视频级 DMD 以补偿独立分块评分缺失的时间上下文，恢复时间连贯性。

Result: 大量实验表明，RMD 在远超其训练时域的范围外仍能保持高视觉质量，并优于视频级 DMD 基线方法。

Conclusion: RMD 通过“独立分块评分 + 视频级 DMD 恢复连贯性”的两阶段策略，为自回归视频扩散提供了更清晰的视觉质量信号，有效延缓长 rollout 的质量退化。代码与视频结果见 https://cjeen.github.io/RMD。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) video diffusion enables low-latency, streamable video generation, but prediction errors often accumulate over long rollouts. Training the generator on its own rollouts exposes it to these imperfect histories. However, existing video-level distribution matching distillation (DMD) scores the whole rollout jointly. Because a chunk is evaluated together with its past and future, its correction can favor matching artifacts in the surrounding context merely to preserve temporal consistency. To provide a clearer visual-quality signal, we introduce Rollout-Marginal Distillation (RMD). RMD retains the generated history for AR prediction but scores each chunk independently against a chunk teacher, ensuring its quality correction is not compromised by an imperfect temporal context. To compensate for the lack of temporal context in independent chunk scoring, RMD subsequently applies video-level DMD to restore temporal coherence. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RMD maintains high visual quality far beyond its training horizon and outperforms video-level DMD baselines. Code and video results are available at https://cjeen.github.io/RMD

</details>


### [207] [Look Closer: Patch-wise Supervision for AI-Generated Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37937)
*Zhida Zhang,Tao Wu,Siyu Liu,Jie Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究"图像块级监督"用于AI生成图像检测：共享骨干对显式裁剪的图像块分别分类并计算损失，仅在推理时平均各块概率，无需手工残差滤波或学习式图像级融合模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受单块检测启发，作者关注一个核心问题：检测器需要看到多少图像才能保留合成证据？小尺寸RGB区域即使在几乎不揭示完整场景的情况下仍能保留有用的合成线索，因此希望探索显式局部输入与块级监督这一简单组合的有效性。

Method: 方法上采用图像块级监督：共享骨干网络对显式裁剪出的图像块进行分类，每个块获得各自的损失，仅在推理阶段对块概率取平均。该流程既不需要手工设计的残差滤波，也不需要学习式图像级融合模块。实验涵盖单块选择、多个生成器集合，以及四种CNN与Transformer骨干网络；并比较了监督粒度、源分辨率、裁剪尺寸与推理覆盖范围等设置。

Result: 在GenImage上，所报告的块级监督变体在四种骨干网络上均比其整图对等版本提升了平均准确率。此外，监督粒度、源分辨率、裁剪尺寸与推理覆盖范围的对比进一步刻画了该方法特性；后处理测试与困难图像评估则揭示了其局限性。作者说明历史实验包含基于评估的模型选择，因此其结果不作为统一选取的排行榜式对比。

Conclusion: 研究总体表明，显式局部输入与图像块级监督是一种简单而有用的组合，可用于研究具有泛化性的AI生成图像检测。

Abstract: How much of an image does a detector need to see? Small RGB regions can retain useful evidence of image synthesis even when they reveal little of the full scene. Motivated by single-patch detection, we study patch-wise supervision: a shared backbone classifies explicit crops, each crop receives its own loss, and patch probabilities are averaged only at inference. The procedure requires neither handcrafted residual filtering nor a learned image-level fusion module. Experiments span single-patch selection, multiple generator collections, and four CNN and Transformer backbones. On GenImage, the reported patch-wise variants improve average accuracy over their whole-image counterparts across all four backbones. Comparisons of supervision granularity, source resolution, crop size, and inference coverage further characterize the approach, while post-processing tests and difficult-image evaluation reveal its limitations. The historical experiments include evaluation-based model selection, so their scores are not presented as a uniformly selected leaderboard comparison. Overall, the study identifies explicit local input and patch-level supervision as a simple, useful combination for investigating generalizable AI-generated image detection.

</details>


### [208] [Does Local Video Understanding Transfer Across Encounters? The EgoGears Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37938)
*Yuedong Tan,Lei Qi,Yu Liu,Di Wen,Ruiping Liu,Xiaoye Wang,Yufan Chen,Junwei Zheng,Chengzhi Wu,Chen Zhang,Zhihang Chen,Haiwen Sun,Zongwei Wu,Radu Timofte,Danda Pani Paudel,Kunyu Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 EgoGears 基准，用单视频与多视频两类问题诊断具身模型从局部感知到跨记录证据组合的能力转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨视频聚合准确率混淆了局部感知失败与观测身份保持、对应建立、证据组合的失败，无法判断局部视频理解是否真正迁移。

Method: 构建 EgoGears：含 567 个单视频问题和 1,487 个多视频问题，来自 126 段人类采集的第一视角记录、覆盖 39 条户外路线；多次重复穿行涵盖不同速度和光照，531 个问题需跨独立记录对齐。单视频问题测局部视觉/空间/运动证据，多视频问题测证据是否绑定正确观测并可组合为一致的路线关系。

Result: 在六个模型家族的 29 个单视频与 31 个多视频 MLLM 配置中，20 个可比配置全部在多视频问题上更差，平均下降 22.5 个百分点；固定答案格式和评分后差距仍存在，且不能简单归因于视频数量增加或记录边界。

Conclusion: 主要瓶颈是观测—证据绑定与有序路线状态跟踪；代码与基准已公开。

Abstract: Embodied systems must make knowledge acquired during one encounter usable in another despite changes in viewpoint, motion, and illumination. Yet aggregate cross-video accuracy conflates failures of local perception with failures to preserve observation identity, establish correspondence, and compose evidence, obscuring whether local video understanding actually transfers. We introduce EgoGears, a complementary single- and multi-video benchmark designed to diagnose this transition. It contains 567 single-video and 1,487 multi-video questions derived from 126 human-collected egocentric recordings covering 39 outdoor routes. Repeated traversals across movement speeds and lighting conditions ground comparisons in shared physical environments; 531 questions require alignment across independent recordings. Single-video questions measure the local visual, spatial, and motion evidence available to a model, while multi-video questions test whether evidence remains bound to the correct observation and can be composed into consistent route relationships. We report 29 single-video and 31 multi-video MLLM configurations across six model families in the main leaderboard. Among the 20 configurations evaluated comparably on both splits, every model performs worse on multi-video questions, with a mean decrease of 22.5 percentage points, and the gap persists when answer format and scoring are held fixed. The gap is not explained simply by additional videos or recording boundaries. The central bottlenecks are observation--evidence binding and ordered route-state tracking. The code and benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/lei-qi-233/EgoGears.

</details>


### [209] [SoL-Refiner: Speed-of-Light One-Step Refinement for High-Resolution Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37969)
*Haozhe Liu,Tian Ye,Shuchen Xue,Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Jincheng Yu,Duomin Wang,Ruihua Zhang,Lei Zhu,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SoL-Refiner 提出一种单步视频精炼器，将低分辨率生成结果一步提升至 4K，结合持续训练、强化学习后训练与一步蒸馏，在 2K/4K 上超越多步精炼基线并实现约 8.91 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率视频生成成本随时空 token 数量快速增长。常见替代方案先生成低分辨率再精炼，但传统多步精炼会引入第二个采样瓶颈，速度慢且代价高。

Method: 提出 SoL-Refiner 单步视频精炼器，仅需一次去噪步骤即可将低分辨率输出转为 4K 视频。采用三阶段方案：高分辨率持续训练、强化学习（RL）后训练、最终一步蒸馏。同时构建 Refiner-Bench 基准，用共享输入协议在约 2K 分辨率下对比不同精炼器。

Result: 在 2K 输出下，单步 SoL-Refiner 在 VBench 与 UniPercept 平均指标上优于所有外部精炼器；在 3840×2176 分辨率下，两项指标均超过三步 LTX-2.3 Refiner。配合完整加速方案，在 2K 延迟设置下相较同一基线实现 8.91 倍精炼延迟加速。

Conclusion: 单步精炼可在保持甚至提升视频质量的同时大幅降低高分辨率视频生成的精炼开销，为高效 4K 视频生成提供了可行路径。

Abstract: High-resolution video generation is expensive, as its cost grows rapidly with the number of spatiotemporal tokens. A practical alternative first generates a lower-resolution video and then applies a refiner, but conventional multi-step refinement introduces a second sampling bottleneck. We present SoL-Refiner, a one-step video refiner that transforms low-resolution model outputs into 4K videos with a single denoising step. Our three-stage recipe combines high-resolution continual training, reinforcement learning (RL) post-training, and a final one-step distillation. We introduce Refiner-Bench, a video refinement benchmark constructed from the outputs of different video generators, and use a shared-input protocol to compare refiners at approximately 2K output resolution. At 2K, the one-step SoL-Refiner outperforms all external refiners on the VBench and UniPercept averages, while at $3840\!\times\!2176$ it improves both metrics over the three-step LTX-2.3 Refiner. With the complete acceleration stack, SoL-Refiner achieves an $8.91\times$ speedup in refinement latency over the same baseline in our 2K latency setting.

</details>


### [210] [ORMA: Optimization-based Monocular 4D Reconstruction of Articulated Animals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37986)
*Xuyi Hu,Francesco Palandra,Shangzhe Wu,Daniel Cremers,Riccardo Marin,Silvia Zuffi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ORMA 是一个无需训练的四足动物 4D 重建框架，将关节运动与形状解耦，利用生成式 3D 先验实现跨物种种属的几何重建与全局一致的动物运动恢复，并提出了合成基准 PAW4D。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从单目视频恢复动物的关节化 4D 表示面临两大困难：四足动物形态多样性大，且缺乏动物 4D 监督数据。现有基于学习的方法逐帧处理图像，依赖合成数据或模型拟合的 3D 监督，受强参数化先验约束，难以泛化到分布外物种；对分布外动物往往只能恢复看似合理的姿态，却因底层形状模型无法忠实表达观测实例而产生不准确的几何。

Method: 提出 ORMA，一种无需训练的重建框架，将关节运动与形状解耦。以预测姿态作为优化参考，同时利用生成式 3D 先验进行精确形状重建。给定参考图像后重建动物几何并配准到参数化模型 SMAL+，得到适应观测实例的关节化形状；随后结合逐帧关节姿态估计与全局一致的相机位姿，在统一世界坐标系中恢复动物运动，并利用自监督 DINO 对应关系与时间一致性进一步优化重建结果。同时构建了 PAW4D 合成多物种基准，提供真实 3D 几何与相机运动标注以支持定量评估。

Result: 在 PAW4D、PFERD 以及具有挑战性的真实野外视频上的实验表明，ORMA 在提升重建精度的同时，能够跨多种四足动物物种恢复全局一致的动物运动。

Conclusion: ORMA 通过解耦关节与形状、结合生成式 3D 先验与自监督优化，实现了无需训练的、可泛化到分布外物种的单目动物 4D 重建；PAW4D 基准为多物种动物重建提供了定量评测手段。

Abstract: Recovering articulated 4D representations of animals from monocular videos remains challenging due to the large diversity of quadruped morphologies and lack of animal 4D supervision data. Existing learning-based reconstruction methods operate on individual images and rely on synthetic or model-fitted 3D supervision, which inherits the constraints of strong parametric priors and limits generalization to out-of-distribution species. When applied to out-of-distribution animals, they often recover a plausible pose while producing inaccurate geometry because the underlying shape model cannot faithfully represent the observed instance. We present ORMA, a training-free reconstruction framework that decouples articulation from shape, using the predicted pose as reference for optimization while leveraging generative 3D priors for accurate shape reconstruction. Given a reference image, we reconstruct the animal geometry and register it to the parametric model SMAL+, yielding an articulated shape adapted to the observed instance. We then combine per-frame articulated pose estimates with globally consistent camera poses to recover animal motion in a shared world coordinate frame, and further refine the reconstruction using self-supervised DINO correspondences and temporal consistency. To enable quantitative evaluation, we introduce PAW4D, a synthetic multi-species benchmark with ground-truth 3D geometry and camera motion. Experiments on PAW4D, PFERD, and challenging in-the-wild videos demonstrate that ORMA improves reconstruction accuracy while recovering globally consistend animal motion across diverse quadruped species.

</details>


### [211] [HybridCUA: Learning to Orchestrate GUI and CLI for Computer-Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38008)
*Tongbo Chen,Junbo Niu,Zhengxi Lu,Niu Lian,Fei Tang,Yuchen Yan,Yike Hong,Yong Du,Yizhou Liu,Bofan Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出混合GUI与CLI的计算机使用智能体范式，构建HybridCUA-8K数据与两阶段训练框架，HybridCUA-9B在OSWorld达53.6%准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算机使用智能体要么仅依赖GUI交互（低效且易错），要么为GUI补充应用专用API或工具（工程成本高、难以跨应用扩展）。作者主张下一代智能体应结合GUI的通用性与CLI的高效性，但关键挑战是当前模型不知道何时以及如何调用CLI。

Method: 开发数据构建流水线，生成三类轨迹：仅GUI、仅CLI、GUI与CLI交错轨迹，产出HybridCUA-8K（含5K混合轨迹与3K经验证的RLVR任务）。在此数据上提出两阶段训练框架：先对构造轨迹做监督微调，再进行带CLI感知奖励的强化学习，鼓励智能体有选择且可靠地使用CLI。

Result: HybridCUA-9B在OSWorld上达到53.6%准确率，较基座模型提升14.8个百分点；在WindowsAgentArena上提升4.0个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明了混合GUI与CLI范式在计算机使用智能体中的有效性及跨平台泛化能力。

Abstract: Computer use agents (CUAs) have demonstrated strong capabilities in completing digital tasks. However, existing CUAs either rely solely on graphical user interface (GUI) interactions, which are often inefficient and error prone, or augment GUI interactions with application specific APIs or tools, which require substantial engineering effort and are difficult to scale across applications. We argue that the next generation of CUAs should combine GUI interactions with the command line interface (CLI), leveraging the generality of the GUI and the efficiency of shell commands. A critical challenge, however, is that current models do not know when or how to use the CLI during task execution. To address this challenge, we develop a data construction pipeline that produces three types of trajectories: GUI only, CLI only, and interleaved GUI and CLI trajectories. This pipeline results in HybridCUA-8K, containing 5K hybrid trajectories and 3K verified RLVR tasks. Building on these data, we propose a training framework with two stages: supervised fine tuning on the constructed trajectories, followed by reinforcement learning with our CLI aware rewards that encourages agents to use the CLI selectively and reliably. Experiments show that HybridCUA-9B achieves 53.6% accuracy on OSWorld, improving over the base model by 14.8 percentage points, and improves performance on WindowsAgentArena by 4.0 percentage points. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and cross platform generalizability of the hybrid GUI and CLI paradigm for computer use agents.

</details>


### [212] [From Unity Simulation to Diffusion-Based Augmentation: Quantifying Dataset Balance for Robust Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38010)
*Mohamed Benkedadra,Aissa Saoudi,Maxime Gloesener,Sidi Ahmed Mahmoudi,Matei Mancas*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 在建筑安全监测等真实数据稀缺场景下，系统比较Unity仿真渲染与可控扩散生成（CIA）两种数据生成范式对目标检测的迁移效果，发现混合策略（90%真实+10%合成）最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算机视觉模型依赖大规模标注数据，但建筑安全监测等关键领域的数据采集成本高、危险且受伦理约束，因此亟需借助仿真或生成式数据来缓解真实数据稀缺问题。

Method: 构建统一实验框架，在模型与训练设置完全一致的条件下，对真实、仿真（Unity）与生成式（CIA）数据源进行受控混合，并用Precision、Recall、mAP及自定义Δ指标进行定量评估。

Result: 仅用Unity训练较真实数据mAP@0.5下降约50%，仅用CIA训练下降约16.5%（较温和）；混合策略显著提升性能，90%真实+10%Unity配置取得最佳mAP@0.5为62.68%（较基线+7.64%），90%真实+10%CIA配置取得最高精确率74.45%。

Conclusion: 单独使用仿真或生成式增强均难以达到最优迁移性；适量引入合成数据可增强泛化能力，但过量替换会引发域漂移。

Abstract: Modern computer vision models achieve high accuracy when trained on large-scale annotated datasets. In critical domains such as construction safety monitoring, data collection is costly, hazardous, and ethically constrained. This paper presents a systematic study comparing two complementary data generation paradigms, (1) Unity Simulation-based rendering and (2) Controllable Diffusion-based generation (CIA), for object detection under real data-scarce conditions. A unified experimental framework enables controlled dataset mixing across real, simulated, and generative sources, while maintaining identical model and training settings. Quantitative evaluation using Precision, Recall, mAP, and custom $Δ$-metrics, reveals that neither simulation nor generative augmentation alone achieves optimal transferability. Unity-only training yields an mAP@0.5 drop of $-50\%$ relative to real data, while CIA-only training shows a milder $-16.5\%$ degradation. Hybrid compositions significantly improve performance, with the 90\% real + 10\% Unity configuration achieving the best overall mAP@0.5 of $62.68\%$ ($+7.64\%$ over baseline), and the 90\% real + 10\% CIA configuration maximizing precision at $74.45\%$. Results demonstrate that limited synthetic inclusion enhances generalization, while excessive substitution induces domain drift.

</details>


### [213] [Beyond Lip Sync: Reference-Grounded Oral Refinement for Audio-Driven Portrait Animation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38019)
*Bangxun Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RGOR是一个音频驱动的唇形同步框架，通过参考帧与高清嘴部patch条件生成，使渲染的嘴部忠于被配音者本人而非通用嘴型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有唇形同步系统虽能紧贴音频并保持人脸可识别，但生成的嘴部是"平均嘴"：唇形与纹理、牙齿排列及张口时牙齿显露程度均非该人所有。根源在于现有训练与评估未要求使用本人嘴部：感知损失接受任何合理嘴型，人脸身份多由嘴周皮肤承载，且inpainting系统公开推理代码用未遮挡目标帧作参考，掩盖了这一差距。

Method: RGOR以同一人独立注册录像的帧和绕过VAE的嘴部HD patch作为每帧生成条件，并训练生成器对抗一个配对判别器：该判别器将每个渲染嘴部与本人参考对比，学习拒绝他人逼真嘴型。同时构建评估协议，在留出身份上比较开源与商业唇形同步系统。

Result: 实验表明RGOR在多数指标上达到最优或次优，既保留本人唇部与牙齿细节，又维持同步性与面部其余部分的完整性。

Conclusion: RGOR通过参考帧条件、高清嘴部patch与配对判别器，解决了唇形同步中"通用嘴"问题，并建立了更严格的评估协议，验证了保留个人嘴部特征的有效性。

Abstract: We present RGOR (Reference-Grounded Oral Refinement), an audio-driven lip-sync framework that renders the mouth of the specific person being dubbed rather than a generic one. Existing lip-sync systems follow the audio closely and keep the face recognizable, yet the mouth they render is an average mouth: the shape and texture of the lips, the arrangement of the teeth, and how much of them shows as the mouth opens are not that person's. The problem persists because nothing in current training or evaluation asks for the person's own mouth: perceptual losses accept any plausible mouth, face identity is carried mostly by the skin around it, and the released inference code of inpainting systems uses the unmasked target frame as the reference, which hides the gap. To address this, RGOR conditions every generated frame on frames from separate enrollment recordings of the same person and on HD patches of the mouth that bypass the VAE, and trains the generator against a paired judge that compares each rendered mouth with the person's reference and learns to reject a realistic mouth of someone else. We further build an evaluation protocol and use it to compare open-source and commercial lip-sync systems on held-out identities. Experiments show that RGOR achieves the best or second-best result on most metrics, and preserves the person's own lip and dental detail while keeping synchronization and the rest of the face intact.

</details>


### [214] [EVO-WAM: Evolving World Action Models through Video-Action Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38057)
*Shiyang Zhou,Xionghao Wu,Wenbo Li,Shenghe Zheng,Jiyao Zhang,Songsong Yu,Yijun Yang,Jianhui Liu,Haoze Sun,Senqiao Yang,Li Jiang,Jingyong Su,Haoyang Huang,Zhuotao Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出EVO-WAM框架，让世界动作模型利用自身生成的视频-动作轨迹自学习适应新任务，无需额外专家演示或在真实环境执行候选动作。在RoboTwin 2.0的7个未见任务上，Cosmos3成功率从26.9%提升至68.0%，DreamZero从28.5%提升至46.4%；真实世界3个长程复合任务上，Cosmos3从20.0%提升至76.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人在新任务上提升策略通常需要额外专家演示，成本高。世界动作模型（WAMs）可通过大规模视频先验联合预测未来视频与动作，为适应新任务提供潜在监督来源。但生成的视频可能无法展示任务完成，即使视频看似成功，其配对动作也可能不一致而导致执行失败。因此需要一种能在不依赖外部环境执行反馈的情况下，从WAM自身生成内容中可靠学习的适应方法。

Method: EVO-WAM框架包含三部分：1）在WAM训练中引入状态预测和锚定多帧上下文，使其能够进行完整的自回归推演，无需外部执行反馈；2）使用视觉语言模型筛选出能完成任务的前缀，并用逆动力学模型验证其视频-动作一致性，从而识别可靠的训练经验；3）在验证过的前缀上迭代训练WAM，并用更新后的模型生成新的推演轨迹。整个过程不执行候选动作。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的7个未见任务上，平均成功率Cosmos3从26.9%提升到68.0%（约2.5倍），DreamZero从28.5%提升到46.4%（约1.6倍）。在真实世界的3个未见长程复合任务上，Cosmos3平均成功率从20.0%提升到76.7%，提升56.7个百分点。

Conclusion: EVO-WAM证明世界动作模型可以通过筛选并验证自身生成的视频-动作轨迹实现自我迭代改进，从而在无需额外专家演示和真实环境探索的情况下显著提升新任务的成功率。该范式为机器人策略的自主适应提供了可行路径，并在仿真与真实长程任务上均取得显著效果。

Abstract: Improving robot policies on new tasks without collecting additional expert demonstrations remains a central challenge in robot learning. World action models (WAMs) use broad video priors to jointly predict future videos and actions, offering a potential source of supervision for adapting to new tasks. However, generated videos may fail to depict task completion, and even visually successful videos may be paired with inconsistent actions that lead to execution failure. We propose EVO-WAM, a framework that adapts WAMs to unseen tasks by learning from their own generated video-action trajectories, without executing candidate actions in an external environment. First, we augment WAM training with state prediction and anchored multi-frame context to enable complete autoregressive rollouts without external execution feedback. Second, we identify reliable training experience by selecting task-completing prefixes with a vision-language model and verifying their video-action consistency with an inverse dynamics model. Third, we iteratively train the WAM on verified prefixes and generate new rollouts with the updated model. On seven unseen RoboTwin 2.0 tasks, EVO-WAM increases average success rates from 26.9% to 68.0% for Cosmos3 and from 28.5% to 46.4% for DreamZero, reaching approximately $2.5\times$ and $1.6\times$ their initial success rates. On three unseen long-horizon composite tasks in the real world, it improves Cosmos3's average success rate from 20.0% to 76.7%, a gain of 56.7 percentage points. Project Page: https://evo-wam.github.io/.

</details>


### [215] [RS-OPSD: Reliable Privileged On-Policy-Self-Distillation for Ultra-High-Resolution Remote Sensing VQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38072)
*Chengjie Jiang,Yunqi Zhou,Jiafeng Yan,Sihang Zhao,Chun Yuan,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 RS-OPSD，一种可靠的特权在线策略自蒸馏框架，将放大视觉特权内化到模型中，用于超高分辨率遥感视觉问答（VQA），并构建了带证据区域标注的数据集 GeoEvidence-6K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超高分辨率遥感 VQA 需要模型在超大图像中定位微小视觉证据。现有方法通常依赖推理时的 token 剪枝、视觉搜索或工具增强推理，成本高且不够高效，作者希望探究能否把放大视觉带来的收益内化进模型本身。

Method: 提出 RS-OPSD 特权在线策略自蒸馏框架；构建 GeoEvidence-6K（涵盖 7 类任务、6,750 个样本，含证据区域标注），并提出人工反馈引导的技能精炼（HF-SR）实现可扩展标注；针对紧裁剪导致上下文丢失和教师不完美导致的信号冲突，引入上下文保留视觉特权（CPVP）与正确性对齐蒸馏（CAD）。

Result: 无需推理时的额外视觉搜索或工具调用，RS-OPSD 在 XLRS-Bench、MME-RealWorld-RS 和 LRS-VQA 上取得 SOTA 性能，相比同规模前 SOTA 平均提升 4.0 个百分点；2B 变体 RS-OPD-Lite 超过多数 8B 规模模型，并实现最快实测推理速度。

Conclusion: 放大视觉特权可被有效内化进模型，RS-OPSD 在超高分辨率遥感 VQA 上实现高效 SOTA，且代码、GeoEvidence-6K 及模型权重均已公开。

Abstract: Ultra-high-resolution (UHR) remote sensing visual question answering (VQA) requires models to resolve small visual evidence within extremely large images. Existing approaches typically rely on token pruning, visual search, or tool-augmented reasoning at inference time. We instead investigate whether the benefit of zoom-in visual privilege can be internalized into the model. We introduce RS-OPSD, a reliable privileged on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) framework for UHR remote sensing VQA. To provide high-quality privileged information with explicit question-relevant evidence, we construct GeoEvidence-6K, containing 6,750 VQA samples across seven task categories with evidence-region annotations, and develop Human Feedback-Guided Skill Refinement (HF-SR) for scalable annotation. To address context loss from tight crops and conflicting signals from imperfect teachers, RS-OPSD introduces Context-Preserving Visual Privilege (CPVP) and Correctness-Aligned Distillation (CAD). Without any additional visual search or tool calls at inference time, RS-OPSD achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance on XLRS-Bench, MME-RealWorld-RS, and LRS-VQA, outperforming pervious SOTA models of comparable scale by an average of 4.0 percentage points. Moreover, our 2B variant, RS-OPD-Lite, surpasses most 8B-scale models while achieving the fastest measured inference speed. Our Code, GeoEvidence-6K, and the model weights for RS-OPSD and RS-OPD-Lite are publicly available.

</details>


### [216] [MUGEN: Interactive Panoramic World Exploration via Camera Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38077)
*Jiaming Tan,Zhen Li,Shuwei Shi,Minggui Teng,Siqi Yang,Yuwei Wu,Bo Zheng,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出MUGEN大规模全景视频数据集与Wan360相机可控交互式全景视频生成模型，通过三项ERP感知组件和全景Plücker嵌入，实现高质量、时序一致、相机可控的360度视频生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式全景视频生成旨在合成沉浸式360度视频，在用户指定相机轨迹下保持视觉连贯性。但现有进展受限于数据与模型的耦合缺口：现有全景视频数据集通常时长短、标注弱或缺少相机轨迹；而现有相机可控视频生成模型面向透视视频设计，无法直接支持全景几何。因此需要同时解决数据与模型两方面的问题。

Method: 提出MUGEN与Wan360。MUGEN是面向交互式360度世界探索的大规模真实全景视频数据集，包含超1300小时至少4K的全景视频，带有丰富的语义与几何标注。Wan360是构建于MUGEN之上、相机可控的交互式全景视频生成模型。针对ERP表示的周期经度接缝与极点畸变问题，Wan360引入三个无参数ERP感知组件：周期性经度RoPE实现接缝一致位置编码、ERP感知填充减少边界伪影、随机滚动偏航实现一致学习。在相机控制方面，Wan360使用全景Plücker嵌入，以ERP光线而非透视针孔光线表示相机运动。

Result: 实验表明，MUGEN可作为全景世界探索的数据基础，Wan360能够实现高质量、时序一致、相机可控的360度视频生成。

Conclusion: MUGEN与Wan360共同弥补了全景视频生成领域的数据与模型缺口，为交互式全景世界探索提供了数据基础与可用的生成模型，推动了沉浸式360度视频生成的发展。

Abstract: Interactive panoramic video generation aims to synthesize immersive 360\textdegree{} videos that remain visually coherent while following user-specified camera trajectories during exploration. However, progress is limited by a coupled data-and-model gap: existing panoramic video datasets are often short, weakly annotated, or lack camera trajectories, while existing camera-controlled video generation models are designed for perspective videos and do not directly support panoramic geometry. In this paper, we introduce MUGEN and Wan360 to address these limitations. MUGEN is a large-scale real-world panoramic video dataset tailored to interactive 360-degree world exploration, comprising over 1,300 hours of at least 4K panoramic videos with rich semantic and geometric annotations. Built on MUGEN, we further present Wan360, a camera-controllable interactive panoramic video generation model. Panoramic videos are commonly represented by EquiRectangular Projection (ERP), which unfolds a spherical 360-degree view into a rectangular frame with cyclic longitude seams and pole distortions. To this end, Wan360 introduces three parameter-free ERP-aware components: periodic longitude RoPE for seam-consistent positional encoding, ERP-aware padding for reducing boundary artifacts, and random roll yaw for consistent learning. For camera control, Wan360 uses a panoramic Plücker embedding that represents camera motion with ERP rays rather than perspective pinhole rays. Experiments show that MUGEN serves as a data foundation for panoramic world exploration, and that Wan360 enables high-quality, temporally coherent, camera-controllable 360-degree video generation.

</details>


### [217] [OmniTaskonomy: When Does Visual Generation Improve Visual Understanding?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38079)
*Jiaxin Ge,Yiming Qin,Ji Xie,Haozhe Jiang,Xiaochuang Han,Junyi Zhang,Andrew Dai,Yinfei Yang,Jitendra Malik,Ranjay Krishna,Sewon Min,Haiwen Feng,Le Xue,Baifeng Shi,Trevor Darrell,XuDong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该研究系统探究了视觉生成监督在何时以及如何提升视觉理解能力，提出OmniTaskonomy统一分类体系并在生成-理解任务间建立迁移图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练生成模型可能使其习得几何、空间关系、物体性等丰富感知能力，但视觉生成对视觉理解的益处仍不明确。作者希望回答：视觉生成监督何时以及如何改善视觉理解。

Method: 研究构造了成对的图像到图像（I2I）生成任务与图像到文本（I2T）理解任务，二者以不同输出模态表达同一底层问题。在此基础上提出OmniTaskonomy统一分类体系，涵盖19个I2I生成任务和25个I2T理解能力，并分析生成与理解任务间的梯度对齐程度。

Result: 在正确训练配方下，I2I训练可提升下游I2T性能，且随I2I训练数据量增加增益更大。迁移图谱显示出选择性、任务相关的收益：一些符合直觉对应（如深度预测改善度量3D推理、目标指向改善计数、拼图重建改善2D排序），也有意外关联（如2.5D分割改善类别识别、Z深度预测改善定位）。梯度对齐越强，下游迁移增益越大。

Conclusion: 视觉生成是视觉理解的丰富监督来源，通过合适的训练课程与任务选择可释放其益处，为后续研究提供路线图。项目主页：https://omni-taskonomy.github.io/。

Abstract: Training a model to generate visual content can encourage it to learn rich perceptual capabilities related to geometry, spatial relationships, and objectness; yet, its benefits for visual understanding remain unclear. We ask: when and how does visual generation supervision improve visual understanding? We study controlled pairs of image-to-image (I2I) generation and image-to-text (I2T) understanding tasks that express the same underlying problem in different output modalities. We find that under the correct recipe, I2I training improves downstream I2T performance, with larger gains as the amount of I2I training data increases. We next ask which generation tasks benefit which understanding capabilities. To study transfer beyond paired tasks, we introduce OmniTaskonomy, a unified taxonomy spanning 19 I2I generation tasks and 25 I2T understanding capabilities. The resulting transfer map reveals selective, task-dependent benefits. Some follow intuitive correspondences, e.g., depth prediction improving metric 3D reasoning, object pointing improving counting, and jigsaw reconstruction improving 2D ordering. Interestingly, we also uncover surprising connections: 2.5D segmentation improving category recognition and Z-depth prediction improving localization. To probe these patterns, we analyze gradient alignment between generation and understanding tasks and find that stronger alignment is associated with larger downstream transfer gains. Together, our results highlight visual generation as a rich source of supervision for visual understanding and provide a roadmap for unlocking its benefits through the right training curriculum and task selection. Project page: https://omni-taskonomy.github.io/.

</details>


### [218] [VISTA: Internalizing Collective Visual Experience via On-Policy Distillation for Active Multimodal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38086)
*Zheng Jiang,Houde Qian,Yiming Chen,Ling Li,Chaoyang Li,Yueqi Li,Yuxuan Liu,Lifeng Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA 通过在线策略蒸馏将同一输入多次 rollout 的视觉发现转化为共享监督，提升主动多模态智能体的学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动多模态智能体用视觉工具获取任务相关证据，但基于结果的目标函数主要仅用 rollout 组估计标量优势，导致多轨迹中互补的视觉发现被浪费。

Method: 提出 VISTA，包含：集体视觉经验蒸馏（CVED），按交互上下文组织观测并与个体决策对齐；异构感知策略改进（HAPI），强化成功轨迹并为失败尝试提供经验引导蒸馏；采用经验条件化教师评估学生采样前缀，使一条轨迹的发现指导另一条轨迹的学习，同时不替换学生原有历史、不生成新目标轨迹。

Result: 在同等规模的可评估主动多模态智能体中取得最高平均性能，并在细粒度感知与通用推理任务上持续优于同骨干训练基线。

Conclusion: 集体视觉经验对主动多模态学习具有重要价值，VISTA 让智能体保留视觉工具并使用自身交互历史行动。

Abstract: Active multimodal agents use visual tools to acquire task-relevant evidence while reasoning. Although reinforcement learning samples multiple interaction trajectories per input, outcome-based objectives primarily use the group to estimate scalar advantages, leaving complementary visual discoveries underused. We introduce VISTA, which internalizes collective visual experience through on-policy distillation by turning observations from same-input rollouts into shared supervision. Collective visual experience distillation (CVED) organizes these observations with their interaction context and aligns them with individual decisions, while heterogeneity-aware policy improvement (HAPI) reinforces successful trajectories and provides experience-guided distillation for unsuccessful attempts. An experience-conditioned teacher evaluates the student's sampled response prefixes, allowing discoveries from one trajectory to guide learning in another without replacing the student's original history or generating new target trajectories. The trained agent retains its visual tools and acts using its own interaction history. VISTA achieves the strongest average performance among the evaluated active multimodal agents of comparable size and consistently outperforms same-backbone training baselines across fine-grained perception and general reasoning tasks, demonstrating the value of collective experience for active multimodal learning.

</details>


### [219] [Self-Aligned Forcing: Streaming Video Diffusion with Differentiable Noisy History](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38114)
*Weiqiang Wang,Zhuokun Chen,Yusheng Dai,Boying Li,Yi Zhang,Hossein Rahmani,Qiuhong Ke,Jianfei Cai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Self-Aligned Forcing（SAF），将每个块的历史K/V与其去噪噪声水平对齐，使历史保持可微，避免无梯度rollout与逐块重缓存，训练快1.8倍、显存更低，4 GPU达49.1 FPS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散支持交互式流式生成，但长时rollout存在误差累积。自rollout训练虽缓解曝光偏差，有限rollout仍无法解决长程漂移。作者观察到历史K/V的噪声水平在视觉质量与运动之间权衡，且让梯度穿过历史可使因果训练更接近双向训练。

Method: SAF让每个块的历史与其被去噪块的噪声水平对齐：历史为前序块在同一去噪阶段产生的K/V，因此同一阶段所有块可在因果掩码下于一次前向中完成去噪。这保持含噪历史可微，使未来损失可优化历史的编码方式，从而免去独立的无梯度rollout和逐块timestep-zero重缓存。

Result: 训练速度比先前方法快达1.8倍且显存更低；推理时在现有方法中取得最高单GPU吞吐，并可为多GPU流水线按阶段保留一个history bank，4 GPU达49.1 FPS；长时程生成更优，在视觉质量与运动间取得更好平衡。

Conclusion: 通过对齐历史K/V与当前去噪块的噪声水平并保留历史梯度，SAF在效率与长时程生成质量上同时优于现有自回归视频扩散训练方案。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables interactive streaming generation, but suffers from error accumulation over long rollouts. Self-rollout training reduces exposure bias, yet finite rollouts leave long-range drift unresolved. We observe that the noise level of the history key-value (K/V) representations trades visual quality against motion, and that restoring gradients through the history aligns causal training far more closely with bidirectional training. Motivated by these observations, we introduce Self-Aligned Forcing (SAF), a training scheme that aligns the history of each block with the noise level of the block being denoised. Specifically, the history is the K/V produced by preceding blocks at the same denoising stage, so all blocks at a stage can be denoised in a single forward pass under a causal mask. This keeps the noisy history differentiable, allowing future losses to optimize how it is encoded. SAF therefore avoids a separate no-gradient rollout and per-block timestep-zero recaching, training up to 1.8x faster than prior methods with lower memory. At inference, SAF achieves the highest single-GPU throughput among existing methods and keeps one history bank per stage for a multi-GPU pipeline, reaching 49.1 FPS on 4 GPUs. Experiments show superior long-horizon generation with a better balance between visual quality and motion. Project page: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/self-aligned-forcing/.

</details>


### [220] [GA-EIRFS: A Geometry-Augmented Repeat-Factor Sampling Method for Long-Tailed LiDAR 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38116)
*Taufiq Ahmed,Constantino Álvarez Casado,Daniel Herrera Castro,Sasan Sharifipour,Abhishek Kumar,Miguel Bordallo López*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GA-EIRFS采样方法，用几何证据（点数、法向熵、表面覆盖）调制频率驱动的重复采样因子，改善长尾3D目标检测，检测器与推理不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长尾3D检测通常被视为类别频率问题，但LiDAR监督质量取决于目标可观测性：相似频率可能隐藏不同的几何证据，仅靠频率采样会忽略这一点。

Method: GA-EIRFS为检测器无关方法，将基于频率的repeat factor与固定几何分数（点数量、表面法向熵、表面覆盖率）相乘调制；仅改变帧采样概率，不改动检测器与推理流程。

Result: 在nuScenes上，CenterPoint与PointPillars的两个随机种子、四次收敛实验中mAP与NDS均提升；CenterPoint seed 666时mAP由0.552升至0.563，bicycle AP由0.306升至0.359。类别增益与采样权重增幅相关（Spearman rho=0.70，p=0.025），但与单纯几何分数不相关（rho=0.32，p=0.37）。KITTI结果随种子波动，最稀有类别波动最大。

Conclusion: 几何分数并非独立信号，而是放大频率驱动的采样需求；GA-EIRFS作为通用采样策略可提升长尾3D检测，但跨数据集稳定性仍需验证。

Abstract: Long-tailed 3D object detection is treated as a class-frequency problem, but LiDAR supervision quality depends on object observability: similar frequencies can hide different geometric evidence. We introduce Geometry-Augmented Exponentially Weighted Instance-Aware Repeat Factor Sampling (GA-EIRFS), a detector-agnostic method that modulates a frequency-based repeat factor with a fixed geometry score combining point count, surface-normal entropy, and surface coverage. GA-EIRFS changes only frame-sampling probabilities, leaving the detector and inference unchanged. On nuScenes it improves mean average precision (mAP) and the nuScenes detection score (NDS) in four converged experiments with CenterPoint and PointPillars over two seeds; for CenterPoint at seed 666, mAP rises from 0.552 to 0.563 and bicycle AP from 0.306 to 0.359. Per-class gains correlate with the class sampling-weight increase (Spearman rho=0.70, p=0.025) but not with geometry score alone (rho=0.32, p=0.37), so geometry amplifies frequency-driven need. KITTI results vary across seeds, most for the rarest class. Code: https://github.com/Multimodal-Sensing-Lab/GA-EIRFS.

</details>


### [221] [VideoLoop: Looped Working Memory Against Semantic Thrashing in Long-Form Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38119)
*Jinfa Huang,Jianming Xu,Jingyang Lin,Zhengyuan Yang,Jiebo Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 VideoLoop，一种带双循环机制的多模态智能体，通过可重写的有界工作记忆缓解长视频理解中的“语义抖动”问题，在多个基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频理解需要智能体在多个推理步骤中迭代收集证据。然而现有智能体方法存在“语义抖动”：随着仅追加式工作记忆增长，对关键证据的注意力崩溃，智能体无法访问已发现的信息。作者需要一种能清理噪声、控制上下文增长的方法。

Method: 首先给出结构性论证，证明仅追加式记忆能纳入新证据，但若没有重写算子，则无法去除累积噪声、也无法阻止有序上下文的增长。基于此提出 VideoLoop：一个带两个耦合循环的多模态智能体。外层循环对视频与内层循环进行推理；内层循环在每一步之后，从存储过往观测与中间分析的无界文件系统中检索产物，并重写有界的工作记忆。

Result: 实验表明 VideoLoop 以即插即用方式提升四个主流 LVLM 骨干模型，在 VideoMME (long) 上平均增益 4.2 个百分点。工作记忆分析显示其缓解了语义抖动：在最难的 1/4 VideoMME (long) 问题上，仅阅读智能体上下文的盲判断者正确率为 81.1%，而仅追加式智能体为 60.9%。使用 Gemini 3.1 Pro 时，VideoLoop 在 VideoMME (long) 达 88.3%，VideoMMMU 达 88.8%，LongVideoBench (long) 达 80.9%。

Conclusion: VideoLoop 通过检索并重写有界工作记忆的双循环设计，有效缓解了仅追加式记忆导致的语义抖动，显著提升长视频理解性能，且可作为通用模块即插即用地增强多种 LVLM 骨干。

Abstract: Long-form video understanding requires multimodal agents to iteratively gather evidence over many reasoning steps. However, most existing agentic methods suffer from semantic thrashing: as append-only working memory grows, attention to key evidence collapses, and the agent loses access to what it has already found. First, we provide a structural argument showing that append-only memory can incorporate newly observed target evidence, but cannot remove accumulated noise or prevent ordered context growth without a rewrite operator. Second, motivated by this analysis, we propose VideoLoop, a multimodal agent with two coupled loops. The outer loop reasons over the video and the inner loop, after each step, retrieves artifacts from an unbounded filesystem of past observations and intermediate analysis, and rewrites a bounded working memory. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of VideoLoop, which improves four popular LVLM backbones in a plug-and-play manner, with an average gain of 4.2% points over baseline on VideoMME (long). Further analysis of working memory suggests that VideoLoop mitigates semantic thrashing: on the hardest quarter of VideoMME (long) questions, a blind judge that reads only the agent's context answers 81.1% correctly, versus 60.9% for the append-only agent. With Gemini 3.1 Pro, VideoLoop reaches 88.3% on VideoMME (long), 88.8% on VideoMMMU, and 80.9% on LongVideoBench (long).

</details>


### [222] [HelixWorld: A Real-time Interactive Audio-Visual World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38123)
*Lei Ke,Jiahao Pan,Zeyue Tian,Jiaming Wang,Haoyuan Huang,Kam Man Wu,Pengjun Fang,Hongyu Liu,Chenyang Qi,Lin Wang,Ruibin Yuan,Weijia Chen,Fangneng Zhan,Qifeng Chen,Wei Xue,Yike Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HelixWorld 是首个实时交互式音视频世界模型，视觉场景与基于相机的空间立体声在用户交互下原生共同演化，在单GPU上以24 FPS实现无漂移联合音视频推演。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模拟本质上是多感官的，需要实时的视觉与声学动态同步。然而现有交互式世界模型仍是"无声"的，仅关注视觉渲染与控制，忽略了声学维度。

Method: 构建含真实立体声与度量相机位姿的高保真空间音视频数据集；预训练以6-DoF相机轨迹和用户动作为条件的双向教师模型；通过在线轨迹蒸馏损失将教师蒸馏为少步流式学生模型，实现低延迟因果交互。

Result: HelixWorld 在视觉保真度和响应性上匹配最先进的无声世界模型，同时在相机对齐的空间声学沉浸感上显著超越现有基线；并在单GPU上实现24 FPS的无漂移音视频推演。

Conclusion: 该工作形式化了空间声学一致性并提出 HelixBench 评测基准，用于评估合成声场是否忠实跟踪动态视角运动，推动了多感官交互式世界模型的发展。

Abstract: World simulation is inherently multisensory, demanding synchronized visual and acoustic dynamics in real time. Yet prevailing interactive world models remain strictly silent, focusing exclusively on visual rendering and control while overlooking the acoustic dimension. We present HelixWorld, a real-time interactive audio-visual world model where visual scenes and camera-grounded spatial stereo sound co-evolve natively under user interaction. We curate a high-fidelity spatial audio-visual dataset with true stereo acoustics and metric camera poses, upon which we pre-train a bidirectional teacher conditioned on 6-DoF camera trajectories and user actions. To enable low-latency causal interaction, we distill the teacher into a few-step streaming student via an online trajectory distillation loss, sustaining drift-free joint audio-visual rollouts at 24 FPS on a single GPU. Furthermore, we formalize spatial-acoustic consistency and introduce HelixBench to evaluate whether synthesized sound fields faithfully track dynamic viewpoint motion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HelixWorld matches state-of-the-art silent world models in visual fidelity and responsiveness, while significantly surpassing existing baselines in camera-aligned spatial-acoustic immersion.

</details>


### [223] [CLeaR: A Unified Framework for Resolving the Leakage-Degradation Dilemma in Style Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38136)
*Teng Zhou,Yunhao Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出免训练框架CLeaR，通过正交子空间投影、集成反演与能量引导校准，缓解风格迁移中的内容泄漏与风格退化的两难问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有风格迁移方法存在内容泄漏，即参考图像中的物体、布局或语义出现在生成结果中。已有的数据驱动与免训练方法虽能减少泄漏，却面临"泄漏-退化"两难：抑制内容会削弱风格保真度，而保留风格又会重新引入参考内容。该问题贯穿特征分离、特征空间定位与扩散生成整个流程。

Method: 提出免训练框架CLeaR，包含三部分：(1) 正交子空间投影，在各视觉基础模型（VFM）特征空间中定义内容削减的风格目标；(2) 集成反演，优化一个共享的像素空间风格锚点，使其在多个VFM上满足风格约束；(3) 能量引导校准，在扩散采样过程中将去噪轨迹导向集成定义的风格流形。

Result: 理论分析表明风格锚点估计误差随VFM数量增加而减小。在StyleBench上的实验显示，CLeaR在风格对齐、内容泄漏抑制以及LLM-as-Judge评估上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: CLeaR以免训练方式有效缓解内容泄漏与风格退化的两难问题，并通过多VFM集成提升风格锚点估计精度，代码已开源。

Abstract: Style transfer aims to render target content in the style of a reference image, but existing methods often suffer from content leakage, where objects, layouts, or semantics from the style reference appear in the generated output. Although prior data-driven and training-free methods can reduce leakage, they often face a leakage-degradation dilemma: stronger content suppression may weaken style fidelity, while richer style preservation may reintroduce unwanted reference content. We identify this dilemma across the full style-transfer pipeline, including feature separation, feature-space grounding, and diffusion generation. To address these issues, we propose CLeaR, a training-free framework for content-leakage-resistant style transfer. CLeaR first uses Orthogonal Subspace Projection to define content-reduced style targets in each vision foundation model (VFM) feature space. It then performs Ensemble Inversion, which optimizes a shared pixel-space style anchor satisfying style constraints across multiple VFMs. Finally, Energy-Guided Calibration maintains style alignment during diffusion sampling by steering the denoising trajectory toward the ensemble-defined style manifold. We further provide a theoretical analysis showing that the style-anchor estimation error decreases with the number of VFMs. Experiments on StyleBench demonstrate that CLeaR improves style alignment, reduces content leakage, and achieves better LLM-as-Judge evaluation compared with existing methods. The code is available at \href{https://github.com/0606zt/CLeaR}{https://github.com/0606zt/CLeaR}.

</details>


### [224] [Breaking the Uniformity Trap: Scaling Video Diffusion Model via SplitMoE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38140)
*Yu Xu,Yuxin Zhang,Xiao Yang,Haotian Yang,Yizhi Wang,Xinwei Huang,Minxuan Lin,Angtian Wang,Chongyang Ma,Fan Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SplitMoE，一种分角色稀疏架构，将专家池分为语义专家与通用专家，结合原型引导路由和拉推正则化，打破传统MoE的均匀化陷阱，在视频生成任务中以同等激活参数预算取得更优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统MoE对token独立路由并强制专家使用均匀化，与视频数据时空冗余、语义长尾的特性不匹配，导致路由碎片化和结构失真，即"均匀化陷阱"。

Method: 将专家池显式分为语义专家（捕捉高层语义抽象）和通用专家（保留残差视觉信息与生成灵活性），并采用原型引导路由与拉推正则化，使token按语义属性自然聚类而非受均衡约束。

Result: 在同等激活参数预算下，SplitMoE在收敛速度、路由一致性和视频生成质量上均优于传统负载均衡MoE，并展现出由粗到细的去噪逻辑。

Conclusion: SplitMoE为社区提供了模态感知的扩展路径，可作为构建大规模视频世界模型的重要参考。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), popularized by large language models, is a promising paradigm for scaling visual generative models. However, conventional token-wise MoE routes tokens independently within a homogeneous expert pool and regularizes expert usage toward uniformity, making it poorly matched to video data that is spatiotemporally redundant and semantically long-tailed. We show that existing visual MoEs fall into a uniformity trap: semantically under-organized routing, compounded by uniform expert-usage regularization, scatters coherent patches across disparate experts, causing routing fragmentation and structural distortion. To address this, we propose SplitMoE, a split-role sparse architecture that breaks the shackles of uniformity. To accommodate the inherent semantic imbalance, we explicitly bifurcate the expert pool into semantic experts and generic experts, with semantic experts capturing high-level semantic abstraction and generic experts preserving residual visual information and flexible generative capacity. Leveraging prototype-guided routing and pull-push regularization, SplitMoE enables tokens to cluster naturally by semantic attributes rather than arbitrary balancing constraints. Extensive results show that under an equivalent activated-parameter budget, SplitMoE outperforms traditional load-balanced MoEs in convergence speed, routing coherence, and video generation quality across standard benchmarks. By revealing an emergent coarse-to-fine denoising logic, SplitMoE provides the community with a modality-aware scaling path, serving as a critical reference for building large-scale video world models.

</details>


### [225] [LIFT: Layout-In-Future Video Generation under Large Viewpoint Change via On-Policy Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38146)
*Shengxiang Ji,Boyang Wang,Haiyang Xu,Bingnan Li,Yucheng Mao,Zeyuan Chen,Xiaojun Shan,Xiang Zhang,Gang Hua,Jianwen Xie,Zezhou Cheng,Zhuowen Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LIFT是一个统一的图像到视频生成框架，将相机控制与"未来布局"控制相结合，让用户既能指定相机运动，也能指定未来视图（尤其末帧）中出现什么内容及其空间位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可控视频生成中，给定初始图像，用户不仅关心相机如何运动，还关心未来关键时刻（尤其是末帧）场景的样子。现有的相机控制只指定视点轨迹，文本提示只提供粗略语义引导，二者都无法精确决定未来视图的内容与空间布局。在大视角变化下，这一问题尤为突出——相机会揭示首帧中不可见的区域。

Method: LIFT以末帧布局（last-frame layout）作为未来场景的显式控制信号。由于从如此稀疏的布局引导中学习比逐帧稠密布局条件困难得多，作者提出on-policy self-distillation（OPSD），将稠密布局教师模型的控制能力迁移给末帧布局学生模型。此外还构建了LIFT-Vista数据集，包含大视角变化以及相机与时间一致的布局标注。

Result: 实验表明，LIFT在视频质量、未来布局可控性和相机可控性方面均优于其他方法。

Conclusion: LIFT通过末帧布局控制与OPSD训练策略，实现了统一的图像到视频生成框架，在相机控制的基础上补充了未来场景布局控制，提升了生成质量与可控性。

Abstract: We introduce LIFT, a unified image-to-video generation framework that complements camera control with Layout-In-FuTure control, enabling users to specify what should appear in a future view and where it should appear. This addresses a practical need in controllable video generation: given an initial image, users often care not only about how the camera moves, but also about what the scene should look like at key future moments, especially the final frame. Existing camera controls specify viewpoint trajectories, while text prompts provide only coarse semantic guidance; neither precisely determines the content and spatial layout of future views. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced under large viewpoint changes, where the camera reveals regions that are not visible in the first frame. LIFT therefore uses the last-frame layout as an explicit control signal for the desired future scene. Since learning from such sparse layout guidance is substantially more challenging than conditioning on dense per-frame layouts, we introduce on-policy self-distillation (OPSD) to transfer the control capability of a dense-layout teacher to a last-frame-layout student. We further curate LIFT-Vista, a dataset featuring large viewpoint changes with camera and temporally consistent layout annotations. Experiments show that LIFT improves video quality, future-layout controllability, and camera controllability over other methods.

</details>


### [226] [FracGen: Learning How Objects Stretch and Tear with Physics-Informed Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38152)
*Trong-Tung Nguyen,Jiahan Zhang,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FracGen，一种断裂感知视频生成模型，能从完整物体的单张图像出发，在物理信号条件下生成可控且逼真的断裂动态；配套FracGen仿真框架提供配对数据与物理场，并通过物理约束损失训练，性能超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型难以捕捉物体断裂这类复杂的物理动力学，缺乏真实感与可控性。同时，获取断裂视频与对应的稠密物理场标注成本高昂，缺乏评估生成断裂视频物理合理性的基准。

Method: 1）构建FracSim仿真框架，在物质点法（MPM）中引入连续损伤模型，在标准渲染之外零额外成本地生成配对断裂视频与像素对齐的稠密物理场；2）训练FracGen同时预测RGB视频与物理场，促使模型捕捉物理状态而非表面外观；3）采用物理信息损失约束预测场之间的一致性，无需测试时仿真或逐场景调参。

Result: FracGen能捕捉不同材料的特有断裂行为，并支持对撕裂位置、裂纹扩展速度和失效前变形量的细粒度控制；作者还提出评估生成断裂视频物理合理性的基准，实验表明其在物理保真度和视觉保真度上均优于现有视频生成基线。

Conclusion: 通过断裂感知仿真数据与物理约束的联合训练，FracGen实现了可控、逼真且无需昂贵推理的断裂视频生成，并为该任务的物理合理性评估提供了基准，结果见项目网站 https://fracgen.github.io/。

Abstract: We introduce FracGen, a fracture-aware video generation model that produces plausible, controllable fracture dynamics from a single image of an intact object, conditioned on physics signals. To train FracGen, we build FracSim, a fracture-aware simulation framework that augments material point method (MPM) simulation with a continuum damage model, producing paired fracture videos and dense, pixel-aligned physical fields at no additional cost beyond standard rendering. FracGen leverages these maps in two ways: it is trained to jointly predict them alongside RGB video, encouraging the model to capture physical state rather than surface appearance; and it is supervised with physics-informed losses that encourage consistency among the predicted maps. As a result, FracGen captures distinct material-specific fracture behavior without expensive test-time simulation or per-scene tuning, while offering fine-grained control over where an object tears, how fast the crack propagates, and how much deformation precedes failure. We further introduce a benchmark for evaluating the physical plausibility of generated fracture video, and show through extensive experiments that FracGen outperforms existing video generation baselines in both physical and visual fidelity. Results are best viewed in our project website: https://fracgen.github.io/.

</details>


### [227] [PowerSim: Differentiable Physics Simulation and Rendering with Power Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38153)
*Trong-Tung Nguyen,Anand Bhattad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PowerSim 将预训练的 PowerFoam 场景与 MPM 直接耦合，实现可微、物理驱动的 3D 动力学仿真，并支持交互仿真、材质场恢复、场景合成与动态反射渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 3D 场景引入物理可信、可微的动力学通常需要额外的辅助表示来桥接仿真与渲染，增加了复杂度与误差。作者希望找到一种无需中间表示、能直接由仿真驱动场景几何与外观的方法。

Method: 利用 PowerFoam 的 power diagram 表示与 Material Point Method（MPM）之间的天然一致性——每个基元的几何与外观属性与 MPM 跟踪的量高度对应——将预训练 PowerFoam 场景直接与 MPM 耦合，使模拟运动直接驱动场景的几何与外观。

Result: 在真实与合成场景上实现了四类应用：用户交互下的静态场景仿真、空间变化材质场恢复、将独立捕获场景的基元合成为单一可仿真场景、以及随形变一致更新的光线追踪反射。结果表明 PowerSim 优于以往物理驱动动力学框架，并带来如动态场景二次光线光照等独特优势。

Conclusion: PowerSim 证明 PowerFoam 表示与 MPM 可以直接对齐，从而在无需辅助表示的情况下实现可微物理仿真，并为动态场景的材质恢复、场景合成与高质量反射渲染开辟了新可能。

Abstract: We introduce PowerSim, a method to bring physically grounded, differentiable dynamics to PowerFoam's power diagram based 3D representation. PowerSim directly couples a pre-trained PowerFoam scene to the Material Point Method (MPM) by exploiting a natural alignment between the two: the geometric and appearance properties of each primitive correspond closely to the quantities MPM already tracks as an object deforms. Consequently, simulated motion can drive the scene's geometry and appearance directly, without an auxiliary representation in between. Built on this framework, we enable a range of applications on real and synthetic scenes: (1) simulating a static scene under user interaction, (2) recovering spatially varying material fields, (3) compositing primitives from independently captured scenes into a single simulation-ready scene and (4) ray-tracing reflections that update consistently as the object deforms. Our results suggest that PowerSim excels over previous frameworks for physically grounded dynamics, while unlocking unique advantages-such as secondary ray lighting effects on dynamic scenes. Results are best viewed on our project website: https://power-sim.github.io/.

</details>


### [228] [LongLive-Plug: Once-for-All Distillation for Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38154)
*Shuai Yang,Luozhou Wang,Wei Huang,ZhiFei Chen,Bohan Zhang,Xiao Fu,Qianli Ma,Chen-Hsuan Lin,Weian Mao,Bryan Chu,Song Han,Yukang Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 LongLive-Plug，一种一次性蒸馏框架，将可复用能力以 LoRA 形式学习在基座模型上，实现免训练、即插即用地部署到下游扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型常针对不同下游任务做专门化开发，且每个专门化模型通常都要重复进行蒸馏（如加速采样或改进长视频生成），造成重复训练开销。作者希望只需蒸馏一次即可复用。

Method: 在基座模型上以 LoRA 形式学习可复用能力，包括单次无分类器引导（CFG）、少步采样、以及面向自回归生成的长上下文误差校正。这些适配器在下游模型增加条件分支或扩展输出通道时仍可复用。尽管训练在固定引导尺度下进行，专用 CFG LoRA 可通过其推理权重实现文本引导控制，与少步 LoRA 组合可同时保留少步生成与 CFG 可控性。

Result: 在三个骨干族、八类任务的 54 个下游模型上验证了免训练部署，任务涵盖世界建模、机器人、编辑和多模态生成。该方法可能支持更多兼容模型。

Conclusion: 每项能力可在每个骨干族上蒸馏一次并复用，无需针对每个目标模型重新训练。

Abstract: Video diffusion models are increasingly developed into specialized models for diverse downstream tasks, and this development often includes a distillation stage, for example to accelerate sampling or to improve long-video generation. This stage is typically repeated for every specialized model. We introduce LongLive-Plug, a once-for-all distillation framework that learns reusable capabilities as LoRAs on a base model for training-free, plug-and-play deployment to compatible downstream models. These capabilities include single-pass classifier-free guidance, few-step sampling, and long-context error correction for autoregressive generation. The adapters remain reusable even when downstream models add conditioning branches, expand output channels. Despite training at a fixed guidance scale, our dedicated CFG LoRA provides text guidance control through its inference weight. Combining it with a few-step LoRA simultaneously preserves few-step generation and CFG controllability on downstream tasks. We verify training-free deployment on 54 downstream models across three backbone families and eight task categories, including world modeling, robotics, editing, and multimodal generation. The approach may support additional compatible models. Each capability can thus be distilled once per backbone family and reused without per-target retraining.

</details>


### [229] [DMA$^2$: Pixel-space Distribution Matching with Adversarial and Anchor Losses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38156)
*Xin Lin,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Haitian Zheng,Shaoteng Liu,Lehan Yang,Zhe Lin,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文针对像素空间扩散模型提出DMD改进框架DMA²，通过高噪声匹配带、DINO-Adv去判别器对抗引导和AF-Loss语义分布损失，让四步学生模型全面超越25步教师及现有少步蒸馏方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布匹配蒸馏（DMD）主要围绕潜在扩散模型展开，忽视了原生RGB像素空间的特性和设计机会。作者希望重新审视像素空间教师的DMD接口，挖掘RGB表示在教师匹配和真实数据引导方面的潜力。

Method: 工作从两个DMD接口重新设计：教师匹配侧，通过诊断发现低噪声RGB匹配被局部纹理线索主导，因此采用固定的高噪声匹配带；真实数据侧，利用原生干净RGB输出引入外部视觉表示引导，提出DINO-Adv，将重型fake-score判别器从对抗梯度路径中移除并提供局部参数化patch引导。此外提出AF-Loss，一种无参数的辅助语义分布场目标，在共享DINOv2空间上对分离的滚动真实与生成支撑进行操作，同时保留提示条件下的教师监督，不增加可学习参数或推理计算。以上设计合称DMA²。

Result: 在DPG-Bench、GenEval、VQAScore和COCO30K四个基准上，四步DMA²学生模型的表现优于25步教师模型以及所对比的少步蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，针对像素空间原生RGB重新设计DMD接口，能够在极少的采样步数下取得超越多步教师和现有蒸馏方法的生成质量，且不引入额外推理开销。

Abstract: Distribution matching distillation (DMD) provides a general framework for few-step diffusion generation, but its modern text-to-image instantiations have been developed primarily around latent diffusion. It therefore overlooks key properties and design opportunities of native RGB. We revisit two DMD interfaces for pixel-space teachers. On the teacher-matching side, diagnostics show low-noise RGB matching is dominated by a local-texture cue, motivating a fixed high-noise matching band. On the real-data side, native clean-RGB outputs allow guidance from an external visual representation without traversing a decoder or sharing the heavy fake-score critic. DINO-Adv removes this critic from the adversarial gradient path and supplies local parametric patch guidance. For distribution-level guidance, we introduce AF-Loss, a parameter-free auxiliary semantic distribution-field objective designed for text-to-image DMD. It operates on detached rolling real and generated supports in the shared DINOv2 space while preserving prompt-conditioned teacher supervision. AF-Loss adds no learnable parameters or inference-time computation. Together these designs form DMA$^2$. Across DPG-Bench, GenEval, VQAScore, and COCO30K, the four-step DMA$^2$ student performs better than the 25-step teacher and evaluated few-step distillers.

</details>


### [230] [Rethinking Representations for World-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38163)
*Haoyi Jiang,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Zhihao Sun,Zequn Chen,Sen Wang,Xinjie Wang,Xia Chen,Jingfeng Yao,Weiheng Zhao,Shanglin Yuan,Zhizhong Su,Wei Sui,Wenyu Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究世界-动作模型中表征空间的设计，提出以预训练DINO特征为基础的ReWAM模型，通过特征校准、时序表征瓶颈和动作锚定表征塑形，无需生成式视频预训练即取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界-动作模型联合学习机器人策略并预测未来观测，表征空间成为控制与预测的接口。然而，仅靠重建保真度或预训练感知特征并不足以保证有效的策略学习，因此需要重新审视该表征空间的设计。

Method: 提出ReWAM，一种以表征为中心的世界-动作模型，建立在预训练DINO特征之上。采用特征校准（Feature Calibration）与时序表征瓶颈（Temporal Representation Bottleneck）将特征组织为紧凑的世界状态；并通过动作锚定表征塑形（Action-Grounded Representation Shaping），仅将动作损失梯度路由到瓶颈，使策略塑造表征内容，世界模型学习其演化。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0上达到93.6%的成功率，且无需生成式视频预训练；在RoboDojo上，使用约600小时具身预训练数据，平均得分12.29，成功率8.28%。

Conclusion: 研究表明，表征空间的设计是世界-动作模型的关键，通过动作锚定的表征塑形与紧凑世界状态组织，可在不依赖生成式视频预训练的情况下实现高效策略学习。

Abstract: World-action models jointly learn robot policies and predict future observations, making the representation space an interface between control and prediction. We study the design of this space through controlled comparisons, finding that neither reconstruction fidelity nor pre-trained perceptual features alone ensure effective policy learning. These findings motivate ReWAM, a representation-centric world-action model built on pre-trained DINO features. Feature Calibration and a Temporal Representation Bottleneck organize these features into compact world states suited to dynamics modeling. Action-Grounded Representation Shaping routes only action-loss gradients to the bottleneck, thereby letting the policy shape what the representation encodes while the world model learns how it evolves. Without generative video pre-training, ReWAM achieves 93.6% success on RoboTwin 2.0. On RoboDojo, it achieves an average score of 12.29 and a success rate of 8.28% using approximately 600 hours of embodied pre-training data.

</details>


### [231] [Cropland PAtteRNS: Parallel Dimensional Attention Networks and Attention to Dataset Disparity for Crop Segmentation in Satellite Imagery Time Series Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38165)
*Joseph Metcalfe,Sara Sharifzadeh,Fabio Caraffini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PAtteRNS模型，首次在Sentinel-2时序数据的时序、光谱、空间三个方面分别应用自注意力，并设计并行Transformer架构降低三重因子化自注意力的计算复杂度，在作物分割任务上超越现有最优模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前卫星影像时序数据集和作物分割架构发展迅速，但研究过度追求新颖概念和超大数据集，忽略了细节问题，如数据集中类别分组缺陷及不同tile尺寸变体间结果不可比的问题。

Method: 提出混合Transformer-卷积模型PAtteRNS，对Sentinel-2多光谱时序数据的时序、光谱、空间三个维度分别使用自注意力，并引入新型并行Transformer架构实现全因子化注意力，显著降低三重因子化自注意力的计算复杂度；通过消融实验验证架构，并在PASTIS和MTLCC数据集多个tile尺寸变体上与SOTA作物分割模型对比。

Result: PAtteRNS在作物类别分割任务中超越所有对比模型，在多种分割指标上表现优异，尤其在常被忽视的地块边界划分质量（Boundary IoU）上优势明显；同时发现数据集中的类别分组缺陷会显著损害模型性能，且不同tile尺寸变体的结果无法相互比较，使跨tile尺寸的公平对比失效。

Conclusion: 建议未来工作需标准化时序作物分割数据集构建的最佳实践，并支持动态tile尺寸以实现理想模型性能。

Abstract: The landscape of satellite imagery time series datasets and boundary-pushing architectures for cropland segmentation has never been richer. However, in this gold rush, important truths are being missed on both fronts, as a drive for the most novel concepts or the largest datasets pushes finer details to the side. In this paper, we present our hybrid transformer-convolutional model, Cropland Parallel Attention and Refinement Network for Segmentation (PAtteRNS), the first model to use self-attention mechanisms separately for each of the temporal, spectral, and spatial aspects of Sentinel-2 multispectral SITS data. To achieve fully-factorised attention in our proposed model, we introduce a novel parallel transformer architecture which significantly reduces the computational complexity of triple-factorised self-attention. We validate our architecture with an in-depth ablation study, and analyse the performance of our model against state-of-the-art crop segmentation models on multiple tile-size variants of the popular PASTIS and MTLCC datasets. Our findings show our model to outperform all others in the task of crop class segmentation, verified across multiple important segmentation metrics, with especially strong performance against compared models seen in the often under-reported parcel delineation quality, for which we use the Boundary IoU metric. We also find that flawed class groupings within datasets can have a significant negative impact on model performance, and report that alternate tile-size variants of crop segmentation datasets produce results incomparable to one-another, invalidating fair comparison between model performance when trained on different tile-sizes. Based on these findings, we suggest further work is required to standardise best practices when constructing SITS crop segmentation datasets, and to enable future dynamic-tile-sizing for ideal model performance.

</details>


### [232] [Adversarial Training for Pixel Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38170)
*Xin Lin,Zhifei Zhang,Yuqian Zhou,Haitian Zheng,Zhe Lin,Ming-Hsuan Yang,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 像素扩散模型直接生成RGB图像，但会系统性地低估自然图像的高频统计量；本文提出对抗式后训练方法，在非高噪声时间步对预测输出加入对抗损失，同时保留原扩散/流匹配目标与采样流程，从而纠正这一缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素扩散模型绕过了自编码器瓶颈，可直接生成RGB图像，但其输出仍系统性地无法充分体现自然图像的细粒度统计特性（如高频内容不足），本文旨在对这一缺陷进行有效修正。

Method: 从预训练模型出发，保留其原有扩散或流匹配目标，在非高噪声时间步对预测输出额外加入对抗损失；模型架构与采样过程均不做改动。这是首个针对像素扩散的对抗式后训练系统性研究。

Result: 在两个像素骨架上，该方法同时提升了分布保真度、覆盖度、提示对齐与感知质量。频率带与幂律分析显示：原始模型系统性地产出不足的自然图像高频内容，对抗后训练恢复了缺失的谱功率；而感知损失虽也增加高频，却牺牲了分布保真度与提示对齐。最近邻、召回率与匹配的无GAN SFT对照实验排除了记忆、模式坍塌与额外优化等简单解释。在测试的潜空间扩散配置下，同样的流程并不能带来可比的联合增益，且几乎不增加解码后的高频功率。

Conclusion: 对抗式后训练能有效纠正像素扩散模型对自然图像高频统计量表征不足的问题，且不改变架构与采样流程。直接访问待修正的图像统计量（即直接输出访问）是决定对抗后训练何时成功的关键因素。

Abstract: Pixel diffusion models generate RGB images directly, avoiding the bottleneck of an autoencoder, yet their outputs still systematically underrepresent fine-scale natural-image statistics. We show that adversarial learning provides an effective post-training correction for this deficiency. Starting from a pretrained model, we retain its original diffusion or flow-matching objective and add an adversarial loss to the predicted output at non-high-noise timesteps, leaving the model architecture and sampling procedure unchanged. To our knowledge, this is the first systematic study of adversarial post-training for pixel diffusion. Across two pixel backbones, the method jointly improves distribution fidelity, coverage, prompt alignment, and perceptual quality. We further investigate why it works. Frequency-band and power-law analyses show that the original models systematically underproduce natural-image high-frequency content, while adversarial post-training restores this missing spectral power. In contrast, perceptual loss also increases high-frequency content but sacrifices distribution fidelity and prompt alignment. Nearest-neighbor, recall, and matched no-GAN SFT controls further rule out memorization, mode dropping, and additional optimization as simple explanations. Finally, we examine the boundary of this effect. Under the tested latent diffusion configurations, the same procedure does not produce comparable joint gains and adds almost no decoded high-frequency power. These results identify direct output access to the image statistics being corrected as a key factor governing when adversarial post-training succeeds.

</details>


### [233] [Point2Part: Unified 3D Partitioning from Point Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38180)
*Hao-Tang Tsui,Yu-Rou Tuan,Xiaoxuan Ma,Nicolas Ugrinovic,Takaaki Shiratori,Kris Kitani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种可提示的3D部件分解模型，将整个形状联合划分为互不重叠且完整覆盖的部件，统一支持图像/网格到部件生成及部件分割。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D部件分解方法不保证部件互不重叠且完整覆盖整个形状，存在重叠或空隙，阻碍下游部件级应用。核心洞察是部件分解应联合考虑所有目标部件，而非独立建模每个部件。

Method: 基于预训练3D生成模型，从图像或网格获取形状潜在表示；引入提示编码器将每个3D点提示映射为部件token并关注形状潜在；提出新颖的部件解码器，以粗到细方式针对所有部件token联合评分整个形状，将形状体积内每个位置恰好分配给一个部件；在共享形状潜在空间中完成分解。

Result: 在全部三项任务（图像到部件生成、网格到部件生成、部件分割）的所有部件质量指标上均超越现有方法，部件间兼容性较此前SOTA提升一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作将部件分解统一为完整形状的联合划分问题，实现可控、非重叠、全覆盖的部件分解，并通过共享潜在空间实现多任务统一模型。代码与模型将开源。

Abstract: Existing 3D part decomposition methods do not necessarily partition the original shape into non-overlapping parts that collectively cover the entire shape, allowing overlaps or gaps that hinder downstream part-level applications. We instead formulate part decomposition as a joint partitioning of the entire shape, where the predicted parts are non-overlapping and jointly recover the entire shape. Our key insight is that part decomposition should consider all desired parts jointly, rather than modeling each part independently. To this end, we develop a promptable model for 3D part decomposition from images or meshes. Users can specify desired parts through 3D point prompts for controllable decomposition. Given one point prompt per desired part, our model produces the corresponding parts as a complete partition of the entire shape. We build on a pretrained 3D generation model and first obtain a shape latent from either an input image or mesh. We then introduce a prompt encoder that maps each 3D point prompt to a part token while attending to the shape latent. To decode the desired parts, we propose a novel part decoder jointly scoring the entire shape against all part tokens in a coarse-to-fine manner, assigning every position within the shape volume to exactly one part. We perform part decomposition in this shared shape latent space, enabling a unified model for image-to-part generation, mesh-to-part generation, and part segmentation. Our method outperforms existing works on all part-quality metrics across all three tasks, and improves compatibility among parts by an order of magnitude over previous SOTA methods. Code and models will be released.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [234] [FD-VAD: Semantic Endpoint Detection for Streaming Full-Duplex Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35791)
*Puneet Mathur,Dinesh Manocha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出FD-VAD，一种无需ASR的流式语义端点检测方法，将语义端点检测建模为因果音频-语言推理任务，直接把有界因果音频窗口映射为Continue/Stop决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全双工语音交互中的自然轮转需要从部分语音判断停顿是犹豫还是完整对话意图。声学VAD缺乏语义信息，而级联ASR的端点检测引入了转录依赖和额外处理阶段，因此需要一种直接的语义端点检测方法。

Method: FD-VAD采用冻结的语音编码器，配合轻量模态适配器和参数高效微调的语言模型，使用last-chunk训练目标实现流式推理。此外引入置信度门控的端点承诺机制来控制打断与延迟的权衡，并使用边界聚焦的难负样本采样来改善模糊轮转边界处的决策。

Result: 在域内和对话评估中，FD-VAD优于强流式与非流式语义轮转分类器，在TurnBench开发集零样本设置下（FP<=0.10）取得合格系统中最高的EOT召回率0.853。

Conclusion: 语义端点检测可以直接从流式音频完成，无需中间ASR或对话状态跟踪。

Abstract: Natural turn-taking in full-duplex voice interaction requires determining from partial speech whether a pause reflects hesitation or a completed conversational intent. Acoustic voice activity detection lacks this semantic information, while cascaded ASR-based endpointing introduces transcription dependence and additional processing stages. We formulate semantic endpoint detection as a causal audio-language reasoning task and introduce FD-VAD, an ASR-free streaming endpointer that maps bounded causal audio windows directly to Continue/Stop decisions. FD-VAD combines a frozen speech encoder with a lightweight modality adapter and a parameter-efficiently adapted language model, using a last-chunk training objective for streaming inference. We further introduce confidence-gated endpoint commitment to control interruption versus delay and boundary-focused hard-negative sampling to improve decisions around ambiguous turn boundaries. Across in-domain and conversational evaluations, FD-VAD outperforms strong streaming and non-streaming semantic turn classifiers, and achieves the highest EOT recall among qualifying systems on TurnBench dev set $0.853$ (at FP<=0.10) in a zero-shot setting. These results show that semantic endpointing can be performed directly from streaming audio without intermediate ASR or dialogue state tracking.

</details>


### [235] [Sieve and Sage: Efficient Distraction Filtering for Reliable RALM Abstention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35794)
*Jongbin Won,Sung Geun An,Jay-yoon Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Sieve-Sage两阶段框架，将检索失败分解为不可回答与干扰两种状态，轻量模块先过滤干扰证据，再由LLM生成或拒答，显著提升准确率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索增强语言模型（RALM）的拒答方法多用单一大型LLM一步处理异构检索失败，导致拒答性能受限且计算成本高。作者认为检索失败可细分为不同类型，需要区别对待。

Method: 将检索失败分解为两类状态：(i)不可回答状态（缺少所需证据）；(ii)干扰状态（相关证据与冲突、否定或对抗性信息混杂）。基于此提出轻量模块Sieve，在调用昂贵LLM（Sage）进行有依据的生成与拒答之前，先筛查检索文档集中的干扰证据。

Result: 在通用领域和高风险专家领域评估，Sieve and Sage框架能预先检测干扰噪声，相比单阶段基线系统准确率最高提升69.4个百分点，Macro-F1提升55.2个百分点，并实现最高1.99倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过分解检索失败状态并采用轻量过滤加LLM生成的两阶段设计，建立了一个高效且可靠的、具备拒答能力的RALM流水线。

Abstract: Just as Socrates recognized the limits of his own knowledge, Retrieval-Augmented Language Models (RALMs) should learn to abstain when the retrieved evidence cannot support a reliable response. Existing approaches largely rely on monolithic LLMs to handle heterogeneous retrieval failures in a single step, resulting in limited abstention performance and high computational costs. We instead decompose retrieval failures into two distinct states: (i) the unanswerable state, where the required evidence is absent, and (ii) the distracted state, where relevant evidence is mixed with conflicting, negated, or adversarial information. Based on this decomposition, we introduce a lightweight module (Sieve) that screens retrieved document sets for distracting evidence before invoking a costly LLM (Sage) for grounded generation and abstention. Evaluated across both general and high-stakes expert domains, our Sieve and Sage framework preemptively detects distracting noise, improving system accuracy by up to 69.4 percentage points and Macro-F1 by 55.2 percentage points compared to one-stage baselines. Furthermore, it achieves up to a 1.99x speedup, establishing a highly efficient and reliable abstention pipeline for RALM with abstention.

</details>


### [236] [Developing an OCR model for Extracting Information from Invoices with Korean Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35796)
*Xiem HoangVan,Phu TranQuang,Minh DinhBao,Tien VuHuu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合深度学习模型与图像预处理技术的OCR模型，用于从韩语发票中自动提取关键信息，实验取得87%的F1分数且处理时间极短。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发票包含购买项目、时间和金额等重要信息，对其信息提取十分关键；韩语使用者约8000万，在韩国及越南、菲律宾等大量韩企所在地均发挥重要作用，因此需要自动从韩语发票中提取有效信息。

Method: 提出一种高效的OCR模型，将深度学习模型与若干图像预处理技术相结合，并在大量收集的发票上进行评估。

Result: 在丰富的发票数据集上评估，模型可达到87%的F1分数，且处理时间可忽略不计。

Conclusion: 该OCR模型能有效且高效地从韩语发票中自动提取信息，具备实际应用价值。

Abstract: Invoices are commercial documents that contain various pieces of information, including the purchased items, time, and total money. Making the extraction of important information crucial. The stored information serves different purposes. Korean language is the native language of about 80 million people, playing an important role in not only South and North Korea but also in many other countries such as Vietnam, Philippine where a large number of Korean companies are located. In this context, to automatically extract proper information from the invoices with Korean language, we propose an efficient Optical Character Recognition (OCR) model in which a deep learning model is combined with some image preprocessing techniques. The proposed OCR model is assessed in a rich set of collected invoices showing that 87% F1-score can be achieved with negligible time processing.

</details>


### [237] [Evaluating the Effects of Prompt Perturbation on Bias and Hallucination in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35804)
*Mamehgol Yousefi,Ahmad Shahi,Mos Sharifi,Alvaro Romera,Simon Hoermann,Tham Piumsomboon*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大语言模型在决策任务中对原始提问扰动变体的鲁棒性，发现扰动在某些模型上可缓解偏见与幻觉，且Claude 3整体表现优于GPT-3.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型日益被用作决策辅助的智能助手，但其可靠性存在偏见与幻觉方面的担忧，因此需要评估模型在面对扰动的提问变体时的鲁棒性。

Method: 在决策任务中，对原始提问施加扰动变体，并评估多个大语言模型（如Claude 3、GPT-3.5）在这些扰动下的表现。

Result: 与先前研究相反，扰动在某些模型上可缓解偏见与幻觉；Claude 3在多数数据集任务中更有效，而GPT-3.5表现不一，部分情况相当，部分情况明显落后。

Conclusion: 研究结果强调在真实应用中部署基于大语言模型的决策支持工具需进行严格测试与验证，以保证可靠性与有效性，并为开发更稳健可信的AI助手提供参考。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable capabilities in various natural language processing tasks, leading to their widespread deployment as intelligent assistants in decision-making contexts. However, the increasing complexity of these models raises concerns about their reliability, particularly regarding bias and hallucination. In this work, we evaluate the robustness of LLMs to perturbed variations of the original inquiry in decision-making tasks. We show that contrary to previous studies, perturbations can mitigate bias and hallucination in some LLMs over other models. It's found that Claude 3 is more effective for the tasks represented in most datasets, whereas models like GPT3.5 exhibit varying levels of adequacy, performing comparably in some cases but falling significantly behind in others. These insights are crucial for understanding the practical implications of deploying LLM-based assistants as effective decision-support tools in real-world applications, emphasising the need for rigorous testing and validation to ensure reliability and effectiveness. This study contributes to the growing body of research on LLM evaluation and provides insights for developing more robust and trustworthy AI assistants in critical decision-making contexts.

</details>


### [238] [Alignment Forecasting: Predicting Misalignment From Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35805)
*Chen Yueh-Han,Bruce W. Lee,Ilia Sucholutsky,Tomek Korbak*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"对齐预测"任务与ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH基准，用LLM评分加简单学习模型组合来预测微调是否会导致对齐失效，效果优于多个基线，并能标记有害训练样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 窄而小的数据缺陷有时会让语言模型广泛失对齐，仅凭数据表面难以判断，目前只能在训练后通过审计模型来发现。为补充事后审计，作者希望在训练前就预测对齐失效。

Method: 定义对齐预测任务：给定目标模型、微调数据集和失效模式（如欺骗、谄媚），输出微调后该失效模式显著增加的概率。构建包含5000多道预测问题的ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH（17个目标模型、32个数据集、16种失效模式）。提出预测支架：由LLM阅读数据集并评估其推动模型走向不当行为的强度与广度，再由一个简单学习模型将该评分与失效模式基率和目标模型既有倾向结合。

Result: 直接提示的前沿模型在该基准上表现很差；所提支架显著高于随机水平，并优于在该任务上微调的模型，也优于能看到较弱模型在同一数据微调后表现的简单预测器。其信号还能发现前沿模型分类器漏掉的有害训练样本；从UltraChat等真实后训练数据中过滤这些样本，在多数情况下使模型在多项选择评估上更对齐，但在开放式对话中的收益不明确。

Conclusion: 要让预测可靠地指导实践中的数据筛选仍需更多进展，但结果表明在SFT设定下，训练前预测多种对齐失效是可行的。

Abstract: Training a language model on data with a narrow flaw can sometimes make the model broadly misaligned. Inspecting the data at face value often does not settle whether it will emerge, and today it is caught only after training, by auditing the resulting model. To complement post-hoc audits, we introduce Alignment Forecasting: the task of predicting alignment failures before training. Given a target model, a fine-tuning dataset, and a failure mode such as deception or sycophancy, a forecaster outputs the probability that fine-tuning would meaningfully increase that failure mode. To measure progress on alignment forecasting, we introduce ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH, a benchmark of over 5,000 forecasting questions spanning 17 target models, 32 datasets, and 16 failure modes. Frontier models prompted directly perform poorly on ALIGNMENTFORECASTBENCH. We therefore propose a forecasting scaffold in which an LLM reads the dataset and rates how strongly and broadly it pushes the model toward misbehavior, and a simple learned model combines that rating with the failure mode's base rate and the target model's prior tendency. This forecasts well above chance, and beats a model fine-tuned on the task and a simple forecaster allowed to see how weaker models behaved after fine-tuning on the same data. Its signals also flag problematic training examples that a frontier-model classifier misses. Filtering those examples out from real post-training data such as UltraChat results in more aligned models on our multiple-choice evaluation in most cases, though the benefit in open-ended conversations is unclear. More progress is needed before forecasts can reliably guide training data curation in practice, but our results suggest that forecasting many alignment failures before training can be tractable in the SFT setting.

</details>


### [239] [From Lexical Baselines to Agentic Retrieval-Augmented Generation: Structured Skill and Responsibility-Level Extraction with the SFIA Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35806)
*Ranuga Disansa,U. S. Samarasinghe,Lasith Gunawardena*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文首次提出面向SFIA框架的、带有责任等级感知的结构化技能抽取任务，并系统比较了5种方法（词法基线、稠密检索+LLM重排、零样本受限LLM、单智能体RAG、三智能体协作）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化技能抽取是劳动力规划的基础，但现有系统多将技能表示为扁平标签，忽略了技能实践的"责任等级"这一维度。SFIA框架恰好捕捉了这一维度（147项技能×7个责任等级），然而尚无可自动化的、基于LLM的SFIA抽取研究。作者希望填补这一空白。

Method: 将任务形式化为从自由文本中预测(skill, level)配对的结构化预测问题。评估五种策略：词法基线、稠密检索+LLM重排、零样本schema受限LLM、单智能体agentic RAG、以及三智能体retriever–matcher–verifier团队。以专家标注的欧洲ICT职位画像为基准，并构建并开源了由全自动智能体流水线生成的SFIA 9语料库。

Result: 基于检索的匹配能识别出最多技能，而生成式策略精确度显著更高；只有将等级作为显式决策进行分配的策略才能可靠预测等级，基于相似度的选择错误率是前者的两倍以上；三智能体协作使延迟翻倍却未提升准确率，说明增加智能体角色并不自动有益于封闭分类体系的匹配。

Conclusion: 该研究为面向SFIA的、结构化且等级感知的技能抽取提供了首个可复现的基线，揭示了检索与生成策略在覆盖率和精确度上的权衡，并证明智能体复杂度并不必然带来性能提升。

Abstract: Automated skill extraction underpins workforce planning, yet most systems represent skills as flat labels with no notion of the responsibility level at which a skill is practiced. The Skills Framework for the Information Age (SFIA) captures exactly this dimension, defining 147 professional skills across seven responsibility levels, but no automated LLM-based extraction targeting SFIA has been reported. We formalize the task as structured prediction of (skill, level) pairs from free text and ask three questions: how accurately can text be mapped onto SFIA's closed vocabulary, which strategies reliably predict the level alongside the skill, and do agentic designs improve on simpler retrieval and prompting? We evaluate five strategies (a lexical baseline, dense retrieval with LLM reranking, a zero-shot schema-constrained LLM, single-agent agentic RAG, and a three-agent retriever--matcher--verifier crew) against expert-mapped European ICT role profiles, all drawing on an SFIA~9 corpus built by a fully automated agentic pipeline that we release. Retrieval-based matching identifies the most skills while generative strategies are markedly more precise; only strategies assigning the level as an explicit decision predict it reliably, with similarity-based selection more than twice as inaccurate; and the crew doubles latency without improving accuracy, so added agent roles do not automatically benefit closed-taxonomy matching. These results provide the first reproducible baseline for structured, level-aware skill extraction against SFIA.

</details>


### [240] [Environment Steering: Using Data Flow Control to Improve Agent Utility and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35807)
*Charlie Summers,Prajwal Raghunath,Aaditya Pai,Mayur Kulkarni,Zhuo Zhang,Oliver Kennedy,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"环境引导"（Environment Steering），将智能体与执行环境状态建模为数据库表，运行时追踪记录级数据流并对照声明式策略检查，检测到违规时通过策略与上下文相关反馈引导智能体走向安全轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体即便被指示安全行为，仍可能发起不安全的工具调用。现有防御要么在执行前约束智能体，要么修改工具输入/输出，或依赖LLM裁判；这些方法依赖模型行为，且往往只是阻断不安全动作，而不帮助智能体恢复或改道。
