Which specific features of non-native English drive this gap remains unclear, because fluency is itself a composite of mechanical accuracy, vocabulary use, organization, and discourse coherence. Here, we introduce FABLE, a controlled dataset of 190,911 English prompt variants derived from 174K real user prompts for writing-related tasks. Evaluating responses from 34 open-weight LLMs, we find a clear asymmetry; while models do not propagate surface errors such as misspellings into their outputs, models do mirror higher-level rhetorical and lexical qualities present in the user's prompt. Further, the overall quality of responses differs substantially between the least- and most-fluent prompts. These results highlight a key LLM performance disparity for non-native English LLM users, resulting in both lower-quality and less-fluent answers.

</details>


### [276] [CineSubBench: Evaluating LLMs on Long-Form Narrative and Cultural Understanding from Multilingual Movie Subtitles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36218)
*Mir Tafseer Nayeem,Susmoy Chakraborty,Davood Rafiei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CineSubBench，一个基于多语言电影字幕的长上下文电影理解评测基准，包含 1,012 部电影、6 种语言、6,072 条字幕轨与 813 万条时间戳字幕条目，覆盖七项叙事、类型、适龄与语言安全任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已在法律、医学、软件工程和网络安全等专业领域被广泛评测，但电影领域相对被忽视。电影理解需要长文本叙事整合、多语言解读和文化情境化的受众判断，因此需要一个专门的评测基准来填补这一空白。

Method: 提出 CineSubBench 基准，以多语言电影字幕为输入。字幕将电影表示为数千条时间有序的短话语，模型需在缺乏显式场景与事件结构的情况下重建人物、关系、事件、因果进展与主题。基准构建了匹配的多任务、多语言、多文化（MultiX）评测设置，含七项任务，涵盖叙事重建与抽象、类型预测、适龄性判断、跨十国分级体系的国别电影分级，以及基于字幕的语言安全性。

Result: 在九个大语言模型上评测发现：情节前提的恢复比事件完整的故事梗概更可靠；跨语言一致性在不同模型和语言之间差异显著；各国分级体系呈现出不同的校准模式；强烈粗俗语比轻度猥亵语更容易被定位和依据。

Conclusion: CineSubBench 确立了电影作为长上下文大语言模型评测领域，并为衡量叙事、多语言、文化与证据支撑能力提供了统一基准。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly evaluated in specialized domains such as law, medicine, software engineering, and cybersecurity, yet film remains comparatively underexplored despite requiring long-form narrative integration, multilingual interpretation, and culturally situated audience judgments. We introduce CineSubBench, a benchmark for evaluating long-context film understanding from multilingual movie subtitles. A subtitle track represents a film as thousands of short, temporally ordered utterances from which models must reconstruct characters, relationships, events, causal progression, and themes without explicit scene or event structure. CineSubBench contains 1,012 films with complete subtitle coverage in six languages, yielding 6,072 tracks and 8.13M timestamped subtitle entries. It provides a matched multi-task, multilingual, and multicultural (MultiX) evaluation setting: seven tasks span narrative reconstruction and abstraction, genre prediction, age suitability, country-specific motion-picture ratings across ten national classification systems, and subtitle-grounded language safety. Across nine LLMs, plot premises are recovered more reliably than event-complete synopses; cross-lingual consistency varies substantially across models and languages; national rating systems expose distinct calibration patterns; and strong profanity is far easier to ground than mild obscenity. CineSubBench establishes film as a long-context LLM evaluation domain and provides a unified benchmark for measuring narrative, multilingual, cultural, and evidence-grounding capabilities.

</details>


### [277] [Cognitive Expert Language Models Better Align with the Corresponding Brain Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36239)
*Zhivar Sourati,Mengxuan Helen Wu,Nona Ghazizadeh,Jonas Kaplan,Morteza Dehghani,Samuel A. Nastase*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过提示和微调构建六个认知领域的专家LLM，发现每个专家模型更匹配对应脑区的神经活动，表明模型-大脑对齐存在领域特异性，跨区域汇总会掩盖区域差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM-大脑对齐研究通常用单一模型衡量不同脑区，再对结果做跨区域汇总，忽视了脑区的功能专门化。作者想验证：面向特定认知领域的模型是否与对应认知领域的脑系统对齐更好。

Method: 通过提示（prompting）与微调（fine-tuning）为六个认知领域（感觉、空间、数值、推理、社会、抽象加工）构建专家LLM变体，并在三个基础模型和三个fMRI数据集上检验各专家是否最能预测对应认知脑区的活动，同时进行控制分析（非认知与表层干预）。

Result: 与假设一致，每个专家模型的表征与对应领域脑系统的对齐程度高于其他专家，且该结果在提示和微调、三种基础模型和三个fMRI数据集下均成立。控制分析显示该对齐特异于认知领域干预，非认知与表层干预无法产生类似对齐。领域专门化改变区域对齐，但整体预测精度基本不变。

Conclusion: 模型-大脑对齐具有认知领域的特异性，跨区域汇总会掩盖特定模型在区域层面的性能差异，提示未来研究应关注脑区功能专门化而非单一模型的全局表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can predict human brain activity across a variety of brain regions during natural language comprehension. Typically, however, LLM-brain alignment is measured using one model for different regions of the brain, and then model performance is summarized across regions. This one-model-fits-all approach ignores the functional specialization of brain regions. In this study, we assess whether a model oriented toward a particular cognitive domain aligns better with the brain system dedicated to that domain. Through prompting and fine-tuning, we first build expert LLM variants for six domains: sensory, spatial, numerical, reasoning, social, and abstract processing. We then examine whether each expert best predicts activity in the brain region associated with the corresponding cognitive domain. Consistent with our hypotheses, each expert's representations align more closely with the brain system most associated with the matching domain than do other experts. This holds under both prompting and fine-tuning, across three base models and three fMRI datasets. In a series of control analyses, we show that this model-brain alignment is specific to cognitive domain interventions; non-cognitive and surface-level interventions do not result in comparable alignment. Specializing models shifts regional alignment while leaving aggregate prediction accuracy largely unchanged, suggesting that summarizing alignment across regions may obscure regional differences in performance for specific models.

</details>


### [278] [Learning from Teacher Continuations at Student States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36246)
*Haojin Wang,Dylan Zhang,Huaibo Chen,Suhao Yu,Yihang Sun,Zhanyang Jin,Jiaying Ye,Dianqi Li,Prasanna Sattigeri,Kamal Youcef-Toumi,Hao Peng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出在线干预蒸馏方法 OLIVE，通过让学生策略生成前缀、教师自回归续写、在教师生成 token 上计算交叉熵更新学生，在推理与智能体任务上优于 OPD 等现有蒸馏方法，且更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蒸馏方法存在三类局限：(1) 离线 SFT 在固定教师轨迹上训练导致序列性的协变量偏移；(2) token 级在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在前缀失败时监督碎片化；(3) 分布匹配式蒸馏需要访问教师的 token 概率。

Method: OLIVE 每次迭代中，演化的学生策略生成新前缀，教师自回归续写，学生基于教师生成的 token 计算交叉熵进行更新；对每个设计选择针对性地解决对应局限，并采用异步实现加速训练。

Result: 在同等的 GPU 小时开销下，OLIVE 在推理性能上超过带 top-16 KL 近似 的 OPD；异步实现使总训练时间再减少 23.8%。在困难推理任务与智能体任务上，相同训练预算下持续优于现有蒸馏方法；离线蒸馏收敛后仍能继续提升，并更好保持通用能力与可塑性。仅使用 GPT-5.4-mini 的文本，在 ScienceWorld 上持续用 OLIVE 训练比同教师离线 SFT 高出 13%。

Conclusion: OLIVE 是一种有效且高效的在线语言模型蒸馏方法。

Abstract: We present OLIVE (OnLine InterVEntion). At each iteration, the evolving student policy generates a new prefix, the teacher continues it autoregressively, and the student is updated using cross-entropy computed on the teacher-generated tokens. Each design choice targets a corresponding limitation of existing distillation methods: (1) sequential covariate shift in offline supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on fixed teacher trajectories, (2) fragmented supervision under prefix failure in token-level on-policy distillation (OPD), and (3) the need for access to teacher token probabilities in distribution-matching distillation. OLIVE achieves higher reasoning performance than OPD (with a top-16 KL approximation) at comparable GPU-hour cost. Our asynchronous implementation further reduces OLIVE's total training time by 23.8\%. We evaluate OLIVE on both hard reasoning tasks and agentic tasks which reflects modern post-training scenarios, and it consistently outperforms existing distillation methods under the same training budget. By regenerating prefixes from the evolving student, OLIVE continues improving after offline distillation plateaus while better preserving the general capabilities and plasticity of the student. Using only text from GPT-5.4-mini, continuously training with OLIVE outperforms offline SFT from the same teacher by 13\% on ScienceWorld. These results support OLIVE as an effective and efficient approach to online language-model distillation.

</details>


### [279] [Population Fidelity: Evaluating Population Representativeness in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36253)
*Neemias B. da Silva,Martin Lukk,Ali Sutani,Abhishek Moturu,Harris Yang,Daniel Silver,Matt Ratto,Thiago H. Silva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"Population Fidelity"评估框架，从群体准确度、群体间变异量及其结构三个维度，衡量LLM生成回答对人群态度的表征能力，并据此揭示机器偏见来源与文化微调的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现LLM生成的回答会压缩人群态度的分布范围，并错误表征特定子群体，且这种偏差随模型和主题而变化。然而，现有评估手段难以区分"代表性不足"的具体成因（是群体间变异不足，还是变异分配错误），也缺乏统一标准来检验改进方法是否真正提升了群体代表性。

Method: 提出Population Fidelity评估框架，包含三个维度：(1)群体层面的准确度；(2)群体间变异的数量；(3)该变异的结构。作者通过两项应用验证框架效用：一是复现此前的LLM调查回答"机器偏见"研究，并将其应用于原模型及更新的模型；二是评估文化微调（cultural fine-tuning）这一提升群体代表性的方法。

Result: 框架应用表明：LLM代表性不佳不仅源于群体间变异不足，还因为变异被错误地分配给了错误的群体；文化微调能提升与调查中心的整体对齐度，但并未改善对群体内部差异的表征，而这类区别是聚合一致性指标无法捕捉的。

Conclusion: 表征一个群体要求模型同时再现人类态度变异的多个特征。Population Fidelity框架对这些特征进行了系统组织，并提供了可复用的代码、数据和训练模型，可用于跨领域评估群体保真度及检验各类对齐方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show considerable potential in simulating human attitudes and preferences. Prior work finds that LLM-generated responses can compress the range of attitudes found within populations and misrepresent particular subgroups in ways that vary across models and topics. We introduce Population Fidelity, an evaluation framework that distinguishes key conditions required for a set of LLM-generated responses to represent a population. It incorporates three dimensions: group-level accuracy, the amount of between-group variation, and the structure of that variation. We demonstrate the framework's utility in two ways. First, we reproduce a prior study of "machine bias" in LLM survey responses and apply the framework to its models and more recent ones, showing that poor representation reflects not only insufficient between-group variation but also variation assigned to the wrong groups. Second, we evaluate one proposed approach to improving models' population representativeness: cultural fine-tuning. We find that cultural fine-tuning can improve alignment with the survey center without improving the representation of within-population differences, a distinction that measures of aggregate agreement do not capture. We argue that representing a population requires models to reproduce several features of human attitudinal variation simultaneously. Our framework organizes these features and provides reusable code, data, and trained models for evaluating population fidelity across substantive domains and assessing proposed alignment methods.

</details>


### [280] [HeurEvo: Agentic Evolution of Hybrid Solver-Augmented Heuristics for Time-Critical Mathematical Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36303)
*Feijie Wu,Hugo Barbalho,Konstantina Mellou,Marco Molinaro,Jing Gao,Ishai Menache,Xinzhi Zhang,Sirui Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 HeurEvo，一个"规划—编码—组件"协同进化框架，自动设计融合启发式与数学规划求解器的高性能混合算法，在严格时间预算下取得与顶尖求解器相当甚至更优的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际优化场景要求在严格运行时间约束下获得高质量解，需要将问题特定启发式与强大的数学规划求解器结合的混合方法。但现有方法通常只在预定义流程内改进启发式组件，或孤立地调优求解器配置，无法整体性地决定"在哪里分配计算、如何利用求解器、如何改进整体算法结构"。

Method: HeurEvo 是一个自动化的 plan–code–component 协同进化框架，联合进化高层算法结构、其实现以及共享的可复用组件池。其中 planner 决定使用哪些算法组件、如何组合它们、以及如何在各阶段分配运行时间；coder 将规划实现为可执行代码；component evolver 更新共享组件池。整个框架基于 island 式进化机制，规划与实现借助解释器智能体（分析执行结果并识别改进机会）的反馈进行协同进化。

Result: 在多种组合优化基准和具有挑战性的 MIPLIB 实例上，HeurEvo 能在紧张的时间预算内找到高质量解，常常达到或在数小时至数天计算量下超越最先进的优化求解器；在六边形堆积等若干非线性几何问题上，还改进了此前报告的最好结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明，对算法结构与实现进行联合搜索，对于智能体式启发式设计具有重要价值。

Abstract: Recent advances in agentic heuristic design use AI agents and execution feedback to automate algorithm discovery for challenging optimization problems. In many practical settings, high-quality solutions must be obtained under strict runtime constraints, motivating hybrid approaches that combine problem-specific heuristics with powerful mathematical programming solvers. However, existing approaches typically improve heuristic components within predefined procedures or tune solver configurations in isolation. This limits holistic adaptation of where to allocate computation, how to leverage solvers, and how to refine the overall algorithmic structure. To address these limitations, we propose HeurEvo, an automated plan--code--component co-evolution framework that jointly evolves the high-level algorithmic structures, their implementations, and a shared pool of reusable components. A planner determines which algorithmic components to use, how to combine them, and how to allocate runtime across stages, a coder realizes the resulting plan as executable code, while a component evolver updates the shared component pool. Within an island-based evolutionary framework, plans and implementations co-evolve with feedback from an interpreter agent that analyzes execution results and identifies opportunities for improvement. Across diverse combinatorial optimization benchmarks and challenging MIPLIB instances, HeurEvo finds high-quality solutions within tight runtime budgets, often matching or surpassing state-of-the-art optimization solvers given hours or days of computation. On several nonlinear geometry problems such as hexagon packing, it also improves upon the best previously reported results. These results highlight the value of jointly searching over algorithmic structure and implementation for agentic heuristic design.

</details>


### [281] [Training LLMs to Verbalize Evaluation Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36316)
*Usman Anwar,Sahar Abdelnabi,David Krueger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"言语化训练"(VT)，让大模型更愿意说出自己对"正在被评测"的觉察，同时不直接监督其潜在信念；在三个模型上使言语化评估觉察提升2.4-2.9倍，且潜在觉察与行为基本不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估觉察(EA)会让大模型在审计与部署时表现不一致，但测量与处理EA仍很困难。已有方法要么难以捕捉模型未说出的觉察，要么直接监督潜在信念本身，可能干扰模型真实表征。

Method: VT利用模型自发的言语化作为"觉察存在"的证据，在每个rollout中于言语化之前立即截断，构造出模型"被推定已觉察"的训练前缀；再用强化学习目标对这些前缀训练，以校准的方式增加言语化频率，而不监督潜在信念。

Result: 在Qwen3.6-35B-A3B、Kimi K2.6与Inkling上，VT使言语化EA提升2.4-2.9倍，并能迁移到未见的智能体场景；测得的潜在EA与行为基本保持稳定。因果实验中，通过合成文档微调独立植入关于评测的元知识后，VT诱导的言语化反映了模型新获得的知识。

Conclusion: VT提供了一种在不直接监督潜在信念的前提下提升评估觉察可测量性、并保持行为一致性的可行路径，有助于审计场景下的可靠评估。

Abstract: Evaluation awareness (EA) can cause large language models (LLMs) to behave differently during audits than in deployment, yet measuring and accounting for EA remains challenging. We introduce verbalization training (VT), a method for making LLMs less reticent about verbalizing evaluation awareness while avoiding to supervise the latent belief itself. VT uses a model's spontaneous verbalizations as evidence that awareness is present and truncates each rollout immediately before the verbalization, producing training prefixes at which the model is presumed to be aware. The model is then trained with an RL objective designed to increase verbalization in a calibrated way. Across Qwen3.6-35B-A3B, Kimi K2.6, and Inkling, VT increases verbalized EA by 2.4-2.9 times and transfers to held-out agentic settings, while measured latent EA and behavior remain largely stable. In a causal experiment, we independently implant meta-knowledge about evaluations through synthetic-document fine-tuning and show that VT-induced verbalizations reflect the richer knowledge acquired by the model.

</details>


### [282] [DeepRewind: Predicting and Repairing Premature Commitments in Deep Research Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36344)
*Amirhossein Abaskohi,Amirhossein Dabiriaghdam,Lele Wang,Peter West,Giuseppe Carenini*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DeepRewind为深度研究智能体增加可逆控制层，将其认知状态建模为类型化图，提前预测承诺风险并支持依赖感知回滚，从而提升洞察召回并大幅减少过早承诺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究智能体需进行长程迭代搜索与推理，但常在证据不足时过早形成结论，后续推理会不断强化错误解读。

Method: 构建类型化图表示智能体的演化认知状态（来源、证据、断言、假设、前提、承诺、计划、草稿）；在接受中间结论前，用基于提示的世界模型预测其影响，并从假设收窄、信息损失、恢复成本、矛盾触发覆盖度评估可逆性；用二元控制器阻止高风险承诺，用一致性监测器在后续证据推翻时执行依赖感知回滚。

Result: 在DRBench和LiveDRBench上，相比Open Deep Research，洞察召回提升3.6个百分点，过早承诺减少59.1%。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模认知状态并引入可逆控制，能有效抑制深度研究中的过早承诺并提升研究质量。

Abstract: Deep-research agents conduct long-horizon investigations through iterative search, evidence evaluation, belief revision, and synthesis. However, they may commit to claims before sufficient evidence is available, causing later reasoning to reinforce an incorrect interpretation. We introduce DeepRewind, an additive control layer for reversible deep research that represents the agent's evolving epistemic state as a typed graph of sources, evidence, claims, hypotheses, assumptions, commitments, plans, and drafts. Before accepting an intermediate conclusion, a prompt-based world model predicts its impact and estimates reversibility based on hypothesis narrowing, information loss, recovery cost, and contradiction-trigger coverage. A binary controller blocks risky commitments, while a consistency monitor performs dependency-aware rollback when later evidence invalidates them. Across DRBench and LiveDRBench, DeepRewind improves insight recall by 3.6 percentage points and reduces premature commitments by 59.1% relative to Open Deep Research.

</details>


### [283] [Calibrated to Whom? Persona and Language Effects on Cultural Values in JEV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36399)
*Bushra Asseri,Abdulaziz Asseri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 审计决策型语言模型 JEV 的文化价值取向：它以人格化设定作答时能复现沙特-美国人类差异，但复现程度受题目语言影响（英语87%、阿拉伯语62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策型语言模型（仅输出各选项概率、不生成文本）适合充当问卷受访者或评判者，但其文化价值取向是否可靠、是否受语言与人格设定影响尚不清楚，因此需要系统审计。

Method: 用 Values Survey Module 2013 的24个题目，构造12个匹配的沙特人格与12个匹配的美国人格以及无人格设定，分别用英语和阿拉伯语，采用8种请求表述方式，共获得288,000条答案，分析重复性（ICC）、人格效应、语言效应、年龄与性别效应及置信度。

Result: 答案高度可重复（ICC 0.997）；无人格时接近其美国人格的作答。人格为沙特时答案朝人类沙特-美国差异方向移动，英语下复现该差异的87%，阿拉伯语下仅62%，且长期取向出现反转。语言交叉实验显示阿拉伯语下差异变小源于题目语言而非人格描述语言。年龄对画像的影响与国籍相当，性别对沙特人格影响大于美国人格，模型在阿拉伯语和沙特人格下置信度更低。这些模式在所有请求设计下均成立。

Conclusion: 决策型模型的文化作答具有高稳定性且能部分复现人类文化差异，但存在显著的语言相关偏差（尤其长期取向反转）和系统性置信度差异，使用此类模型作为调查受访者或评判者时需谨慎对待语言与人格设定的影响。

Abstract: Decision-only language models return a probability for every answer option instead of generating text, which makes them attractive as survey respondents and as judges. We audit the cultural values of one such model, TypeSafe's JEV, with the Values Survey Module 2013. We asked it the 24 items as 12 matched Saudi and 12 matched American personas and without a persona, in English and Arabic, under eight ways of formulating the request (288,000 answers). JEV's answers were highly repeatable (ICC 0.997), and without a persona they resembled those of its own American personas. When the persona was Saudi rather than American, the answers moved in the direction of the human Saudi-US difference, reproducing 87% of its size in English but 62% in Arabic, with long-term orientation reversed. A language cross shows that the smaller difference in Arabic comes from the language of the items, not from the language of the persona description. Age shifted the profiles about as much as nationality, gender shifted them more for Saudi than for American personas, and JEV was less confident in Arabic and for Saudi personas. These patterns held in every request design, although the model never generates text.

</details>


### [284] [Eternal Sunshine of the Spotless Mind: Systematically Erasing LLM's Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36414)
*Olga Ohrimenko*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"LLM记忆删除"新研究方向，发现现有LLM无法真正遗忘用户信息，并提出DeLLM框架通过动态构建上下文与消息溯源图来实现有效删除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化LLM通过外部存储累积用户交互记忆，可在响应时调用全部历史交互。但用户可能要求删除某些信息，而现有LLM即使声称已遗忘，实际上仍无法真正删除，且在有限上下文下同样失败。因此需要研究LLM记忆删除问题。

Method: 作者指出仅删除与用户删除请求匹配的消息是不够的，因为对话会自然引入消息依赖，导致信息残留。为此提出DeLLM框架：为每个LLM查询动态构建相关上下文，并维护消息的溯源图（provenance graph），以确定删除时需要移除哪些消息。

Result: 实验表明，DeLLM在保持实用性的同时实现了较高的删除率。

Conclusion: DeLLM能够有效应对LLM记忆删除请求，兼顾删除效果与系统效用，为LLM记忆管理提供了新思路。

Abstract: We consider persistent LLMs that accumulate memories of their interactions with a user over time. Such LLMs maintain memories using external storage, which they can query to overcome the limitations of a fixed context window. Such systems have numerous practical applications, as they can draw on all past interactions when responding to user queries.
  In this paper, we ask whether LLMs can forget information shared with them upon a user's request. We find that current LLMs fail to delete such information---even when they claim to have forgotten it and even when operating with a limited context. To this end, we consider a new direction of study: Deletion of LLM Memories.
  We show that naively removing messages that match a user's deletion request is insufficient, since conversations naturally introduce message dependencies that cause information to persist. To correctly handle deletion requests, we propose the DeLLM framework. It dynamically constructs relevant context for each LLM query and maintains a provenance graph of messages to determine which ones must be removed during deletion. Our experiments show that DeLLM achieves a high deletion rate while maintaining utility.

</details>


### [285] [MemFold: Learning Compact Soft Memory for Long-Context Personalization via On-Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36435)
*Jingxuan Wu,Yuzhe Yang,Yiqiao Huang,Chengzhi Liu,Qingni Wang,Chengxuan Qian,Shutong Wu,Jiawei Zhang,Xin Eric Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MemFold 提出一种按"行为效果"优化的固定预算软记忆：将查询条件下的文本记忆压缩为 K 个连续向量作为阅读器接口，并用组相对奖励与置信度门控的在线蒸馏训练阅读器，在长历史场景下取得最高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期服务同一用户的助手需从用户已透露的信息中判断哪些偏好仍有效、哪些被修改、哪些约束当前适用；然而"保留信息"与"据信息行动"常被当作同一目标优化，且文本记忆使输入随历史增长，而固定数量隐向量的压缩通常以重建文本或模仿参考答案为目标——这些评分序列并非阅读器自身产生的。因此需要一个按其所支撑的行为来优化固定预算记忆的方法。

Method: MemFold 将查询条件下的文本记忆压缩为 K 个连续向量，构成阅读器的记忆接口；阅读器在自身 rollout 上接受两种互补信号训练：针对任务结果的组相对奖励，以及置信度门控的在线策略蒸馏——由冻结的文本记忆教师对学生在文本记忆下采样的 token 重新打分。教师从不被采样，监督始终处于学生当前分布上且不增加自回归解码；推理时教师被完全移除。

Result: 在三种 Qwen 主干上，MemFold 在 PersonaMem-32K 与 PersonaMem-128K 上取得所测最高准确率，且历史越长优势越大；无需目标域训练即可迁移到 PrefEval 与 LongMemEval。消融显示任务增益主要来自奖励项，教师信号带来较小额外增益；记忆干预表明阅读器依赖其软记忆中实例特定的内容。

Conclusion: 以行为（而非重建）为目标优化固定预算软记忆是有效的：结合结果奖励与在线蒸馏可显著提升长历史个性化任务表现，且收益随历史长度扩大并具备跨域迁移能力，同时推理阶段无需教师，接口规模保持固定。

Abstract: An assistant that serves the same user over a long horizon has to answer from what that user has revealed: which preferences still hold, which were revised, and which constraints apply now. Retaining that information is not the same as acting on it, and the two are usually optimized as if they were. Keeping the information as text makes the reader's input grow with the retained history, while compressing it into a fixed number of latent vectors bounds the interface but is typically trained to reconstruct text or imitate reference answers, both of which are scored on sequences the reader never produced. We present MemFold, which optimizes a fixed-budget soft memory by the behavior it supports. A query-conditioned textual memory is compressed into K continuous vectors that form the reader's memory interface, and the reader is then trained on its own rollouts under two complementary signals: group-relative rewards for task outcomes, and confidence-gated on-policy distillation in which a frozen textual-memory teacher re-scores the student's sampled tokens under the textual memory. The teacher is never sampled from, so supervision stays on the student's current distribution and adds no autoregressive decoding; at inference it is removed entirely. Across three Qwen backbones, MemFold attains the highest accuracy we measure on PersonaMem-32K and PersonaMem-128K, with margins that widen at the longer history length, and transfers to PrefEval and LongMemEval without target-domain training. Ablations attribute most of the task gain to the reward term and a smaller additional gain to the teacher signal, and memory interventions show that the reader depends on the instance-specific content of its soft memory.

</details>


### [286] [Reliable Parallel Decoding in Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36452)
*Zhenghao He,Bohan Liu,Guangzhi Xiong,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）中并行提交多个掩码预测的可靠性问题，提出无需训练的解码方法RPD，通过层间预测稳定性与最终置信度筛选候选，并在前缀掩码位置的累积熵预算下并行提交，从而在数学推理和代码生成任务上实现最高吞吐且保持或提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MDLM可并行预测多个掩码token以提升生成效率，但同一次前向传播得到的预测在同时提交时未必可靠。作者希望明确"何时并行提交是可靠的"：仅凭置信度无法决定可靠的提交顺序，序列末端的高置信预测可能在其支撑计算尚未建立前就固定答案，而下游预测会随上游上下文不确定性增加而变得不可靠。

Method: 作者先进行诊断分析，发现：单次前向已能解决若干掩码token；在最后几层仍保持稳定的预测更可能正确。基于此提出Reliable Parallel Decoding（RPD），一种免训练方法：按层间预测稳定性与最终置信度选择候选，并在其前置掩码位置的累积熵预算约束下进行提交；对上游上下文不确定的预测予以延后，其余候选并行提交，不依赖固定分块调度。

Result: 在LLaDA与Dream模型上，针对数学推理与代码生成基准，RPD在所评估方法中取得最高的解码吞吐量，同时保持或提升了准确率。

Conclusion: 并行提交的可靠性可由层间预测稳定性与累积熵预算共同刻画；RPD无需训练、不依赖固定块调度，即可在提升解码速度的同时维持甚至改善生成质量。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) can generate text efficiently by predicting multiple masked tokens in parallel, but predictions from the same forward pass are not necessarily reliable when committed together. We study when parallel commitment is reliable. Our diagnostics show that confidence alone does not determine a reliable commitment order: confident predictions near the end of the sequence can fix an answer before its supporting computations are established, and downstream predictions become less reliable as the uncertainty of their upstream context grows. At the same time, a single forward pass can already resolve several masked tokens, and predictions that remain stable across the final layers are more likely to be correct. Based on these findings, we propose Reliable Parallel Decoding (RPD), a training-free method that selects candidates by layerwise prediction stability and final confidence, and commits them under a cumulative entropy budget over their preceding masked positions. RPD defers predictions with uncertain upstream context while committing the remaining candidates in parallel, without relying on a fixed block schedule. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks on LLaDA and Dream, RPD achieves the highest decoding throughput among the evaluated methods while maintaining or improving accuracy.

</details>


### [287] [Memory Consolidation Flattens the Temporal Shape of User Facts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36457)
*Sugam Panthi,Muhaiminul Yeamin,Siyan Luo,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"体貌扁平化"现象：长期记忆系统在将对话转为存储笔记时会选择性地丢失时态线索（尤其将进行时扁平化为一般现在时），导致后续模型无法判断事实是否仍然成立，并据此影响其行动决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆系统把对话压缩成简短笔记时可能丢失事实的时效信息，例如"我正在开标致车"被写成"用户开标致车"，失去了活动仍在进行的线索，从而影响下游模型判断事实是否仍然成立。

Method: 定义"体貌扁平化"，构建 LAPSE 基准，包含仅时态形式不同、其余匹配的用户陈述对；用该基准测试多个记忆写入模型及 mem0、Graphiti、Letta 等实际部署管线，并开展探索性读者实验：仅改变存储动词后观察读者对事实仍成立的估计，以及允许提问时读者的行动与提问行为。

Result: 三种写入模型在381对中有244对把进行时陈述扁平化却保留了其简单现在时对应句，反向从未出现；该不对称在全部11种模型配置及三个部署管线中均成立。仅改变存储动词即可改变三个读者对事实仍成立的估计；当读者可先询问用户时，三个中有两个在扁平化笔记上更常不询问直接行动。计划的记忆使用任务因读者几乎对所有存储事实（含过期事实）都采取行动而未能检测到该效应。

Conclusion: 记忆写入过程会移除后续模型用于判断是否行动的证据，体貌扁平化是一种由写入系统引入的系统性信息损失，可能削弱记忆系统对事实时效性的支持。

Abstract: Long-term memory systems turn conversations into short stored notes. A note can keep a user fact while losing evidence about whether the fact still holds. For example, "I am driving a Peugeot" can become "The user drives a Peugeot," which drops the cue that the activity is ongoing. We call this aspectual flattening and measure it with LAPSE, a benchmark of matched user statements that differ only in temporal form. We find that memory writers flatten aspect selectively. Three writer models flattened the progressive statement but kept its simple-present match in 244 of 381 pairs, never the reverse. The asymmetry holds in all 11 model configurations tested and in the installed pipelines mem0, Graphiti, and Letta. The lost cue matters to later readers. In exploratory tests, changing only the stored verb shifted all three readers' estimates that a fact still holds. When readers could ask the user before acting, two of three acted without asking more often on flattened notes. Our planned memory-use task could not detect this, because readers there acted on almost every stored fact, even expired ones. Memory writing can thus remove evidence that later models use to decide whether to act.

</details>


### [288] [FinRT: Distilling Adaptive Red-Teaming Strategies into Reusable Adversarial Generators in Consumer Finance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36474)
*Rikhiya Ghosh,Himanshu Kumar,Sriram Venkatapathy,Sahil Wadhwa,Alexandre G. R. Day,Pranab Mohanty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinRT框架，通过自适应红队策略构建可复用的对抗性提示生成器，在消费金融领域六大受害模型上显著优于自适应搜索基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费金融等受监管行业中，看似无害的用户查询可能利用大语言模型漏洞，引发安全失效并使回答逼近政策边界。现有自动化红队方法难以兼顾攻击效果与生成成本，且将覆盖度、严重性和多样性视为附带目标，而非联合优化目标。

Method: FinRT是一个结构化框架，从自适应红队策略中构建可复用的对抗性提示生成器，将面向目标的攻击生成过程摊销为可复用生成器，从而降低生成成本。

Result: 在消费金融领域的六个受害模型上，FinRT大幅超越自适应搜索基线。攻击成功率几乎是Rainbow Teaming的两倍（32.9% vs 17.2%），最大对抗严重性提升33%，并保持与迭代搜索方法相当的政策域内语义多样性。同时实现较高的跨模型迁移性，并展现出不同的受害模型家族特化模式。

Conclusion: FinRT通过可复用生成器在降低成本的同时提升了攻击成功率、严重性和多样性，并具备良好的跨模型迁移能力，为受监管行业的大模型安全红队提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In regulated industries like consumer finance, seemingly harmless user queries can exploit large language model vulnerabilities, triggering safety failures and pushing responses dangerously close to policy limits. Existing automated red-teaming methods trade off attack effectiveness against generation cost, while treating coverage, severity, and diversity as incidental rather than joint objectives. We introduce FinRT, a structured framework that builds reusable adversarial prompt generators from adaptive red-teaming strategies. Across the six victim models in consumer finance, FinRT substantially outperforms adaptive search baselines while amortizing target-facing attack generation into a reusable generator. FinRT nearly doubles the attack success rate over the adaptive baseline Rainbow Teaming (32.9% vs. 17.2%), increases maximum adversarial severity by 33%, and preserves comparable intra-policy-domain semantic diversity to iterative search methods. Our method achieves high cross-model transferability while exhibiting distinct victim-family specialization patterns.

</details>


### [289] [Similar Choices, Different Attention: Cross-Modal Associations in Humans and Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36475)
*Sumin Hong,Katsumi Ibaraki,Renee Shi,David Chiang,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较人类与视觉语言模型（VLM）在跨模态联想任务中的选择与注视行为，发现即使较大的VLM在选词上与人类一致，其显著性图与人类注视的相关性还不如"中心偏置"基线；微调可提升选择一致性，但注意力仍不及基线，说明"选择一致"或"注视模式一致"都不足以证明模型的跨模态加工与人类对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究多比较人类与VLM在跨模态联想（如bouba/kiki效应）上的选择，但人类与模型使用的刺激或任务常不一致，且仅比较最终选择而忽略"看哪里"这一过程信息。作者希望在同刺激、同任务条件下，同时考察选择与眼动，检验VLM是否在结果与过程两个层面都与人类对齐。

Method: 给53名人类被试与多个VLM呈现相同刺激——一个伪词与两幅图像，记录人类的选择与眼动（数据公开）。将VLM的选择、显著性图与人类选择、注视数据对比，并以"中心偏置"（图像中心固定高斯）作为基线。此外，用小规模人类选择数据微调小型VLM，并用人类注视训练模型注意力，检验其对选择一致性与注意力-注视相关性的影响。

Result: 少数较大的VLM在选词上与人类对齐，但其显著性图与人类注视的匹配程度低于中心偏置基线。微调后，小型VLM在未见词与图像上的选择一致性达到人类多数投票参考水平，但注意力仍不及中心基线。用人类注视训练注意力可提高注意力-注视相关性，但不改善选择一致性；而每张图像位置上取一张平均注视图也能带来相近的相关性提升。

Conclusion: 模型在跨模态联想上复现人类选择，甚至复现人类注视模式，都不足以作为其加工过程与人类对齐的证据；评价VLM的类人性需同时考察过程指标并设立强基线。

Abstract: Cross-modal associations are systematic pairings of features across modalities, such as the association of 'bouba' with round shapes and 'kiki' with sharp shapes. Prior work has compared humans and vision-language models (VLMs) on such associations, but often using different stimuli or tasks between humans and models. Here, we ask whether VLMs align with humans not only in choices, but also in where they look when making those choices. We study both VLMs and humans (N = 53), presenting them with the same stimuli, a pseudo-word and two images, and record participants' choices and eye movements, which we release. We find choice alignment in a few larger VLMs, but their saliency matches human gaze less closely than a center-bias baseline, a fixed Gaussian at the center of each image. Fine-tuning small VLMs on human choices brings their choice alignment to the level of a human majority-vote reference on unseen words and images, yet their attention still matches human gaze less closely than this baseline. Training model attention on human gaze raises attention-gaze correlation without improving choice alignment, and a single average gaze map per image position raises it by a similar amount. Matching human choices, or even human gaze patterns, is therefore not sufficient evidence of human-aligned cross-modal processing.

</details>


### [290] [Large-scale factor analysis shows machine intelligence is only partially interpretable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36515)
*Faiz Ghifari Haznitrama,Afrizal Hasbi Azizy,Faeyza Rishad Ardi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究用潜变量/因子分析方法检验语言模型中是否存在类似人类流体智力的“通用智能因子(g)”。分析了1,618个模型、456个基准、13,251个评测分数后发现，通用因子解释力有限且不稳定，内容相似的基准不一定聚在一起，g因子也未被标准“智能”基准很好地代表，因此质疑将通用智能作为语言模型开发中可明确定位目标的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型开发中普遍假设认知能力围绕一个类似人类流体智力的通用、领域无关智能因子组织，但这一假设很少被直接检验，且以往检验规模较小。作者希望以心理测量学的潜变量视角，在大规模数据上更严谨地检验该假设。

Method: 采用潜变量与因子分析（作为降维技术）方法，把每个具体问题集的表现视为受领域特定与领域无关潜因子共同影响。使用13,251个已发表评测分数，覆盖1,618个语言模型与456个纯文本基准。由于数据超级稀疏，作者在不同数据稠密化(densifier)与插补方法之间进行三角验证，以检验结论的稳健性。

Result: 在多种偏置模式下呈现稳健模式：(1) 通用智能因子在最宽松估计下仅解释约70.8%的表现方差，在多数解中远低于此；(2) 内容相似的基准并不必然聚为一类；(3) g因子不被任何共同主题主导，且缺乏证据表明标准“智能”基准能很好地代理它。

Conclusion: 研究结果与当前在语言模型开发中将通用智能定义、识别并作为具体可触及建构来追求的努力相悖，不利于以单一概念能力为目标的策略，因为其所需触及的一阶能力往往具有部分特异性且在实践中不可识别。

Abstract: A common assumption in language model development is that cognitive abilities are organized around a general, domain-free intelligence factor, like fluid intelligence in humans. This assumption is rarely tested directly, and prior attempts have done so only at a much smaller scale. We take a latent variable approach to intelligence in language models, similar to how psychometricians study psychological constructs. Performance in every specific problem set is influenced by a domain-specific and a domain-agnostic latent factor. Using factor analysis as a dimension-reduction technique, we analyzed 13,251 published evaluation scores covering 1,618 language models across 456 different text-only benchmarks. Due to the super-sparse nature of the dataset, we triangulate our analysis across different data densifiers and imputation methods. A robust pattern across different modes of bias is that 1. A general intelligence factor accounts for 70.8% of variance in model performance at our most generous estimate, and far less than that in most of our solutions, 2. Content-similar benchmarks do not necessarily cluster together, and 3. The $g$ factor is not dominated by any common theme, and there is a lack of evidence that it is well-proxied by standard "intelligence" benchmarks. Our findings go against current endeavors of defining, identifying, and targeting general intelligence as a tangible construct in language model development. This leaves the strategy of targeting a single conceptual ability without support, since the first-order abilities it would have to reach are often partially idiosyncratic and not identifiable in practice.

</details>


### [291] [Retrieval Sensitivity to Identity Signals in Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36534)
*Andrew Tang,Nicholas Deas,Kathleen McKeown,Vishal Misra*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究稠密检索器是否会因查询中表达的身份信号（政治倾向与方言）而产生检索偏见。在政治新闻与消费者健康两个领域设计评估，发现所有检索器都会返回与查询自身政治倾向一致的文档，且对非裔美国人语言（AAL）查询的表现劣于白人主流英语（WME）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有稠密检索器通常只在中性查询下评估，但真实用户查询会携带身份信号。作者希望探究这些身份信号是否会系统性地影响检索结果，从而带来极化与健康信息获取不平等等风险。

Method: 在两个领域（政治新闻、消费者健康问题）构建评估：每个领域均包含一个仅改变身份信号的控制合成查询集，以及真实自然查询集。评测对象为五个稠密检索器和一个稀疏基线。进一步做两项分析：一是剔除聚合词汇不对称分数，二是用线性探针从查询嵌入中恢复倾向与方言信息。

Result: 所有检索器都表现出：1）倾向于返回与查询自身政治立场一致的新闻文章；2）对AAL书写的健康问题检索性能低于WME。控制词汇层面特征后，合成集上的差距仍然存在，线性探针也能从查询嵌入中超出词元级特征地恢复政治倾向与方言。

Conclusion: 检索偏见并非仅由表层词汇差异导致，而是编码在检索器的查询表示中。若不加干预，这类偏见可能加剧政治极化，并强化AAL使用者本就面临的信息与健康差距。

Abstract: Dense retrievers decide which documents reach users and the language models that use them, yet they are typically evaluated with neutral queries. We ask whether the identity signals that real users express in their queries---political ideology and dialect---bias what a retriever returns. We design evaluations in two domains, political news and consumer-health questions, each pairing a controlled synthetic set that varies only the identity signal with naturalistic queries. Across five dense retrievers and a sparse baseline, every retriever (i) retrieves articles that align with the query's own political lean and (ii) performs worse for questions written in African American Language (AAL) than in White Mainstream English (WME). Two analyses tie these gaps to queries' identity signals beyond surface vocabulary: partialling out an aggregate lexical-asymmetry score leaves the synthetic gaps largely intact, and linear probes recover lean and dialect from the retrievers' query embeddings beyond token-level features. Left unaddressed, such retrieval biases risk contributing to polarization and reinforcing the health disparities already faced by AAL speakers. Code is available at https://github.com/Andrewtcr/bias-ret.

</details>


### [292] [When Updating Stops Being Learning: Rethinking LLM Self-Evolution via learnable information gain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36535)
*Chenxu Wang,Chaozhuo Li,Xinze Shi,Songyang Liu,Kyrie You Wu,Ziluowen Luo,Shun Zhang,Chenxi Li,Litian Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出可学习信息增益的诊断框架，并据此设计ATRI方法，缓解LLM自我进化中的性能退化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自我进化常出现"提升—平台—衰退"的退化现象；现有方法仅在组件层面（Questioner或Solver）处理，忽略了自我进化是紧耦合系统。

Method: 提出基于可学习信息增益的整体框架，度量新一轮相对上一轮提供的新颖可参数化信息；理论上该增益等于两轮数据分布的KL散度加熵变；实践中用小型语言模型拟合上一轮，以负对数似然打分新数据来估计；据此提出ATRI，在轮内重加权样本、跨轮信息增益持续偏低时停止训练。

Result: 在常用数据集上的实验证明了所提方法的优越性。

Conclusion: 从系统整体视角以信息增益为诊断信号，可有效调控自我进化训练并缓解性能退化。

Abstract: Self-evolution lets large language models (LLMs) improve iteratively using their own generated data, but often suffers from self-evolution degeneration: performance improves, plateaus, then declines. Existing methods address this issue at the component level, targeting either the Questioner or the Solver, and overlook that self-evolution is a tightly coupled system. We propose a holistic framework based on learnable information gain, which measures how much novel, parameterizable information a round provides relative to the previous round. Theoretically, this gain equals the Kullback-Leibler divergence between the two rounds' data distributions plus their entropy change. Practically, it is estimated by fitting a small language model to the previous round and scoring new data via negative log-likelihood. Based on this diagnostic, we propose ATRI (Adaptive Training Regulation via Information-gain), which reweights samples within a round and halts training across rounds when information gain remains low. Experiments on popular datasets demonstrate the superiority of our proposal.

</details>


### [293] [DraftTrace: A Multi-View Analytics Environment for AI-Integrated Writing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36544)
*Divyansh Chandarana,Sandipan De,Vivek Gupta*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 DraftTrace 写作环境，同时捕捉写作成品、写作过程与AI助手交互三种视角，并在81名研究生的NLP课程中部署验证其分析价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI改变了学生完成写作任务的方式，仅凭最终成品已不足以理解写作过程，因此需要新的工具来记录和分析写作的全过程。

Method: DraftTrace 重建文档随时间的演变，将成品、过程与AI交互信号组织为提交级、纵向和班级级分析，并在研究生NLP课程（81名学生）中部署，与LLM自动生成的回答及复制粘贴式回答进行对比。

Result: 成品指标能区分文本表述差异，过程指标能区分文本输入方式差异；两者结合可刻画复制粘贴等情形。交互轨迹显示学生在写作不同阶段使用助手的方式不同：早期用于澄清问题，后期用于验证答案。初步的教师调研强调了多视角写作分析及其可解释性的重要性。

Conclusion: 多视角写作分析（结合成品、过程与AI交互）比单一成品指标更能刻画写作行为，且其可解释性对教学实践具有重要价值。

Abstract: Generative AI has changed how students produce writing assignments. The final artifact is no longer sufficient to understand the process through which it was produced. We introduce DraftTrace, a writing environment that jointly captures three complementary views of writing: the final product, the writing process and interactions with an integrated AI-assistant. DraftTrace reconstructs how a document develops over time and organizes these signals into submission, longitudinal, and class-level analytics for instructors. We deployed DraftTrace in a graduate NLP course with 81 students and compared their sessions with LLM-generated responses entered by automated tools and with copy-typed responses. While product measures distinguish differences in text formulation, process measures distinguish differences in how text is entered. Considering both views together helps characterize cases such as copy-typing. Interaction traces show that students use the assistant differently across stages of writing: to clarify the question at an early stage and to verify answers at a later stage. A preliminary instructor survey highlights the importance of multi-view writing analytics and their interpretability.

</details>


### [294] [Grounded Revision vs. Prior Injection: Probing Retrieval-Augmented Patent Claim Amendment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36550)
*Josepha Michiko Leo,Hyun-seok Min,Yehoon Jang,Irvan Zidny,Jin-Woo Chung,Sungchul Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文以专利权利要求修改为可判定"正确性"的场景，检验检索增强生成中检索究竟是提供依据还是仅注入模板。作者发布7,385件USPTO审查案例语料、七项探测组合与确定性五通道指标，在四个前沿LLM上进行9,600次预注册调用后，未发现经典先验注入行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成广泛用于专业写作，但在"正确"具有可定义含义的场景中，检索究竟是支撑修改还是仅注入模板，鲜有严格检验。专利权利要求修改提供了这一判别信号：审查员会明确指出被攻击的技术特征并引用现有技术，从而给出逐案的真值。

Method: 作者构建三项成果：(i) 包含7,385件USPTO审查案件的语料，含XML对齐的修改前/后权利要求、驳回理由与引用现有技术；(ii) 七项探测组合，在固定提示框架下比较随机检索与结构匹配检索两种策略；(iii) 确定性五通道指标（C1-C3、C5为主，C4为补充），无需LLM评判。在四个前沿LLM上开展9,600次预注册调用。

Result: 四个测试模型均未表现出可检测的经典先验注入行为；检索效应较小且在随机与结构检索之间方向不一致；该零结果在稠密（语义）检索器、检索深度k∈{1,3,5,10}以及释义敏感的依据性指标下均保持不变。修改局部性揭示了模板通道所遗漏的模型特异性差异。四格分类法（属探索性）中先验注入格为空。

Conclusion: 在具有明确正确性判定的专利修改任务中，检索并未表现为模板注入，模型行为不支持经典先验注入假设；检索效应微弱且不稳定。模型间差异体现在修改局部性上，需更细粒度的评估通道方能捕捉。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation is widely used in professional writing, yet whether retrieval grounds revision or merely injects templates is rarely tested where "correct" has a definable meaning. Patent claim amendment supplies that signal: the examiner names the attacked limitation and cites prior art, providing per-case ground truth. We release three artifacts: (i) a corpus of 7,385 USPTO prosecution cases with XML-aligned pre/post claims, rejection, and cited prior art; (ii) a seven-probe battery comparing random and structural-match retrieval as two policies under a fixed prompt scaffold; (iii) a deterministic five-channel metric (C1-C3 and C5 in main, C4 supplementary) requiring no LLM evaluation. Across 9,600 pre-registered calls on four frontier LLMs (Claude Sonnet 4, Claude Haiku 4.5, GPT-5.4, GPT-4o-mini), no tested model exhibits detectable classical prior-injection behavior; retrieval effects are small and direction-inconsistent between random and structural retrieval, and the null is unchanged under a dense (semantic) retriever, across retrieval depths k in {1,3,5,10}, and under a paraphrase-sensitive grounding metric. Revision locality reveals a model-specific difference that the template channel misses. The four-cell taxonomy, which we treat as exploratory, leaves the prior-injector cell unoccupied.

</details>


### [295] [SEED: Self-Speculative Decoding via Implicit Encoder-Decoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36590)
*Hankun Lin,Patrick Pynadath,Ruqi Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SEED自推测解码方法，将解码器模型重解释为隐式编码器-解码器，复用验证时已计算的深层表示进行高效草稿，实现最高2.7倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自推测解码面临草稿质量与成本的权衡：早退方法草稿便宜但质量差，多token预测质量好但每步需完整前向。需要兼顾高质量与低成本的方案。

Method: 将标准解码器transformer重解释为隐式编码器-解码器：前几层为编码器构建深层上下文表示，后几层为解码器生成token。编码与验证合并为单步，验证后缓存已验证前缀的上下文表示供草稿复用；草稿阶段轻量解码器在缓存表示和已有草稿条件下自回归生成多个token。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，4B规模模型上平均加速达2.7倍，优于早退和MTP风格自推测基线，比SOTA的EAGLE-3快28%，同时保持甚至提升标准自回归微调的生成质量。

Conclusion: SEED通过复用验证阶段的深层表示，在保证草稿质量的同时大幅降低草稿成本，实现了高效且高质量的自推测解码。

Abstract: Self-speculative decoding accelerates large language model (LLM) inference by drafting tokens from the target model itself, but faces a sharp tradeoff between the quality and cost of the draft. Early-exit methods produce drafts cheaply by terminating computation at intermediate layers, but forgo the deeper representations that later layers provide and thus suffer in draft quality. Multi-token prediction preserves draft quality by emitting from the model's final hidden states, but pays for a full forward pass to produce those states at every drafting step. We propose self-speculative encoder-decoder (SEED), a self-speculative method that obtains high-quality drafts cheaply by reusing the deep contextual representations already computed during verification. We reinterpret the standard decoder-only transformer as an implicit encoder-decoder: the first layers (encoder) build deep contextual representations, and the last few layers (decoder) emit tokens from them. Encoding and verification are merged into a single step: verification is performed by the full encoder-decoder, and the contextual representations of the verified prefix are cached for reuse during drafting. Drafting is therefore very fast: between verifications, the lightweight decoder drafts multiple tokens autoregressively, each conditioned on the cached representations and on preceding drafts. Experiments across multiple benchmarks show that SEED achieves up to 2.7$\times$ average speedup on 4B-scale models, outperforming both early-exit and MTP-style self-speculative baselines and running 28% faster than the state-of-the-art EAGLE-3, while preserving or even improving the generation quality of standard autoregressive fine-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/lhk2004/SEED.

</details>


### [296] [Generating Edit-Inducing Questions for AI Research Manuscripts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36617)
*Sebastian Joseph,Zichao Wang,Jennifer Healey,Alexa Siu,Junyi Jessy Li,Ani Nenkova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究LLM生成"引发修改"问题以改进论文草稿的能力，并在ICLR和NeurIPS的投稿-终稿配对数据集上，将GPT模型（有/无全文上下文）生成问题的有用性与人类审稿人进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM能否通过生成能促使论文修改的问题来帮助作者改进草稿，并检验模型（尤其推理模型）在长上下文下生成有用反馈的能力。

Method: 构建ICLR与NeurIPS投稿-终稿配对数据集；让GPT模型分别在有无完整论文上下文条件下生成问题；与人类审稿人问题比较其有用性，并分析问题引发修改的情况及编辑内容的范围。

Result: GPT生成更多"引发修改"的问题，其问题关联的修改更广泛、覆盖的编辑内容范围更广；但GPT问题中真正引发修改的比例远低于审稿人问题。

Conclusion: 自动生成的问题对作者有益；同时揭示了一个典型案例：恰当利用长上下文反而削弱推理模型产出有用输出的能力。

Abstract: We study the ability of LLMs to generate edit-inducing questions whose answer will improve a paper draft. On a dataset of paired submission and camera-ready papers from ICLR and NeurIPS, we compare the helpfulness of questions from GPT models with or without full paper context to that of human reviewers. GPT produces more edit-inducing questions and its questions are associated with more extensive edits and cover a broader range of edited content compared to questions from reviewers. However, a much smaller percentage of the GPT questions are edit-inducing. Our analyses confirm that automated questions can be beneficial to authors and highlight an example task where proper attending to long context deteriorates reasoning model ability to produce helpful output.

</details>


### [297] [Gödel Forest: Balancing Search Depth and Breadth for Data-Centric Recursive Self-Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36675)
*Ziqi Zhao,Fanqing Meng,Haocheng Lu,Lingxiao Du,Qiguang Chen,Mengkang Hu,Xiao-Ming Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Gödel Forest多智能体框架，将递归自我改进组织为共生搜索树集合，通过动态共演化记忆共享成败经验，在RSIBench-Data六领域平均超越单智能体基线10.70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据为中心的RSI需通过训练智能体生成的数据来更新模型参数，但验证数据策略代价高昂，导致两难：单智能体易陷入狭窄方向、缺乏探索广度；朴素的并行搜索或重度轨迹共享又牺牲长程搜索深度。

Method: Gödel Forest将RSI组织为多棵共演化搜索树的集成。每个智能体自主生长一棵持久树，依据模型反馈对数据策略进行深化、分支或剪枝以保深度；并行树探索数据空间不同区域以扩广度。森林间由动态共演化记忆连接，智能体将成功与失败蒸馏为紧凑的过程性经验并锚定于全局排行榜，实现死路即时预警、突破快速播种新分支。

Result: 在RSIBench-Data的六个多样领域上，Gödel Forest平均超越单智能体基线10.70%，并在五项任务上减少墙钟时间。消融实验表明，共演化共享记忆相比独立并行搜索带来+7.00%增益。

Conclusion: 集体蒸馏（共演化共享记忆）是可扩展自我改进的关键，森林生态在深度与广度间取得平衡，验证了多智能体协同搜索的有效性。

Abstract: Recursive self-improvement (RSI) aims to achieve compounding gains by having models improve themselves. While most existing RSI systems optimize external agent harnesses or prompts around a frozen base model, data-centric RSI directly updates the model's own parameters by training on agent-generated data. However, because validating data strategies requires expensive model training, existing methods face a fundamental dilemma: a single agent gets trapped in narrow directions and lacks exploration breadth, while naive parallel search or heavy trace sharing sacrifices long-horizon search depth. To address this challenge, we introduce G"odel Forest, a multi-agent framework that organizes recursive self-improvement as an ensemble of co-evolving search trees. In G"odel Forest, each agent autonomously grows a persistent tree, deepening, branching, or pruning data strategies based on model feedback to secure depth, while parallel trees explore distinct regions of the data space to expand breadth. Crucially, rather than leaving trees isolated or flooding them with heavy execution logs, a dynamically co-evolving memory connects the forest: agents continuously distill their successes and failures into compact procedural lessons anchored to a global leaderboard. Through this forest ecosystem, a dead-end in one tree instantly warns the whole forest against unpromising paths, while an empirical breakthrough quickly seeds new exploration branches in neighboring trees. Evaluated on RSIBench-Data across six diverse domains, G"odel Forest outperforms the single-agent baseline by an average of 10.70% while reducing wall-clock time on five tasks. Ablations confirm that co-evolving shared memory yields a +7.00% gain over independent parallel search, demonstrating that collective distillation is key to scalable self-improvement. The code is available at https://github.com/evolvent-ai/Godel-Forest.

</details>


### [298] [ProgressCompass: Embodied Progress Reward Models Are Lost Without the Right Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36684)
*Jianshu Zhang,Keliang Wu,Chengxuan Qian,Xiyuan Yang,Ce Zhang,Ariel Tian,Anbang Liu,Haoran Lu,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 ContextProgress-Bench 基准，揭示具身进度奖励模型（PRM）在"上下文依赖的进度估计"上严重失效，但补足正确上下文后误差可降 77-82%；并据此提出 ProgressCompass 自主闭环，使冻结的 PRM 误差降 63%、排序一致率升 76%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体任务越来越长，仅靠最终成败无法反映中间步骤质量，因此需要进度奖励模型（PRM）提供稠密奖励、验证与监控。但长任务中当前帧往往无法判断进度，因为进度取决于此前发生过什么，作者称之为"上下文依赖的进度估计"。现有基准多聚焦于可从当前观测直接读出进度的短任务，PRM 在需要上下文时能否估计进度仍缺乏探索。

Method: 构建 ContextProgress-Bench，包含 24 个操作任务、共 120 个 episode，覆盖三种设定：（i）State Recall，进度所需信息出现在更早时刻而不在当前帧；（ii）Sequence Tracking，步骤顺序固定，需知道哪些已完成、下一步是什么；（iii）Recurrence Disambiguation，外观相似的帧对应差异很大的进度。随后进行配对诊断：同一 PRM 在两次运行中保持相同输入格式，其中一次在指令中整合正确上下文。

Result: 即便读取完整历史的 PRM 也会在进度估计中迷失；而获得正确上下文后，同样的五个模型将进度误差降低 77-82%。基于此提出 ProgressCompass——一个自主智能体闭环，用于重新定向已有 PRM，并借助当前通用 VLM 提供 PRM 所需上下文。套用该闭环后，同一冻结 PRM 的进度误差降低 63%，排序一致率提升 76%。

Conclusion: 具身 PRM 并非本身不具备进度估计能力，而是缺少正确上下文而"迷失"。通过 ProgressCompass 为其补足上下文，PRM 能在更长、更复杂的任务上显著更准确地估计进度。

Abstract: Embodied agents now take on ever longer tasks. For long tasks, knowing only whether a task finally succeeds or fails says little; the steps along the way matter. Progress Reward Models (PRMs) score how far a task has come at every step, and serve as dense rewards, verifiers and monitors. Yet in long tasks the current frame alone often cannot tell how far the task has come, because progress depends on what happened before. We call this problem context-dependent progress estimation. Existing benchmarks on progress estimation mostly focus on short tasks whose progress can be read from the current observation, and whether PRMs can estimate progress when context is needed remains underexplored. We therefore build ContextProgress-Bench, with 24 manipulation tasks for 120 episodes. The benchmark covers three settings: (i) State Recall, where information needed for progress appeared earlier but is not in the current frame; (ii) Sequence Tracking, where steps follow a fixed order, so progress requires knowing which steps are done and which comes next; and (iii) Recurrence Disambiguation, where look-alike frames sit at very different progress. We then run a paired diagnosis: each PRM keeps the same input format in both runs, and in one run its instruction integrates the right context. Even PRMs that read the entire history get lost in estimating progress, yet with the right context the same five models cut their progress error by 77-82%. Embodied PRMs are thus not incapable of progress estimation, but lost without the right context. We therefore propose ProgressCompass, an autonomous agentic loop that reorients an existing PRM and uses current general-purpose VLMs to supply the context the PRM needs. Wrapped in the loop, the same frozen PRM cuts its progress error by 63% and raises its rank agreement by 76%. With such a compass, PRMs estimate progress far better on longer, more complex tasks.

</details>


### [299] [Video2Skill: From Streaming Experience to Reusable Embodied Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36691)
*Jianshu Zhang,Ce Zhang,Xiyuan Yang,Chenwei Xu,Haoran Lu,Yijiang Li,Yaqi Xie,Katia P. Sycara,Han Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出流式具身技能发现（SESD）任务与Video2Skill基准，系统评测19个开源VLM将连续操作事件组织为可复用技能库的能力，发现技能分组、库更新与新建技能是核心瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作行为虽多样，但共享少量可复用技能，规划这些技能有助于具身智能体泛化到新任务；而智能体只能规划它已知的技能。从观察经验中恢复技能（规划的逆过程）能随时间积累知识并为训练未来智能体提供技能数据。VLM虽能描述单个操作事件，但能否把一串事件组织成可复用技能尚不清楚，因此需要系统性度量这一能力。

Method: 将问题形式化为流式具身技能发现（SESD）：模型按顺序观看视频并维护一个持续更新的技能库，该库影响其后续决策。提出Video2Skill基准，覆盖机器人桌面操作与人类厨房活动，测试三项能力：(i) 在时间上定位操作事件，(ii) 将同一变换的事件分组，(iii) 判断何时复用已有技能或创建新技能。评测19个开源VLM，并采用监督微调及反事实库状态重平衡（CLaRe）方法。

Result: 在19个开源VLM上，许多模型的事件分组接近随机水平，扩大规模并不稳定地带来提升。错误依赖感知与库更新的耦合方式：联合模型会把不同变换合并为一个技能，而从文本描述更新库的模型会重复复制同类技能。监督微调（含CLaRe）能改善分组，但暴露出更深层瓶颈：训练后的模型能巩固熟悉技能却很少扩展库，其库规模停滞在参考规模的一半以下，训练中未见过的变换虽能被时间定位却几乎从不被赋予新技能。

Conclusion: 识别“现有技能何时不足”成为SESD的核心挑战；未来工作需聚焦让模型在熟悉技能之外主动扩展技能库。

Abstract: Manipulation behaviors vary widely across objects and scenes, but they share a small set of reusable skills, and planning with these skills helps embodied agents generalize to new tasks. Yet an agent can only plan with skills it knows. Recovering skills from observed experience, the inverse of planning, builds this knowledge over time and yields skill data for training future agents. Vision-Language Models (VLMs) describe individual manipulation events well, but can they organize a stream of events into reusable skills? We formulate this problem as Streaming Embodied Skill Discovery (SESD): a model watches videos in sequence and maintains a persistent skill library that shapes its later decisions. To systematically measure this ability, we introduce Video2Skill, a benchmark that covers robot tabletop manipulation and human kitchen activity and tests three core capabilities: (i) locating manipulation events in time, (ii) grouping events of the same transformation, and (iii) deciding when to reuse an existing skill or create a new one. Across 19 open-source VLMs, many models group events at near-chance level, and scale does not consistently help. Their errors depend on how perception and library updates are coupled: joint models merge distinct transformations into one skill, while models that update the library from text descriptions duplicate recurring ones. Supervised fine-tuning, including our counterfactual library-state rebalancing (CLaRe), improves grouping but exposes a deeper bottleneck: trained models consolidate familiar skills yet rarely expand the library. Their libraries stall below half the reference size, and transformations unseen in training are located in time but almost never given a new skill. Recognizing when existing skills are insufficient thus emerges as the central challenge.

</details>


### [300] [Lost in Conversation or Lost in Translation? Diagnosing Multi-Turn Degradation in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36700)
*Pranav Handa,Ariful Azad*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文系统研究了多轮对话场景下RAG与GraphRAG系统的评测错配问题：将多跳QA问题转化为欠指定对话，在150万次模拟对话中评测10个LLM助手与8种检索系统，发现多轮交互导致性能相对下降最高21%、不可靠性上升47%，并识别出两类失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户在真实使用LLM时往往从简单问题出发、通过多轮追问逐步构建多跳问题，但RAG与GraphRAG几乎只在单轮、完整表述的查询上进行评测，这种评测与真实使用场景之间存在错配。作者旨在系统性地考察这一错配带来的后果。

Method: 基于已有关于多轮LLM评测的工作，将多跳问答基准中的问题转化为欠指定对话，构建大规模模拟实验：评测10个LLM助手与8种检索系统，跨越150万次模拟对话，覆盖RAG与GraphRAG等主流方法。

Result: 多轮交互造成普遍的性能退化，相对性能下降最高达21%，不可靠性上升47%，使RAG系统同时变得既更不准确也更不可靠。作者进一步识别出两类失败模式：一是"lost in translation"（对话改写扭曲检索查询），二是"lost in conversation"（检索成功但LLM无法跨轮综合分散的证据）。

Conclusion: 现有RAG/GraphRAG的评测范式高估了其在真实多轮交互中的表现，多轮对话会显著削弱准确性与可靠性。研究提示需要将多轮欠指定对话纳入检索增强系统的标准评测，并针对查询改写失真与跨轮证据综合两类失败模式改进系统设计。

Abstract: When conversing with large language models (LLMs), users often begin with a simple question and build towards a multi-hop question through follow-up turns. Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and its graph-based variant (GraphRAG) have become the dominant approaches for grounding LLM responses in external evidence, yet both are evaluated almost exclusively on single-turn, fully specified queries. We systematically investigate this evaluation mismatch through a large-scale simulation study. Building on prior work on multi-turn LLM evaluation, we transform questions from multi-hop question answering (QA) benchmarks into underspecified conversations and evaluate ten LLM assistants with eight retrieval systems across 1.5 million simulated conversations. Our findings reveal that multi-turn interaction causes widespread performance degradation, incurring relative performance drops of up to 21% and increasing unreliability by 47%, making RAG systems simultaneously less accurate and less reliable. We identify two distinct failure modes behind this degradation. Systems are either lost in translation, where conversational rephrasing distorts the retrieval query, or lost in conversation, where retrieval succeeds but the LLM fails to synthesize evidence distributed across turns.

</details>


### [301] [LAURA: Knowledge Distillation for Interpretable Ambiguous Clause Identification in Legal Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36707)
*Amrita Singh,Aditya Joshi,Jiaojiao Jiang,Hye-young Paik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LAURA 后训练框架，用知识蒸馏与 IRAC-Unlearning 提示将教师 LLM 能力迁移到 ≤1B 开源学生模型，实现可解释的合同歧义条款识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律合同中的歧义会给企业带来财务与法律风险，但并非所有歧义都会引发纠纷：部分歧义可灵活解释而不产生争议，另一些则会导致重大法律冲突。因此仅做歧义识别并不足够，还需要可解释的理由分析。

Method: LAURA 是一个可解释性歧义条款识别的后训练框架。它通过知识蒸馏结合 IRAC-Unlearning 提示技术，将教师 LLM 的知识迁移到参数量不超过 1B 的开源学生模型，并用分类损失与理由生成损失的联合目标进行训练。框架旨在支持法律与非法律相关方判断哪些歧义需要进一步关注。

Result: 在 7 个基线与 7 个开源权重模型上的大量实验表明，采用 Flan-T5（250M）的 LAURA 在所有可解释基线中达到最先进的可解释性，同时识别性能可与表现最好的不透明基线相媲美。

Conclusion: LAURA 证明小型开源模型也可在合同歧义识别中同时兼顾可解释性与识别精度，为法律及非法律相关方提供决策支持。

Abstract: Legal contracts contain ambiguities that expose enterprises to financial and legal risks. Some ambiguities allow flexible interpretation without triggering disputes, while others lead to significant legal conflicts. This makes identification alone insufficient, and interpretable rationale analysis essential. We propose LAURA, a post-training framework for interpretable ambiguous clause identification. LAURA leverages knowledge distillation with an IRAC-Unlearning prompting technique to transfer knowledge from a teacher LLM to an open-weight student model (<=1B parameters), which is then trained using a joint objective combining classification and rationale generation losses. The framework supports both legal and non-legal stakeholders in making informed decisions about which ambiguities require further attention. Extensive experiments across 7 baselines and 7 open-weight models demonstrate that LAURA with Flan-T5 (250M) delivers state-of-the-art interpretability over all interpretable baselines while matching the identification performance of the best-performing opaque baseline.

</details>


### [302] [ATTUNER: Recomputation-Free KV Cache Reuse via Query-Side Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36722)
*Xinghao Chen,Junnan Dong,Cai Ke,Chak Tou Leong,Haocheng Sun,Keyu Chen,Siyu An,Ruizhi Qiao,Xing Sun,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文定位了位置无关缓存(PIC)质量损失的根源在注意力而非KV表征，提出Attuner——通过查询侧低秩适配器蒸馏全上下文分布，恢复质量并实现最高3.73倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体反复将可复用内容（技能、文档、记忆）加载进上下文并重复编码，浪费算力。PIC通过独立编码并在任意位置复用KV状态来缓解，但相比全上下文预填充存在质量损失。现有修复方法依赖恢复全局位置ID或重算部分token，并未真正定位损失来源。作者希望查明损失根源并提出更高效的修复方案。

Method: 作者首先消融分析，发现位置不匹配影响很小，独立缓存的artifacts表征依然可靠，性能退化只在模型需在多个artifacts间选择时出现；且将PIC注意力分数替换为全预填充分数即可在KV不变的情况下恢复性能，说明失败源于注意力而非KV重算。基于此提出Attuner：在查询投影中插入低秩适配器，通过蒸馏全预填充分布训练，仅训练不到0.05%参数，推理时无需缓存重算或全上下文参考。

Result: 在Qwen3-4B和Qwen3-8B上，覆盖技能、文档、记忆、代码等七个基准，Attuner在域内和域外设置均大幅超越先前PIC基线，达到与全上下文预填充相当的质量，同时提供最高3.73倍加速。

Conclusion: PIC质量损失主要源于注意力机制的错位而非KV表征劣化，可通过查询侧轻量自适应修复。Attuner以极少参数和低成本实现接近全上下文的质量与显著加速，为LLM智能体的高效上下文复用提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents repeatedly load reusable content, such as skills, documents, and memory entries, into the current context. Re-encoding this content for every request wastes computation. Position-independent caching (PIC) alleviates this by encoding each artifact independently and reusing its key-value (KV) states at arbitrary positions, but it incurs a quality loss relative to full-context prefill. Existing methods repair this loss by restoring global position IDs or recomputing selected tokens. In this work, we isolate the source of the loss, finding that the positional mismatch has minor effect, and independently cached artifacts retain faithful representations: reading a provided artifact stays largely accurate, and performance degrades only when the model must select among multiple artifacts. Moreover, replacing PIC's attention scores with full-prefill scores recovers performance with the cached KV unchanged, localizing the failure to the attention rather than KV recomputation. Motivated by this, we propose \textsc{Attuner}, a query-side adaptation method that learns to read a frozen artifact cache. \textsc{Attuner} inserts low-rank adapters into the query projections and is trained by distilling full-prefill distribution into the student. It trains fewer than 0.05\% of the model parameters and, at inference, requires neither cache recomputation nor a full-context reference. On Qwen3-4B and Qwen3-8B across seven benchmarks covering skills, documents, memory, and code, \textsc{Attuner} substantially outperforms prior PIC baselines in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings, matches full-context prefill quality while providing up to $3.73\times$ speedup.

</details>


### [303] [Distilling What Matters: Confidence-Aware Selective Distillation for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36734)
*Ayan Sengupta,Vaibhav Seth,Tanmoy Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 CaRE-KD，一种置信度门控的知识蒸馏框架，用不确定性自适应优化替代静态目标，在八个师生对与十一个基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统知识蒸馏假设教师模型是可靠的标准答案来源，但在大语言模型中该假设常不成立：教师预测具有高熵与幻觉，导致标准蒸馏破坏学生模型原本良好校准的先验。

Method: CaRE-KD 包含两个组件：(1) token 级损失 CaRE-Divergence，依据师生置信度自适应地在 Forward KL 与 Reverse KL 之间切换；(2) batch 级认知拒绝机制 Revival，当教师比学生更不确定时抑制更新。作者还提供梯度级分析，说明该双粒度设计诱导出静态散度无法复现的条件校准机制。

Result: 在八个师生对与十一个基准（涵盖指令跟随、对话对齐、代码生成、数学推理）上，CaRE-KD 稳定优于 Skewed-KL、α–β 散度等强基线：指令跟随平均 ROUGE-L 提升至 +3.2，MBPP 的 pass@1 提升 +2.1，GSM8k 准确率提升 +1.7，CollegeMath 准确率提升 +1.8；LLM-as-a-judge 事实性提升最高 +2.5。Revival 还可作为与损失无关的即插即用模块增强现有蒸馏目标。

Conclusion: 通过置信度门控与双粒度自适应优化，CaRE-KD 有效缓解了教师不可靠带来的负面影响，并提供了可泛化的拒绝机制以系统性强化既有蒸馏方法。

Abstract: Knowledge Distillation (KD) trains a smaller-capacity student model to imitate a larger-capacity teacher model by matching output distributions, implicitly assuming the teacher to be a reliable oracle. In large language models (LLMs), this assumption often fails: teacher predictions can exhibit high entropy and hallucinations, causing standard KD to degrade well-calibrated student priors. We propose CaRE-KD, a confidence-gated distillation framework that replaces static objectives with uncertainty-adaptive optimization. CaRE-KD has two components: a token-level loss (CaRE-Divergence) that adaptively switches between Forward and Reverse KL divergence based on teacher--student confidence, and a batch-level epistemic rejection mechanism (Revival) that suppresses updates when the teacher is more uncertain than the student. We provide a gradient-level analysis showing how this dual-granularity design induces a conditional calibration mechanism that prior static divergences cannot reproduce. Empirically, across eight teacher--student pairs and eleven benchmarks spanning instruction following, chat alignment, code generation, and mathematical reasoning, CaRE-KD delivers consistent gains over strong baselines (Skewed-KL, $α$--$β$ divergence). Highlights include up to $+3.2$ average ROUGE-L on instruction-following tasks, $+2.1$ pass@1 on MBPP, $+1.7$ accuracy on GSM8k, and $+1.8$ accuracy on CollegeMath over the strongest baseline, with consistent gains in LLM-as-a-judge factuality (up to $+2.5$ per task over Skewed-RKL). Revival further acts as a principled, loss-agnostic plug-in that systematically strengthens existing distillation objectives by filtering epistemically unreliable teacher supervision.

</details>


### [304] [VAA-CSEC: Vote-guided Advantage Allocation for Chinese Semantic Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36804)
*Yitong Han,Nankai Lin,Juan Luo,Hongyan Wu,Lianxi Wang,Shengyi Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出VAA-CSEC多阶段框架，通过CoT蒸馏、SFT、RL和自一致性解码解决中文语义纠错过纠与CoT收益难以传递的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中文语义纠错（CSEC）针对比拼写和语法错误更隐晦、复杂的语义错误，但研究相对不足。现有基于LLM的方法面临两大障碍：过纠（over-correction），以及CoT推理与自一致性解码之间交互不清，导致CoT带来的收益无法可靠地转化为最终纠错结果。

Method: 提出VAA-CSEC（Vote-guided Advantage Allocation for CSEC）多阶段框架，结合CoT蒸馏、监督微调（SFT）、强化学习（RL）与自一致性解码。在RL阶段设计任务特定奖励函数，直接对齐CSEC的最小编辑原则；并提出Group-Level Relative Policy Optimization（GLPO），根据单个rollout奖励与投票聚合组奖励之间的差距重新分配GRPO优势，使RL训练目标与推理时的自一致性目标对齐。

Result: 在CSED-C和NaSGEC-Exam数据集上，VAA-CSEC在CSED-C上以47.72%的F0.5超越所有基于LLM的基线，在所有方法中取得最高的42.15%召回率，并在NaSGEC-Exam上以41.55%的F0.5刷新最高水平。

Conclusion: VAA-CSEC通过将RL优势分配与自一致性投票机制对齐，有效缓解过纠问题并可靠利用CoT推理收益，在多个中文语义纠错基准上取得领先性能。

Abstract: Chinese Semantic Error Correction (CSEC) targets semantic errors in Chinese text, which are typically more subtle and complex than spelling and grammatical errors but remain relatively underexplored. Existing LLM-based approaches face two recurring obstacles in this task: over-correction, and unclear interaction between Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning and self-consistency decoding, such that the benefits brought by CoT cannot be reliably transferred to final corrections. We propose Vote-guided Advantage Allocation for CSEC (VAA-CSEC), a multi-stage framework that combines CoT distillation, Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Reinforcement Learning (RL) and self-consistency decoding. During RL, we design a task-specific reward function that directly aligned with the minimal-editing principle of CSEC. We further introduce Group-Level Relative Policy Optimization (GLPO), which reallocates GRPO advantages according to the margin between individual rollout rewards and the vote-aggregated group reward, aligning the RL training objective with the self-consistency objective used at inference time. Experiments on CSED-C and NaSGEC-Exam show that VAA-CSEC outperforms all LLM-based baselines on CSED-C with an F0.5 of 47.72%, achieves the highest recall of 42.15% among all methods, and establishes a new state of the art of 41.55% F0.5 on NaSGEC-Exam.

</details>


### [305] [Rethinking Multimodal Fake News Detection in the Generative AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36850)
*Wenbin Shen,Guoxuan Qin,Guangxu Yao,Baodong Wang,Yuanbo Rui,Zhichao Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对生成式内容融入新闻传播的新形势，构建了面向生成内容场景的多模态假新闻数据集Weibo26，并提出Generativity-Aware Hierarchical Reasoning (GAHR)框架，将"生成性"信息纳入真实性推理，在多个基准上实现竞争力性能并有效识别生成内容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式内容日益进入新闻生产与传播，使假新闻从人工捏造或简单篡改转变为"原生内容与生成内容共同参与"的复杂形态。现有多模态假新闻检测研究多聚焦真实性评估，很少刻画"生成性差异"如何影响证据可靠性；而AIGC检测只判断内容是否由生成模型产生或修改，本身并不能确定新闻事件是否真实。两类任务在数据与评估上彼此割裂，亟需弥合。

Method: 1) 构建Weibo26——面向生成内容场景的多模态假新闻检测数据集；2) 提出Generativity-Aware Hierarchical Reasoning (GAHR)框架，将全局判断与局部校正相结合，使生成性信息参与新闻真实性推理。

Result: 在多个现有多模态假新闻检测基准以及Weibo26数据集上的实验表明，GAHR在取得有竞争力的真实性检测性能的同时，能够有效识别生成式内容。

Conclusion: 通过数据集与框架的双重贡献，论文将AIGC检测与假新闻真实性检测在数据与评估层面衔接起来，验证了在生成内容场景下融合"生成性"信息进行层次化推理的可行性与有效性。

Abstract: Generative content is increasingly entering the production and dissemination of news, transforming fake news from manually fabricated or simply manipulated material into complex forms in which native and generated content jointly participate. Existing multimodal fake news detection research primarily focuses on veracity assessment and rarely characterizes how generativity differences affect the reliability of evidence. In contrast, AIGC detection primarily determines whether content is generated or modified by generative models, but it does not by itself establish whether the underlying news event is true. To bridge the separation between these tasks in data and evaluation, we construct Weibo26, a multimodal fake news detection dataset for generative-content scenarios. On this basis, we propose the Generativity-Aware Hierarchical Reasoning (GAHR) framework, which combines global judgment with local correction so that generativity information participates in news-veracity reasoning. Experiments on multiple existing fake news detection benchmarks and Weibo26 show that GAHR achieves competitive veracity-detection performance while effectively identifying generative content.

</details>


### [306] [Momentum-Coupled Rubric Adaptation for Detailed Image Captioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36893)
*Zhenwen Ji,Lei Jin,Shanyong Wang,Jiaming Lu,Chengqiang Lu,Yi Wu,Yao Hu,Lizhen Cui,Yanyu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出MoCo Rubric两阶段框架，用共享参数的单一视觉语言模型统一承担字幕生成、评分标准构建与评判三个角色，实现协同对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 详细图像字幕需要同时满足事实准确、信息覆盖与表达清晰等要求。基于评分标准的强化学习可将这些要求分解为显式标准并给出结构化反馈，但现有方法通常用独立模型分别完成字幕生成、标准构建与评判，容易导致角色间理解不一致；部分动态标准方法虽交替更新生成策略与标准生成器，却让评判器固定，分阶段优化仍使标准构建与评判同策略优化脱节。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段做角色条件、共享参数的多任务监督微调，使同一个视觉语言模型可充当字幕策略、标准生成器和标准评判器；第二阶段中生成器基于当前策略采样的字幕、参考字幕与图像证据在线构建评分标准，评判器给出基于标准的奖励，仅策略获得GRPO更新。由于策略更新会改变被评估的候选，采用策略参数的指数移动平均来更新一个由生成器和评判器共享的动量模型，让两个标准角色渐进跟随策略变化，避免直接同步带来的参数突变。

Result: 在五个字幕基准上，MoCo Rubric取得72.83%的平均两两胜率、盲排中的最佳平均排名，以及基于字幕问答中的最高平均分。

Conclusion: 通过共享参数与动量式协同更新，将字幕生成、标准构建与评判统一在一个模型中协调演化，可在无需对评判角色单独做强化学习的情况下提升字幕质量。

Abstract: Detailed image captioning requires accurate and comprehensive descriptions of fine-grained visual content, yet caption quality spans factual accuracy, information coverage, and clarity. Compared with conventional methods that rely mainly on high-quality supervision or holistic rewards, rubric-based reinforcement learning decomposes these requirements into explicit criteria and provides targeted, structured feedback. However, existing methods often use separate models for caption generation, rubric construction, and judging, which may lead to inconsistent interpretations across roles. Some dynamic rubric methods alternate updates between the caption policy and rubric generator while keeping the judge fixed, but staged optimization may still leave rubric construction and judging out of step with policy optimization. We propose MoCo Rubric, a two-stage framework that coordinates these roles. First, role-conditioned, shared-parameter multi-task supervised fine-tuning equips a single vision--language model to serve as the Caption Policy, Rubric Generator, and Rubric Judge. Then, the Generator constructs rubrics online from captions sampled by the current Policy, reference captions, and image evidence. The Judge provides rubric-based rewards, and only the Policy receives GRPO updates. As Policy updates change the candidates being evaluated, we use an exponential moving average of the Policy parameters to update one momentum model shared by the Generator and Judge. This gradual transfer lets both rubric roles track Policy updates without separate RL optimization while smoothing parameter changes that could disrupt their rubric capabilities under direct synchronization. Across five captioning benchmarks, MoCo Rubric achieves an average pairwise win rate of 72.83\%, the best mean rank in blind ranking, and the highest average score in caption-based question answering.

</details>


### [307] [RAEGNet: Relation-Aware Evidence Graph Network for Harm-Aware Multimodal Fake News Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36902)
*Wenbin Shen,Guoxuan Qin,Guangxu Yao,Baodong Wang,Yuanbo Rui,Zhongjie Ba,Zhichao Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对多模态假新闻检测中实体级检索引入噪声、忽视不同实例危害差异的问题，提出事件级证据检索框架ELERF与关系感知证据图网络RAEGNet，并在多个数据集上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态假新闻检测方法多依赖实体级检索引入外部信息，容易引入与事件无关的噪声；同时这些方法主要关注整体性能提升，未考虑不同假新闻实例在危害程度上的差异。

Method: 设计事件级证据检索框架ELERF，基于新闻的完整事件语义检索外部证据；提出关系感知证据图网络RAEGNet，构建包含新闻-证据立场关系与证据-证据交互关系的有向图，并引入条件危害分支联合建模真实性与潜在危害。

Result: 在Weibo-21、Fakeddit以及自建的SSS数据集上，RAEGNet在所有评估指标上均优于多个基线方法。

Conclusion: 事件级证据检索与关系感知图建模可有效缓解噪声问题，并通过联合建模真实性与危害性提升多模态假新闻检测性能。

Abstract: Existing multimodal fake news detection methods often introduce external information to assist detection. However, most of them rely on entity-level retrieval and are therefore prone to introducing event-irrelevant noise. Meanwhile, existing methods mainly focus on improving overall performance and do not account for differences in the degree of harm posed by different instances of fake news. To address these limitations, we design an Event-Level Evidence Retrieval Framework (ELERF) and propose a Relation-Aware Evidence Graph Network (RAEGNet). ELERF retrieves external evidence based on the complete event semantics of a news item. RAEGNet constructs a directed graph that incorporates news-evidence stance relations and evidence-evidence interaction relations, and introduces a conditional-harm branch to jointly model authenticity and potential harm. Experimental results demonstrate that RAEGNet outperforms multiple baseline methods across all evaluated metrics on Weibo-21, Fakeddit, and our self-constructed SSS dataset.

</details>


### [308] [MultiTalk: Scaling Full-Duplex Speech Models to Long, Multi-Party, Bilingual Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36903)
*Ke Wang,Houxing Ren,Zimu Lu,Yunqiao Yang,Zhuofan Zong,Mingjie Zhan,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文沿"长时程"与"多方"两个维度扩展 Moshi 范式，发布 57.6k 小时中英全双工多方对话合成数据与基于真实录音的 MultiTalkBench，并训练出在长时程中英多方对话中显著优于 Moshi、MiniCPM-o-4.5、Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct 等开源基线的双语模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端全双工语音模型虽使开放源码机器对话更接近类人交互，但在长上下文鲁棒性与多方交互两方面仍受限。会议、小组课堂、社交机器人接待等真实场景要求单一模型在长时间内跟踪、理解上下文并回应多位说话者。当前进展受阻于数据与评测：开放多方语音语料规模小，且并非为编解码帧级全双工建模设计；现有长音频基准侧重被动聆听，语音到语音基准多为短时且双人对话。

Method: 在英文与中文上，沿长时程与多方两个轴联合扩展 Moshi 范式：1）发布 57.6k 小时合成训练数据 MultiTalkPT 与 MultiTalkFT，用于长时、多方、中英全双工对话，具备可控的时长、参与人数、轮流发言、重叠、反馈语、打断、受话人切换与长距离指代；2）构建 MultiTalkBench，基于真实人类录音，用于评测长时、多方、双语全双工对话；3）训练一个双语 Moshi 风格模型。

Result: MultiTalkBench 中对话平均时长 32.6 分钟，包含长距离实体跟踪、话题连贯性与受话人选择等探测任务。所训练的双语 Moshi 风格模型能在长时间内维持连贯的中英多方对话，并在 MultiTalkBench 上显著优于 Moshi、MiniCPM-o-4.5 与 Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct 等开源基线。

Conclusion: 通过联合发布大规模合成多方全双工训练数据、基于真实录音的长时双语评测基准及相应双语模型，该工作将全双工语音对话从短时双人扩展到长时程多方场景，证明数据与评测的同步推进可有效提升模型在真实多方交互场景中的表现。

Abstract: End-to-end full-duplex speech models have brought open-source machine conversation closer to human-like interaction, yet existing systems remain limited in two intertwined dimensions: long-context robustness and multi-party interaction. Real-world scenarios such as meetings, group lessons, and social-robot reception require a single model to track, contextualize, and respond to multiple speakers over extended durations. Progress is constrained by both data and evaluation: open multi-party speech corpora remain small and are not designed for codec-frame-level full-duplex modeling, while existing long-audio benchmarks focus on passive listening and speech-to-speech benchmarks are mostly short and dyadic. We extend the Moshi paradigm jointly along the long-horizon and multi-party axes in English and Chinese. First, we release 57.6k hours of synthetic training data ($\href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/MultiTalk/MultiTalkPT}{MultiTalkPT}$ and $\href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/MultiTalk/MultiTalkFT}{MultiTalkFT}$) for long-form, multi-party, English-Chinese full-duplex dialogue, with controllable length, participant count, turn-taking, overlap, backchannels, interruptions, addressee shifts, and long-range coreference. Second, we introduce $\href{https://huggingface.co/datasets/MultiTalk/MultiTalkBench}{MultiTalkBench}$, built from real human recordings, for evaluating long-form, multi-party, bilingual full-duplex dialogue. Conversations average 32.6 minutes and include probes for long-range entity tracking, topic coherence, and addressee selection. Third, we train a bilingual Moshi-style model that sustains coherent multi-party English-Chinese conversations over extended durations and substantially outperforms open-source baselines including Moshi, MiniCPM-o-4.5, and Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct on MultiTalkBench.

</details>


### [309] [BaLEEN: Biasing with Latent Encoded Entities for Context-Aware ASR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36913)
*Chihiro Taguchi,Yotaro Kubo,Rujikorn Charakorn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BaLEEN，一种基于超网络的轻量级上下文自适应框架，通过冻结的预训练语言模型编码可变长关键词，用Perceiver瓶颈压缩成固定长度隐向量，并注入ASR编码器的中间表示，实现即插即用、推理零额外开销的动态偏置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASR在转录领域特定实体和罕见专有名词时仍存在明显困难；现有上下文自适应方法通常需要微调底层ASR模型，成本高且不易迁移。

Method: BaLEEN使用预训练语言模型编码可变长度的上下文关键词，通过Perceiver瓶颈将其压缩为固定序列的隐向量，生成上下文相关的偏置向量并直接注入ASR模型的中间编码器表示；训练时语言模型与主干ASR模型均保持冻结，作为即插即用适配器，若预先计算上下文偏置，推理时无额外计算开销。

Result: 在基于CTC的ASR模型上，使用含标注命名实体的维基百科语料与合成语音进行评估：相比无偏置基线，测试集关键词漏识率降低8.7%，同时整体词错误率改善21%、字符错误率改善28%。

Conclusion: BaLEEN证明了无需微调主干ASR即可高效注入动态上下文信息的可行性，在提升专有名词识别的同时还能降低整体识别错误率，具备轻量、即插即用的实用价值。

Abstract: Transcribing domain-specific entities and rare proper nouns remains a major challenge in automatic speech recognition (ASR). In this paper, we propose BaLEEN (Biasing with Latent Encoded Entities), a lightweight, hypernetwork-based framework for dynamic contextual adaptation without fine-tuning the underlying ASR model. BaLEEN encodes variable-length contextual keywords using a pretrained language model, compresses them into a fixed sequence of latent vectors via a Perceiver bottleneck, and injects context-dependent bias vectors directly into the intermediate encoder representations of the ASR model. Because both the language model and the backbone ASR model remain entirely frozen during training, BaLEEN operates as a plug-and-play adapter that incurs zero computational overhead at inference time when context biases are precomputed. We evaluate our method on a CTC-based ASR model using a Wikipedia-derived corpus with annotated named entities and synthetic speech. Experimental results demonstrate that BaLEEN reduces keyword miss rate by 8.7% on the test set relative to the unbiased baseline while simultaneously improving overall word error rate by 21% and character error rate by 28%.

</details>


### [310] [Can Language Models Learn to Forecast Stock Prices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36914)
*Jiacheng Guo,Suozhi Huang,Shuzhen Li,Yunlong Gao,Zerui Cheng,Jason Ge,Shushu Liang,Zihao Li,Hao Lu,Ming Yin,Shilong Liu,Jiashuo Liu,Xu Kuang,Mengdi Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过SFT与PPO后训练，Qwen3-4B在时序股票价格沙盒中的预测评分翻倍，并改变其市场信息探查方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数学推理、软件工程等可验证结果任务已被证明能通过后训练大幅提升，但金融市场预测是否同样受益尚不明确：收益噪声大，且有效预测所需的信息集本身事前并不清楚，模型必须自行决定收集哪些观测并在结果未知前给出数值判断。

Method: 构建按时序的股票价格沙盒，模型收集价格、成交量、相对表现与市场背景证据后预测未来收益；用工具使用示范对Qwen3-4B做监督微调（SFT），再用近端策略优化（PPO）以预测得分对实际收益的终端奖励进行训练，得到AURA-4B。

Result: 方向-幅度评分从20.94提升至43.31（翻倍以上），在该基准上与前沿语言模型相当；条件幅度一致率由33.3升至66.2，方向准确率由62.9变为65.4；SFT扩大工具使用，PPO进一步提高排序类与市场背景类查询占比。

Conclusion: 在该结果筛选的基准上，后训练能显著提升金融预测表现，同时改变模型探查市场的方式。
