Abstract: Post-training has been shown to significantly improve language models' performance on tasks with verifiable outcomes, including mathematical reasoning, software engineering, and computer use. However, whether the same approach can improve forecasting in financial markets is much less clear. Compared with tasks with verifiable outcomes, not only are realized returns noisy, but even what constitutes a relevant information set for making effective predictions is not obvious a priori: the model must decide which observations to gather and then commit to a numerical judgment before the outcome is known. We study this question in a chronological stock-price sandbox, where a language model gathers price, volume, relative-performance, and market-context evidence and predicts a future return. We post-train Qwen3-4B with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on tool-use demonstrations, then proximal policy optimization (PPO) with a terminal reward given by the forecast score against the realized return. The resulting AURA-4B more than doubles the starting direction--magnitude score, from 20.94 to 43.31, and is comparable to frontier language models on this benchmark. Conditional magnitude agreement rises from 33.3 to 66.2, while directional accuracy changes from 62.9 to 65.4. SFT expands tool use, and PPO further increases the share of ranking and market-context queries. These results show that post-training can substantially improve financial forecasting performance, together with changes in how the model investigates the market, on this outcome-selected benchmark.

</details>


### [311] [Benchmarking Automatic Speech Recognition Tools for Iberian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36920)
*Fernando López,Pablo Gómez,David Solans,Paulo Villegas,Jordi Luque*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对11个ASR系统在五种伊比利亚语言及两种对照语言上进行准确率与效率的综合评测，发现无单一模型全面占优，低资源语言（尤其巴斯克语）显著退化，并普遍存在性别差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对伊比利亚语言的ASR综合评测仍有限，低资源语言、偏差与效率权衡方面研究不足，缺乏面向实际模型选择的实用指导。

Method: 基准测试11个系统（10个开放权重模型+1个商业API），覆盖巴斯克语、加泰罗尼亚语、加利西亚语、葡萄牙语、西班牙语，并以德语和土耳其语作为对照；使用85小时数据集，涵盖朗读语音、广播媒体与有声书；以词错误率（WER）和实时因子（RTF/RTFx）评估准确率与效率。

Result: 没有单一模型占据主导地位，准确率、效率与语言覆盖度之间存在明显权衡；低资源语言（尤其巴斯克语）性能显著下降，体现训练覆盖度的影响；多数系统上观察到一致的性别差异。

Conclusion: 该基准为真实场景下的模型选择提供了实用指导，并揭示多语言ASR在公平性方面仍面临挑战。

Abstract: Comprehensive evaluations of automatic speech recognition (ASR) for Iberian languages remain limited, and low-resource languages, biases, and efficiency trade-offs are underexplored. We benchmark eleven systems, ten open-weight models and one commercial API, across five Iberian languages (Basque, Catalan, Galician, Portuguese, Spanish), with German and Turkish as controls. Evaluation uses an 85-hour dataset covering read speech, broadcast media, and audiobooks, assessing accuracy and efficiency via word error rate (WER) and real-time factors (RTF/RTFx). Results show no single model dominates: accuracy, efficiency, and language coverage present clear trade-offs. Low-resource languages, especially Basque, degrade significantly, highlighting the role of training coverage. We observe consistent sex disparities across most systems, highlighting fairness challenges in multilingual ASR. Overall, the benchmark provides practical guidance for real-world model selection.

</details>


### [312] [Dating the Model: Hidden Dates in System Prompts Affect LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36931)
*Mario Sanz-Guerrero,Minh Duc Bui,Manuel Mager,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现系统提示中隐式注入的当前日期是导致LLM输出不可复现的关键被忽视因素，其影响甚至超过批量大小和数值精度，并会改变模型排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究已知LLM输出会随硬件和批处理变化，但忽略了一个用户无法控制、且每天变化的因素：系统提示中被隐式注入的当前日期。作者希望揭示该因素对可复现性和公平比较的影响。

Method: 在9个近期LLM和6个数据集上进行实验，覆盖多项选择问答（MCQA）、数学推理、代码生成和机器翻译任务，仅改变当前日期来观察性能变化，并与批量大小、数值精度等其他非确定性来源对比，同时测试思维链和少样本提示是否缓解该敏感性。

Result: 性能仅随当前日期变化：MCQA最高波动6%，数学推理14%，代码生成7%，机器翻译2.84 BLEU；模型排名也随之改变，影响排行榜。日期效应超过批量大小和数值精度等非确定性来源；思维链和少样本提示不能降低敏感性，思维链甚至放大该效应。

Conclusion: 研究强调需建立谨慎的评估协议，以保证LLM研究中的可复现性和公平比较。

Abstract: Reproducibility is essential for scientific research, yet prior work shows that LLM outputs vary with hardware and batching. We identify an overlooked factor: the hidden injection of the current date into system prompts, which users cannot control and which changes every day. Across 9 recent LLMs and 6 datasets spanning multiple-choice QA (MCQA), math reasoning, code generation, and machine translation, performance varies solely with the current date, with deltas of up to 6% on MCQA, 14% on math reasoning, 7% on code generation, and 2.84 BLEU on machine translation. Model rankings also shift, affecting leaderboards. This date effect exceeds other sources of non-determinism, such as batch size and numerical precision. Standard prompting techniques -- chain-of-thought and few-shot prompting -- do not reduce the sensitivity; chain-of-thought even amplifies it. Our findings underscore the need for careful evaluation protocols to ensure reproducibility and fair comparisons in LLM research.

</details>


### [313] [ER-JEPA: Experience Replay Improves Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36952)
*Jingnan Pu,Zi-En Fan,Feng Lian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ER-JEPA，在LLM-JEPA基础上引入情节回放路径，将训练对存入记忆并在每步检索相关数据以提供额外监督，从而结合当前批次与存储训练对进行token预测与表示对齐的学习。在NL-RX、GSM8K、Spider和NQ-Open数据集上，ER-JEPA持续优于LLM-JEPA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM擅长token级生成，但可能学习到不理想的抽象语义且缺乏全面感知。LLM-JEPA通过联合嵌入预测架构对齐同一知识的不同视图来缓解该问题，但强对齐并不必然带来准确、稳定的预测，因此需要引入额外监督机制。

Method: 在LLM-JEPA基础上增加情节回放（episodic replay）路径，使用记忆模块存储训练对；每一步存储并检索相关数据，提供额外监督，使模型能同时从当前批次和已存储训练对中学习，用于token预测和表示对齐。

Result: 在NL-RX、GSM8K、Spider和NQ-Open四个数据集上的实验表明，ER-JEPA持续优于LLM-JEPA。

Conclusion: 通过情节回放引入额外监督可弥补强对齐与准确稳定预测之间的差距，ER-JEPA是一种有效的改进方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) excel at token-level generation but may learn undesirable abstract semantics and lack comprehensive perception. LLM-JEPA mitigates this by aligning different views of the same underlying knowledge via a joint-embedding predictive architecture (JEPA). However, strong alignment does not necessarily lead to accurate, stable predictions. To address this, we propose ER-JEPA, which adds an episodic replay path to LLM-JEPA. ER-JEPA stores training pairs in a memory. At each step, it stores and retrieves relevant data to provide additional supervision. This enables learning from both the current batch and stored training pairs, providing additional supervision for token prediction and representation alignment. Experiments across multiple datasets (NL-RX, GSM8K, Spider, and NQ-Open) demonstrate that ER-JEPA consistently outperforms LLM-JEPA.

</details>


### [314] [Chinese-Jev: Bringing System One Model to Chinese-Language Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36965)
*Zexiao Wang,Zihao Zhang,Xudong Wang,Pan Wang,Ziyi Ye,Haoyu Zhao,Zuxuan Wu,Shuicheng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chinese-Jev，一个面向中文决策任务的 System One 模型，通过统一数据处理与训练流程提升中文决策准确率，并实现高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Jev 类 System One 模型在中文决策任务上准确率有限，限制了其在通用及专业场景中的实用性，因此需要一个专门针对中文决策的模型。

Method: 采用统一的数据处理与训练流程：将异构中文标注转换为候选选项上的概率目标，实现跨领域与题型的共享训练；使用轻量级 encoder-only 骨干网络，通过面向决策的训练学习候选答案打分；先在千万级通用语料上预训练，再分别针对医疗、法律、金融领域微调。

Result: 第一阶段预训练后，Chinese-Jev 在通用任务上准确率超过闭源 Jev 模型 1.24%，并实现 20.3 倍加速；领域微调后在医疗领域较 Jev 提升 4.0%，在专业领域平均达到 Jev 的 92% 准确率，加速 17 倍，平均延迟仅 15 ms；INT8 量化模型可在移动端部署，单次决策推理延迟约 1.0 秒。

Conclusion: Chinese-Jev 有效弥补了中文决策任务上的性能差距，兼顾准确率与推理效率，并支持端侧部署；同时提出了 CJ-Bench 用于评估中文通用与领域决策的准确率与校准。

Abstract: System One models such as Jev offer an efficient alternative to generative language models for tasks that require decisions rather than open-ended responses. However, existing Jev models exhibit limited Chinese-language decision accuracy, restricting their utility in both general and specialized settings. In this paper, we introduce Chinese-Jev, a System One model that addresses this gap through a unified data processing and training pipeline. Our data processing protocol converts heterogeneous Chinese-language annotations into probability targets over candidate options, enabling a shared training formulation across domains and question formats. To enable efficient inference, Chinese-Jev adopts a lightweight encoder-only backbone for text encoding and learns to score candidate answers through decision-oriented training. To address the misalignment between the pre-training distribution and downstream Chinese-language scenarios, we first train the model on a general-purpose corpus of 10 million examples, then fine-tune it separately for the medical, legal, and financial domains. To evaluate decision accuracy and calibration in both general and domain-specific Chinese-language settings, we introduce Chinese-Jev Bench (CJ-Bench). After first-stage pre-training, Chinese-Jev exceeds the accuracy of the closed-source Jev model by 1.24% on general-domain tasks while achieving a 20.3x speedup. Subsequent domain-specific fine-tuning yields a 4.0% accuracy improvement over Jev in medicine and achieves 92% of Jev's average accuracy across specialized domains, with a 17x speedup and an average latency of only 15 ms per example. We further demonstrate on-device deployment of an INT8-quantized model on mobile devices, achieving an inference latency of approximately 1.0 second per decision. The project is available at https://gulucaptain.github.io/Chinese-Jev/.

</details>


### [315] [Repetition, Not Length: Isolating the Counting Failure in Neural Text-to-Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36974)
*Kirill Borodin,Vasilii Kudryavtsev,Maxim Maslov,Grach Mkrtchian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 重复本身（而非长度）是导致TTS模型失效的原因：重复句控制组94.3%完全正确，重复组仅18.2%，且该差距在多种鲁棒性检验下从未反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTS模型在文本反复重复同一短语时会出现循环、截断和计数丢失，通常被归因于文本长度。作者希望区分"重复"与"长度"两个混淆因素，验证究竟是重复结构本身破坏了模型。

Method: 为每个重复句子配一个句数和词数匹配、但无相邻重复词的控制组；测试三种架构的六个模型；进行贪婪解码、重复惩罚扫描、四个独立语音识别器和420种分析规格的稳健性检验；另用留出的第四种架构做预测验证，并检验两个非自回归基线；通过改变文本周期观察失效随周期性变化。

Result: 控制组近乎完美，重复组在k>=6时仅18.2%完全正确（控制组94.3%）；差距在所有420种规格中均未反转；留出的第四种架构误差在1个百分点内；两个非自回归基线中有一个出现同样失效；失效随周期性平滑增长，即使无相邻重复词仍保留一半。

Conclusion: 重复结构本身（而非长度）是TTS失效的根本原因，且该效应具有跨架构、跨解码策略的普遍性和平滑的周期性依赖。

Abstract: Text-to-speech models loop, truncate and lose count on text that repeats a phrase many times. We show that repetition itself is what breaks them, not the length that comes with it. Every repeated sentence in our test set is paired with a control of matched sentence and word count in which no word ever repeats back-to-back. Six models from three architectures render the controls almost perfectly and fail the repeated twins: 94.3% against 18.2% exactly right at k >= 6. The gap survives greedy decoding, repetition-penalty sweeps, four independent speech recognisers and 420 analysis specifications without once reversing sign; a held-out fourth architecture lands within a point of its predicted gap, and one of two non-autoregressive baselines shows the same failure. Varying the period of the text shows the failure grows smoothly with periodicity, half of it surviving when no word is adjacent to itself.

</details>


### [316] [AMU:Admission and Memory Update for Personalized Conversations---Structured Memory with SLM Guided Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36976)
*Tao Hwang,Yishi Diao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AMU是一个由小语言模型引导的结构化框架，在写入时对个性化对话记忆进行准入与更新控制，保持记忆更干净、更可检索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型已成为个性化助手的基础，但长期交互中的持久用户记忆维护仍具挑战。现有记忆系统多关注存储、检索或整合，写入环节控制不足：临时请求、重复陈述和过时用户状态可能进入记忆，并在后续被检索用于个性化，影响效果。

Method: 提出AMU（Admission and Memory Update for Personalized Conversations），一个SLM引导的结构化框架，用于写入时记忆控制。包括两部分：结构化记忆过滤，决定哪些内容应进入记忆；SLM引导的存储管理，判断被准入记录应单独存储、作为重复项丢弃，还是作为更新融合。

Result: 在受控的记忆写入与检索设置下评估AMU，实验结果表明AMU能维护更干净且更可检索的个性化记忆。

Conclusion: 通过在写入阶段引入结构化过滤与SLM引导的存储管理，AMU有效提升了个性化记忆的质量与可检索性，为长期交互中的记忆控制提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become the foundation of personalized assistants, but maintaining persistent user memory across long-term interactions remains challenging. Existing memory systems often focus on storage, retrieval, or consolidation, while memory writing remains less controlled: transient requests, duplicate statements, and outdated user states may enter memory and later be retrieved for personalization. In this paper, we present AMU: Admission and Memory Update for Personalized Conversations, an SLM-guided (Small language model guided) structured framework for writing-time memory control. AMU uses structured memory filtering to decide what should enter memory and SLM-guided storage management to determine whether an admitted record should be stored separately, discarded as a duplicate, or fused as an update. We evaluate AMU in a controlled memory writing and retrieval setting. Experimental results show that AMU maintains cleaner and more retrievable personalized memories.

</details>


### [317] [SRJudge: Empowering Large Language Models with Selective Reasoning for Fine-Grained Knowledge Concept Tagging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36982)
*Zhiwei Yang,Jiahua Yang,Huiru Lin,Xing Chen,Quanlong Guan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出三阶段Select-Reason-Judge (SRJudge) 框架，用于细粒度知识概念标注：先由SLM缩小候选集，再用轻量LLM推理，最后由大LLM评判，并构建生物、物理两个数据集验证其优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在大规模候选概念集中因决策空间维度过高而难以准确选择正确概念，需要降低决策空间并增强推理能力以提升细粒度知识概念标注效果。

Method: 三阶段框架SRJudge：Stage 1 Selector用微调的小语言模型（如BERT）将候选概念缩小到top-K短名单；Stage 2 Reasoner用轻量LLM对候选进行精细推理，结合改进的强化学习策略、动态任务相关奖励函数和剪枝机制；Stage 3 Judge用更大的LLM评估推理过程与解释的整体合理性并确定最终输出。另构建S_Bio和S_Phy两个高质量数据集。

Result: 在基准数据集上持续优于现有最优基线，验证了方法的有效性与优越性；同时构建了生物学数据集S_Bio和物理学数据集S_Phy用于进一步验证。

Conclusion: SRJudge通过选择-推理-评判的三阶段协同，结合强化学习与剪枝机制，有效缓解LLM在大候选集中的选择难题，提升知识概念标注性能。

Abstract: Knowledge concept tagging aims to assign specific concept or topic labels to educational content, which is essential for both educators and learners in traditional and online teaching practices. Recent work has explored large language models (LLMs) for this task, achieving promising performance. However, LLMs still struggle to select the correct concept from a large-scale candidate set due to the high dimensionality of the decision space. In this paper, we propose a novel three-stage Select-Reason-Judge (SRJudge) framework, which empowers LLMs with selective reasoning capability for fine-grained knowledge concept tagging. Specifically, the Selector in Stage 1 first narrows the candidate concepts to a top-K shortlist by fine-tuning a small language model (SLM), e.g., BERT, since the top-$K$ predictions hit the correct concept in most cases, thereby reducing the decision space of correct candidates. Next, the Stage 2 Reasoner employs a lightweight LLM for refined reasoning over the shortlisted candidates. It further integrates an improved reinforcement learning strategy with a dynamic task-specific reward function and a pruning mechanism to better align with human reasoning preferences. Finally, a larger LLM acts as a judger that evaluates the overall rationality of the reasoning process and its explanations to determine the final output. In addition, we construct two high-quality datasets for further validation, i.e., the biology dataset S_Bio and the physics dataset S_Phy. Experimental results demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across benchmark datasets, verifying its effectiveness and superiority. Resources are available at: https://github.com/Nicozwy/SRJudge.

</details>


### [318] [CypherTurn: A Multi-Turn Benchmark for Conversational Text-to-Cypher Evaluation and the Autonomy Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36987)
*Yuzhe Zhang,Weijie Zhu,Haolin Yang,Ziyun Zhang,Xianwei Xue,Mengke Chen,Qiutong Pan,Huaqian Cai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个面向对话式Text-to-Cypher的基准CypherTurn，含721个会话、5,927轮对话，覆盖7个知识图谱与13类对话现象，并揭示多轮图数据库查询中的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图数据库自然语言查询基准仅评估孤立的单轮查询，无法反映分析师实际工作的多轮会话场景，缺乏对对话式Text-to-Cypher的系统评测。

Method: 构建CypherTurn基准（721会话、5,927轮、7个知识图谱、13类对话现象），并在引导式oracle协议与完全自主agentic协议下评测15个模型。

Result: 最佳模型执行准确率仅64.7%，会话级正确率不足5%；发现Autonomy Divergence现象（自主运行下前沿模型排名重排）；动作预算从3倍增至10倍无法弥合自主性差距（强模型每轮自限于约2个动作）；单轮Cypher微调损害多轮指令遵循，而架构适配的专门化优于部分前沿模型。

Conclusion: CypherTurn确立了对话式图数据库推理的开放挑战，表明错误管理与生成能力部分独立，多轮自主查询仍有巨大提升空间。

Abstract: Graph databases are increasingly queried through natural language, yet every existing benchmark evaluates isolated single-turn queries rather than the multi-turn sessions through which analysts actually work. We introduce CypherTurn, the first benchmark for conversational Text-to-Cypher evaluation, comprising 721 sessions and 5,927 turns across 7 knowledge graphs and 13 conversational phenomena. We evaluate 15 models under a guided oracle protocol and a fully autonomous agentic protocol, yielding four findings. First, the best model reaches only 64.7% execution accuracy, and session-level correctness remains below 5%. Second, despite strong overall rank correlation, frontier models exhibit a consequential reordering of the top of the leaderboard under autonomous operation, a phenomenon we term the Autonomy Divergence, which reveals error-management as a partially independent capability from raw generation skill. Third, scaling action budgets from x3 to x10 fails to close the autonomy gap, as the strongest frontier models self-limit to approximately two actions per turn regardless of available budget. Fourth, single-turn Cypher fine-tuning degrades multi-turn instruction following, while architecture-appropriate specialization outperforms several frontier models. These results establish CypherTurn as an open challenge for conversational graph database reasoning. Code and data are available at https://github.com/BarryQ/CypherTurn.

</details>


### [319] [LatCom: Cross-Agent Latent Compression for Efficient Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37017)
*Shinan Zhang,Tao Zhang,Qihui Zhu,Mengjie Zhang,Dong Jin,Yunpeng Hou,Shuangwu Chen,Xiaobin Tan,Quan Zheng,Jian Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 LatCom 跨智能体潜在压缩框架，将多发送方潜表示映射为固定数量的接收方可读且任务相关的槽位，实现高效多智能体潜在协作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 LLM 的多智能体系统越来越多使用潜在协作以规避自然语言通信的信息损失与重复编解码开销。但直接转发所有发送方潜表示会使接收端上下文随智能体数量和推理长度增长，增加计算、内存和协作延迟。现有潜在压缩方法对每个发送方独立压缩后再拼接，跨智能体冗余未被解决。

Method: LatCom 将多个发送方潜表示映射到固定数量的接收方可读、任务相关槽位，不重建全部发送方隐藏状态，而是针对接收端任务效用优化压缩潜表示。其压缩器采用两阶段训练：先进行单发送方可读性学习，建立冻结接收方可解释的潜接口；再进行多发送方融合学习，训练压缩器融合互补证据并去除智能体间冗余。

Result: 在多个基准上使用 Qwen3-4B 实验，相比 LatentMAS 平均推理加速 2.46 倍，输出 token 用量降低 70.3%，同时保持相近的平均准确率。

Conclusion: LatCom 通过跨智能体潜在压缩与两阶段训练，有效消除智能体间冗余，在保持精度的同时显著提升多智能体潜在协作的推理效率并降低 token 消耗。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) increasingly use latent collaboration to avoid the information loss and repeated encoding-decoding overhead of natural-language communication. However, directly forwarding all sender latents makes the receiver-side context scale with both the number of agents and the reasoning length, increasing computation, memory usage, and collaboration latency. A natural solution is latent compression. But we find that cross-agent redundancy remains unresolved in existing latent compression approaches, which typically compress each sender independently and then concatenate the results. We propose LatCom, a cross-agent latent compression framework for efficient multi-agent latent collaboration. LatCom maps multiple sender latents into a fixed number of receiver-readable and task-relevant slots. Rather than reconstructing all sender hidden states, it optimizes the compressed latents for receiver-side task utility. LatCom trains the compressor in two stages: single-sender readability learning first establishes a latent interface interpretable by the frozen receiver, and multi-sender fusion learning then trains the compressor to fuse complementary evidence and remove redundancy across agents. Experiments on multiple benchmarks with Qwen3-4B show that LatCom achieves an average 2.46x inference speed-up over LatentMAS and reduces output token usage by 70.3% while maintaining comparable average accuracy.

</details>


### [320] [Selecting The Most Informative Tokens in Natural Language Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37040)
*Federico Torrielli,Gianluca Barmina,Andrea Blasi Núñez,Amon Rapp,Luigi Di Caro,Peter Schneider-Kamp,Lukas Galke Poech*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究自然语言自编码器审计中应优先解释哪些 token 位置，发现在 470 万条解释上，仅依赖对话结构的排序器比计算信号更有效，且解释约 5% 位置即可保留近乎全部成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逐 token 位置生成解释成本高昂，审计者需要一个高效的选择策略，以在有限预算下发现潜在威胁（如提示注入与隐瞒行为）。

Method: 在提示注入与隐瞒任务的 470 万条解释上，比较两类位置选择信号：模型计算信号与仅基于对话结构训练的排序器；并测试预训练 verbalizer 在无额外训练下恢复模型经微调隐瞒的词。

Result: 对话结构通常比计算信号选出更相关的解释，且无需前向传播。四个数据集中的三个，仅解释 5% 位置即可保留近乎全部成功率（成功指获得关于威胁的解释），收益随审计任务变化。预训练 verbalizer 能恢复被微调隐瞒的词。

Conclusion: 审计可集中解释生成于少数关键位置；有用的解释可超越 verbalizer 原本训练的模型范围，为高效威胁审计提供依据。

Abstract: Natural language autoencoders translate a language model's internal activations into readable explanations. Explaining every token position is costly. Which positions should an auditor inspect to understand a potential threat? We study this question across $4.7$ million explanations on prompt injection and concealment. We compare signals from model computation with a ranker trained only on chat structure. Chat structure usually selects more relevant explanations than the computational signals, without requiring a model forward pass for position selection. On three of four datasets, explaining just $5\%$ of positions retains nearly all of the success rate from explaining every position, where success means obtaining an explanation about the threat. The benefit varies with the audit task. We also show that pretrained verbalizers recover words that models have learned to conceal through fine-tuning, without additional verbalizer training. These results identify where auditors can concentrate explanation generation and show that useful explanations can extend beyond the model a verbalizer was trained to describe.

</details>


### [321] [Learning from Think-Mode Advantage via On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37044)
*Wanqi Ren,Jianxiang Wang,Danxuan Liu,Linyi Ding,Yuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究"显式中间推理（think-mode）优势"如何通过 on-policy distillation（OPD）迁移到学生模型，提出 trace-response divergence (TRD) 概念，并设计 ThinkOPD 方法，按响应级别路由监督，在数学推理与代码生成任务上优于 Uniform ThinkOPD 及多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 显式中间推理让 LLM 具备更强的解题模式。作者希望将这种"think-mode 优势"通过 on-policy distillation 传递给学生模型，但发现自然的 think-enabled OPD 基线（Uniform ThinkOPD）存在缺陷：固定教师被条件化在一条共享 think trace 上，而该 trace 未必与每个学生的完整响应路径兼容，即便响应结果相同，也会产生不同的师生差异。因此需要更精细的监督路由方式。

Method: 作者将上述交互总结为 trace-response divergence (TRD)，并据此提出 ThinkOPD 方法：在响应级别进行监督路由，将 group-relative reward gain 与基于 TRD 的兼容性代理相结合；最终响应权重在每次 rollout group 内归一化。OPD 保留学生生成的轨迹，并在学生访问过的前缀上提供密集的 token-level 教师目标，特权推理仅用于蒸馏阶段而非学生推理阶段。

Result: 在数学推理与代码生成任务上，ThinkOPD 在同模型设置以及两组跨模型师生配对中均优于 Uniform ThinkOPD，并在受控比较中超过代表性的 rationale 与 self-distillation 基线。受控干预实验表明，outcome benefit 与 TRD 代理提供了互补的路由信号。

Conclusion: Enable think 的 OPD 为研究"教师优势如何沿学生响应变得可迁移"提供了一个受控实验环境。ThinkOPD 通过 TRD 兼容性代理与结果收益的响应级路由，有效提升了蒸馏效果。

Abstract: Explicit intermediate reasoning gives large language models (LLMs) a stronger problem-solving mode. We study learning from this think-mode advantage via on-policy distillation (OPD). OPD preserves student-generated trajectories and provides dense token-level teacher targets at student-visited prefixes. Privileged reasoning is used during distillation rather than student inference. Uniform ThinkOPD, a natural think-enabled OPD baseline, conditions a fixed teacher on one shared think trace and uniformly distills every sibling student response. Although its prefixes are on-policy, the trace need not follow a route compatible with every complete response: the same privileged trace can induce different teacher-student discrepancies even when responses reach the same outcome. We summarize this interaction with trace-response divergence (TRD) and introduce ThinkOPD, which routes supervision at the response level by combining group-relative reward gain with a TRD-based compatibility proxy. Final response weights are normalized within each rollout group. Across mathematical reasoning and code generation, ThinkOPD outperforms Uniform ThinkOPD in both same-model settings and both cross-model teacher-student pairs, and it exceeds representative rationale and self-distillation baselines in a controlled comparison. Controlled interventions show that outcome benefit and the TRD-based proxy provide complementary routing signals in this setting. Think-enabled OPD provides a controlled setting for studying how teacher advantage becomes transferable along student responses.

</details>


### [322] [Traverse: Learning When to Remember, Reset, and Redirect for Long-Horizon Web Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37082)
*Jingyuan Ma,Lynx Aster,He Zhang,Siyao Song,Weijie Yuan,Zhe Zhang,Kai Jia,Zhifang Sui*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自主搜索框架（Rubric-Answer-Verify三状态）配合Seal Memory主动上下文管理工具，用强化学习训练，发现Seal Collapse问题并通过只训练上下文管理后最终片段解决，35B模型在BrowseComp达72.83。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程信息检索智能体易累积噪声或误导性上下文，导致早期错误持续存在、难以恢复。

Method: 构建自主搜索框架：智能体通过Rubric（定义有效答案标准）、Answer（按标准搜索）、Verify（独立验证结果并决定终止或继续）三状态管理自身搜索过程；配备Seal Memory工具实现主动上下文管理；发现RL训练引发Seal Collapse导致训练不稳定，采用只训练上下文管理后最终片段的策略解决。

Result: 35B模型在BrowseComp取得72.83，优于同类开源系统，并在BrowseComp-ZH、xbench、DeepSearchQA、WideSearch、金融调查、产品搜索上均较基座模型持续提升。

Conclusion: 消融实验表明自主压缩优于自动压缩，验证了RL设计的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon information-seeking agents often accumulate noisy or misleading context, causing early mistakes to persist and making recovery increasingly difficult. We introduce an autonomous search harness in which the agent manages its own search process through three states: Rubric, Answer, and Verify. The agent first defines criteria for a valid answer, searches under these criteria, and then independently verifies the result before deciding whether to terminate or continue searching. It is further equipped with a Seal Memory tool that enables active context management. Training this behavior with reinforcement learning, however, can induce Seal Collapse, resulting in unstable training and preventing the agent from reliably learning when and how to use its memory tools. We solve this with a simple strategy that trains only the final segment after context management. Our 35B model achieves 72.83 on BrowseComp, outperforming comparable open-source systems, and consistently improves over the base model across BrowseComp-ZH, xbench, DeepSearchQA, WideSearch, financial investigation, and product search. Ablations show that autonomous compression outperforms automatic compaction and validate our RL design.

</details>


### [323] [What Does Post-Training Change in Multilingual Reasoning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37104)
*Hongyang Li,Xiao Li,Caesar Wu,Grégoire Danoy,Pascal Bouvry*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 开源推理模型在不同语言间的推理能力获取不平等。作者审计Qwen3在11种语言上的竞赛数学任务，追踪正确性、语言遵循、终止性与交付效率，揭示瓶颈随后训练阶段迁移，并给出实现多语言可靠推理的建设性路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当模型能解题却无法用用户语言给出完整解答时，语言就从性能差异的来源变成能力获取的障碍。研究旨在定位这一不平等在训练流程中的具体来源。

Method: 审计Qwen3多个检查点在11种语言、竞赛数学任务上的表现；评估同一模型家族的13个端点，涵盖已发布检查点、两种规模的多语言SFT、受控SFT消融，以及三种强化学习奖励设计，并联合追踪正确性、语言遵循、终止与交付效率。

Result: 十种非英语语言中，仅15.4-17.9%的题目能在任意16次采样中获得可见推理的、正确且终止的目标语言解答，英语则为92.9%。已发布模型常以英语推理；多语言SFT恢复了目标语言推理，但准确率下降，且该代价并非多语言混合特有；非英语推理链易陷入不终止循环。RL在两臂均无准确率损失地恢复终止，但只有含语言项奖励的臂能真正实现目标语言交付，仅奖励正确性会使模型退回英语。

Conclusion: 这些阶段共同确立了一条从英语枢轴能力出发、可可靠交付的多语言推理后训练路径。

Abstract: Open-source reasoning models provide unequal access to reasoning capability across languages. When a model can solve a problem but cannot deliver a complete solution in the user's language, language becomes an access barrier rather than merely a source of performance variation. We audit Qwen3 checkpoints on competition-mathematics tasks in eleven languages. Across the ten non-English languages, only 15.4-17.9% of problems receive a correct, terminating solution with visible reasoning in the requested language in any of 16 samples, compared with 92.9% in English. To identify the source of this disparity, we evaluate thirteen endpoints from one model family, spanning released checkpoints, multilingual supervised fine-tuning (SFT) at two scales, controlled SFT ablations, and three reinforcement-learning (RL) reward formulations. We jointly track correctness, language adherence, termination, and delivery efficiency. The dominant bottleneck shifts across post-training stages. Released models often reason in English. Multilingual SFT restores target-language reasoning, but accuracy declines across multilingual, English-only, and single-language SFT runs, showing that this cost is not specific to multilingual mixing; non-English reasoning traces additionally become prone to non-terminating loops. RL restores termination in both arms at no cost in accuracy, but only the arm whose reward includes a language term delivers: rewarding correctness alone returns the model to English. Together, these stages establish a constructive post-training path from English-pivoted capability to multilingual reasoning that is reliably delivered.

</details>


### [324] [Cross-Linguistic Effects in Bilingual Phoneme BabyLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37121)
*Nikitas Theodoropoulos,Maria Lymperaiou,Giorgos Filandrianos*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究训练了以音素为输入的双语BabyLMs，将英语固定为L2，L1在德语、瑞典语、波斯语和巴斯克语之间变化，以考察音素输入条件下L1-L2交互。结果显示，相较正字法输入，音素输入下语法学习轨迹的L1相关差异更强，而早期词汇差异与音素库相似性一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨语言影响是双语一语习得的核心议题。人工学习者可通过受控的语言组合与学习条件比较来研究L1-L2交互。已有研究以发展上合理的约束训练双语语言模型，但人类与模型学习者仍存在根本差异，其中最突出的是输入模态：儿童主要从口语输入学习，而语言模型通常基于正字法文本训练。为缩小该差距，作者结合"音素表征训练"与"双语BabyLMs"两个方向。

Method: 训练以音素表征为输入的双语BabyLMs。固定英语为L2，L1分别取德语、瑞典语、波斯语和巴斯克语，选择标准是这些语言与英语在句法距离和音素库距离上构成不同对比组合，从而可在受控条件下比较L1-L2交互。

Result: 在音素输入下，语法学习轨迹表现出比正字法输入更强的L1相关变异；早期词汇层面的差异则与音素库相似性相对应。

Conclusion: 音素输入能放大L1对语法学习轨迹的影响，并揭示词汇早期差异与音素库相似性的关联，说明输入模态是缩小人类与模型学习者差距、研究跨语言影响的重要维度。

Abstract: Cross-linguistic effects are a central topic in bilingual first-language acquisition. Artificial learners can help investigate L1-L2 interactions by enabling controlled comparisons across language combinations and learning conditions. Recent work explores this direction by training bilingual language models under developmentally plausible constraints. However, human and model learners still diverge in fundamental ways, with one major difference being input modality: children learn primarily from spoken input, whereas language models are typically trained on orthographic text. To reduce this gap, researchers have trained models on phonemic representations of speech. In this work, we combine these research directions to train bilingual BabyLMs with phonemic input. We keep English fixed as the L2 and vary the L1 across German, Swedish, Persian, and Basque, selected to represent contrasting combinations of syntactic and phoneme-inventory distance from English. Our results show stronger L1-related variation in grammatical learning trajectories under phonemic than orthographic input, while early lexical differences align with phoneme-inventory similarity.

</details>


### [325] [LLM unbranding: Erasing Commercial Identity while Preserving Generic Utility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37127)
*Kajetan Ożóg,Alicja Wojciechowska,Dawid Malarz,Paweł Batorski,Artur Kasymov,Przemysław Spurek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"LLM Unbranding"新任务，聚焦于消除大语言模型输出中的文本化商业外观（trade dress），并提出免参数更新的推理时方法MUTE。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉图像生成中"去品牌化"已是常规实践，用以防止logo产生负面联想。而LLM在多样语境中频繁生成品牌描述，带来商标淡化、虚假归属和品牌诽谤等风险。相较于显性视觉logo，文本中的品牌特征（如特色用语、口号、风格标记）更为隐蔽，现有方法难以应对。

Method: 1) 首次正式定义LLM Unbranding任务，重点处理文本输出中的trade dress；2) 构建涵盖多商业领域知名品牌的综合评测数据集；3) 在该基准上严格评估现有最先进的机器遗忘模型；4) 提出MUTE——一种推理时方法，通过迭代优化循环系统性地调整系统指令，无需脆弱的参数更新即可消除品牌泄漏。

Result: 现有最先进机器遗忘模型在该基准上表现出在选择性文本去品牌化方面的明显局限；MUTE能有效中和文本化trade dress，同时保留LLM的通用能力与实用性。

Conclusion: LLM Unbranding是一项重要且新兴的挑战，MUTE通过推理时指令迭代优化，为文本去品牌化提供了安全、有效且不损害模型通用能力的解决方案。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Establishing unbranding as a critical practice to prevent visual logos from acquiring negative connotations is standard in image generation. Large Language Models (LLMs) now face a parallel and emerging challenge. These models frequently generate brand descriptions within diverse contexts. This frequency introduces significant risks, such as trademark dilution, false attribution, and brand defamation. In response, we formally define the novel task of LLM Unbranding. We specifically address the complex challenge of managing trade dress within textual outputs. This involves neutralizing characteristic language, slogans, and stylistic markers that define brand identity. Crucially, these elements are less evident than explicit visual logos. To benchmark this task, we introduce a comprehensive evaluation dataset incorporating prominent brands from multiple commercial domains. We rigorously evaluate existing state-of-the-art machine unlearning models using this benchmark. This evaluation identifies their limitations in selective textual unbranding. Finally, we propose MUTE, a novel inference-time method that effectively neutralizes textual trade dress while preserving the LLM's general capabilities and utility. By leveraging an iterative refinement loop, MUTE systematically optimizes system instructions to safely eliminate brand leakage without requiring fragile parameter updates.
  Code and dataset: The evaluation dataset and code for LLM Unbranding are available at https://github.com/KajetanOzog/LLM_unbranding. The implementation of MUTE is available at https://github.com/KajetanOzog/MUTE.

</details>


### [326] [Bridging Semantic Gaps in RAG through Generated Context Knowledge Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37171)
*Xinkai Du,Chao Lv,Yalin Sun,Quanjie Han,Lei Yao,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: The paper proposes Knowledge-Aware Semantic Bridging (KASB), a framework that improves passage selection in Retrieval-Augmented Generation by aligning the semantic space between queries and retrieved documents via intelligent knowledge fusion.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG is a fundamental NLP framework, but it is fundamentally constrained by a semantic space mismatch between queries and retrieved contexts.

Method: KASB performs semantic space alignment between queries and retrieved documents through intelligent knowledge fusion, leveraging the complementary strengths of generative and retrieval-based knowledge in a multistage process to enhance both relevance and accuracy.

Result: KASB is evaluated on three popular open-domain Question Answering datasets, demonstrating the effectiveness of the approach.

Conclusion: KASB effectively improves passage selection quality in RAG by bridging the semantic gap between queries and retrieved contexts through knowledge fusion.

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation has established itself as a fundamental framework in natural language processing, seamlessly integrating information retrieval with the generative capabilities of large language models. However, this process is fundamentally constrained by a critical challenge: semantic space mismatch between queries and retrieved contexts. We propose Knowledge-Aware Semantic Bridging (KASB), a novel framework that improves passage selection quality through semantic space alignment between queries and retrieved documents through intelligent knowledge fusion. Our approach leverages the complementary strengths of generative and retrieval-based knowledge through a multistage process that enhances both relevance and accuracy. We evaluate KASB on three popular open-domain Question Answering datasets to demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [327] [VLM Fine-Tuning for End-to-End Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37175)
*Qingsong Yan,Xia Jiang,Yaoxin Wu,Wen Song,Lu Zhang,Yingjie Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种通用的视觉-语言求解器，将文本实例描述与由输入派生的视觉表示结合，用单一VLM跨多种组合优化任务，经监督微调与验证器引导的强化学习训练，提升解的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅用文本序列化表示组合优化问题，可能掩盖对生成有效解至关重要的空间与关系结构。

Method: 用输入派生的视觉表示增强文本实例描述，采用单一视觉-语言模型（VLM）跨不同CO任务；训练方式为监督微调（SFT）加验证器引导的强化学习。视觉输入不含黄金解或任何解派生信息。

Result: 实验显示，VLM总体优于纯文本基线，在CVRP、JSSP等更复杂问题上提升尤为明显；视觉信息的优势在大规模问题上更显著。

Conclusion: 视觉信息可为组合优化提供文本之外的补充价值，通用视觉-语言求解器在复杂和大规模CO问题上更具潜力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have provided a unified interface for end-to-end combinatorial optimization (CO), but textual serialization alone may obscure spatial and relational structures that are important for generating effective CO solutions. This paper presents a general-purpose vision-language solver that augments textual instance descriptions with input-derived visual representations. A single vision-language model (VLM) is applied across different CO tasks and trained using supervised fine-tuning followed by verifier-guided reinforcement learning. While the visual inputs contain no gold solutions or solution-derived information, our experiments show that the VLM generally improves solution quality over its text-only counterpart, with particularly clear gains on more complex CO problems such as CVRP and JSSP. The advantage of visual information is more pronounced at large problem scales.

</details>


### [328] [CredWise: A Controlled Agentic Decision-Intelligence Framework for Explainable and Auditable Credit-Risk Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37223)
*Aakash Kumar Tiwari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出CredWise决策支持框架，将信用风险预测、概率校准、可解释AI、政策检索、SQL分析与受控智能体工作流整合于一体，在Lending Club数据上验证其预测、解释与检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信用风险预测在银行业很重要，但单一预测无法解释申请人为何有风险，也难以与其他证据结合，因此需要将预测、解释、政策证据和结构化分析整合为统一的决策支持流程。

Method: 使用Lending Club数据（1,345,310笔贷款、18个特征），按时间划分训练集（2007–2016）、验证集（2017）和测试集（2018）；训练XGBoost模型并进行概率校准，采用SHAP进行可解释性分析，并集成FAISS等检索方法完成政策检索，同时引入智能体路由与SQL分析基准。

Result: 校准后模型ROC-AUC为0.7109、PR-AUC为0.2993、F1为0.3714、准确率65.44%；Brier分数由0.2157降至0.1273，ECE由0.2862降至0.0585；SHAP特征排序在2017与2018年间Spearman相关为0.9959；FAISS在28条标注查询上Hit@1为0.929、MRR为0.964，三种检索方法Hit@5均为1.0；智能体路由准确率95.6%（45例中43例），SQL基准在六项案例上精确匹配、执行成功与结果匹配均为1.0。

Conclusion: CredWise能够在单一受控工作流中整合预测、解释、政策证据与结构化分析，但其为学术研究原型，最终决策仍由人工审核者作出。

Abstract: Credit-risk prediction is important in banking, but a prediction alone does not explain why an applicant is risky or how it should be combined with other evidence. This paper presents CredWise, a decision-support framework that integrates credit-risk prediction, probability calibration, explainable artificial intelligence, policy retrieval, SQL analytics, and controlled agent-based workflows. An XGBoost model is trained on Lending Club data (1,345,310 loans, 18 features) using a temporal split: 2007--2016 for training, 2017 for validation, and 2018 for testing. On the 2018 test set, the calibrated model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.7109, PR-AUC of 0.2993, F1-score of 0.3714, and accuracy of 65.44\%. Calibration reduced the Brier score from 0.2157 to 0.1273 and the expected calibration error from 0.2862 to 0.0585. SHAP explanations were temporally stable, with a Spearman correlation of 0.9959 between 2017 and 2018 feature rankings. On 28 labeled queries covering nine policy sections, FAISS achieved the best Hit@1 (0.929) and MRR (0.964), while all three retrieval methods reached Hit@5 = 1.0. Agent routing achieved 95.6\% accuracy (43 of 45 cases), and the SQL benchmark scored 1.0 on exact-match, execution-success, and result-match across six cases. These results show that CredWise can combine predictions, explanations, policy evidence, and structured analytics in one controlled workflow. It is an academic research prototype, and final decisions remain with a human reviewer.

</details>


### [329] [Follow the Entities: A Corpus Map for Agentic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37226)
*Soyeong Jeong,Sujay Kumar Jauhar,Sung Ju Hwang,Andrew Joohun Nam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CorpusMap是一种离线构建的导航层，围绕文档中可识别的反复出现的实体组织语料库，将每个实体表示为聚合信息并链接所有相关文档的Entity Page，形成实体-文档图供智能体遍历，从而提升证据发现与答案质量并降低token消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型文档集合上回答问题常需连接跨文档的分散证据（如项目审批、需求、最新状态分别记录在不同文档中）。现有LLM智能体采用迭代搜索整个语料库的方式，但扁平文件集合缺乏文档间关系指示，导致智能体每次查询都需重新发现这些关系，既容易遗漏互补证据，又消耗大量token。

Method: 提出CorpusMap导航层：围绕文档自身可识别的反复出现实体组织语料库。将每个实体表示为Entity Page，聚合并链接所有引用该实体的文档，构建实体与文档之间的图，供智能体遍历以收集原本孤立的证据。由于CorpusMap在离线阶段通过跨文档实体mention解析构建，其链接可在不同查询间共享，而非在推理时反复重新发现。

Result: 在3个基准数据集上使用7种不同模型进行实验，CorpusMap在证据发现和答案质量上均优于原始语料的智能体搜索，且平均使用更少token；同时优于4种替代导航层。

Conclusion: 实体可作为导航大型文档集合的有效锚点，CorpusMap通过离线实体解析与图结构共享链接，实现了更高效、更准确的跨文档证据发现。

Abstract: Answering questions and completing tasks over large document collections often requires connecting evidence spread across multiple documents, such as a project's approval recorded in one, its requirements in another, and its latest status in a third. Recent LLM agents approach this by iteratively searching the full corpus rather than reading only a fixed set of top-ranked documents. However, when the corpus is exposed only as a flat collection of files, a relevant document gives no indication of how it relates to others, so the agent must rediscover these relationships for every query, often missing complementary evidence while simultaneously consuming substantial additional tokens. To address this, we introduce CorpusMap, a navigation layer that organizes the corpus around its recurring entities, which are identifiable from the documents themselves and can link a single document to many others across sources. Specifically, CorpusMap represents each recurring entity as an Entity Page that aggregates information about it and links to every document that refers to it, forming a graph between entities and documents that the agent can traverse to gather otherwise disconnected evidence. Moreover, since CorpusMap is constructed offline by resolving mentions of the same entity across documents, its links are shared across queries rather than rediscovered repeatedly at inference time. Using 7 different models with 3 benchmark datasets, we show that CorpusMap improves both evidence discovery and answer quality over raw-corpus agentic search while using fewer tokens on average, and further outperforms 4 alternative navigation layers, suggesting that entities serve as effective anchors for navigating large document collections.

</details>


### [330] [SemOPT: Fixing Semantic Errors in LLM-based Optimization Modeling via Reward-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37361)
*Zetong Zhou,Wentao Zhang,Jingyuan Wang,Yifan Yang,Zizhuo Wang,Shixi Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SemOPT，一个语义引导的优化建模纠错框架，结合语义奖励模型与自适应纠错系统，解决LLM生成求解器代码中的语义错误，在七个基准上取得SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运筹学问题求解需从自然语言描述转为可执行求解器代码，LLM可自动化该过程但易出错。错误分为语法错误（无法运行或被判不可行）和语义错误（能返回目标值但违背原问题意图）。语义错误不触发运行时失败，难以检测和纠正，是亟需解决的问题。

Method: SemOPT框架包含两部分：一是语义奖励模型，用于区分忠实于原问题的数学建模与看似合理但错误的建模；二是自适应纠错系统，在建模空间上执行分层奖励引导的搜索。

Result: 在七个优化建模基准上的实验表明，SemOPT达到新的最优性能，在复杂数据集上相比最强基线平均提升7.6%的准确率。

Conclusion: 语义引导的建模纠错能有效缓解LLM优化建模中的隐蔽语义错误，显著提升自动化建模的可靠性与准确率。

Abstract: Operations research supports decision-making in domains such as energy, economics, and healthcare. Solving operations research problems typically begins with optimization modeling, which translates a natural-language problem description into executable solver code. LLMs offer a promising way to automate this process, but they remain prone to errors. In practice, these errors can be divided into two categories: syntactic errors refer to solver code that fails to run successfully or is judged infeasible by the solver; semantic errors refer to solver code that successfully returns an objective value but violates the intent of the original problem. Since semantic errors do not trigger runtime failures, they are difficult to detect and rectify. To address this problem, we introduce SemOPT, a semantic-guided framework for correcting LLM-based optimization models. SemOPT combines a semantic reward model that distinguishes faithful math models from plausible but incorrect ones with an adaptive correction system that applies hierarchical reward-guided search over the modeling space. Experiments on seven optimization modeling benchmarks show that SemOPT establishes a new state of the art and achieves an average 7.6% accuracy improvement over the strongest baseline on complex datasets.

</details>


### [331] [Compiling Learning Problems into Adaptation Programs for Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37371)
*Rebecca Ramnauth,Brian Scassellati*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出"适配编译"框架，将模型适配视为联合预测与决策问题，通过预测候选程序的反事实响应曲面，在适配开始前选择最优程序，实现适配搜索的摊销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型适配通常采用固定配方，但不同更新程序会产生显著不同的行为结果，固定配方难以应对不同学习情境下最优适配策略的差异。

Method: 引入适配编译，将"在哪适配、如何适配、适配程度"重构为联合预测与决策问题。编译器从以往适配中学习，预测候选程序在多维行为（习得、迁移、边界性、保持性）上的向量值反事实响应曲面，并在适配前选定程序。该预测几何可复用于不同下游优先级而无需重训。

Result: 在五种学习类型上，偏好程序随情境显著变化且可由适配前信息预测。在Llama-3.1-8B上，编译器所选程序接近穷举搜索且优于全局及目标特定默认策略。在Gemma-2-9B上的复现保持了程序异质性和选择空间，但表明利用该空间需在偏离强默认时考虑不确定性。

Conclusion: 适配搜索可跨相关学习问题摊销，将既有适配经验转化为决定未来学习方式的依据。

Abstract: Model adaptation is typically governed by a fixed recipe, even though different update programs can produce substantially different behavioral outcomes. We introduce adaptation compilation, which reframes where, how, and to what extent a model should adapt as a joint prediction and decision problem. Rather than searching over candidate programs anew for each learning episode, a compiler learns from prior adaptations to predict a vector-valued counterfactual response surface over candidate programs---their expected effects on acquisition, transfer, boundedness, and preservation---and selects a program before adaptation begins. Because this predicted geometry captures multiple behavioral consequences rather than a single winner or scalar score, it can be reused under different downstream priorities without retraining. Across five learning types, preferred programs vary meaningfully across episodes, and this variation is predictable from pre-adaptation information. On Llama-3.1-8B, compiler-selected programs approach exhaustive search while outperforming global and objective-specific defaults. Replication on Gemma-2-9B preserves program heterogeneity and selection headroom, but shows that exploiting this headroom requires accounting for uncertainty when departing from strong defaults. Together, these results show that adaptation search can be amortized across related learning problems, turning prior adaptation experience into a basis for deciding how future learning should occur.

</details>


### [332] [Look What You Made Us Cluster: Hate Narrative Extraction from Reddit Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37408)
*Annabelle K. L. Chua,Forster J. Khoo,Joel C. R. Tan,Huey Ting Ang,Kheng Hwee Tan,Joel Y. A. Sim,Shirley W. H. Ow,Ria Mundhra,Elsie C. K. Toh,Youfeng Xu,Lynnette H. X. Ng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种叙事抽取流水线，将网络仇恨叙事表示为"实体-评价对"，利用大语言模型推理扩展基于方面的情感分析来抽取叙事，再通过Leiden聚类与LLM引导的细化实现多粒度聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算式叙事抽取方法依赖语义表示，往往只能捕捉表层含义，导致精确度有限，难以支撑严格且可解释的仇恨叙事检测系统。

Method: 1）以"实体-评价对"形式表示叙事；2）用LLM推理流程扩展Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis：识别方面、判定其判断类型作为评价依据，并推导相应评价；3）用Leiden算法对抽取的叙事实体进行聚类；4）通过LLM引导的细化过程将聚类解析到目标粒度。

Result: 以2024年英文Reddit中批评Taylor Swift的评论为案例验证该流水线，分析了一个呈现仇恨言论模式的代表性聚类，展示了其解释价值。

Conclusion: 将叙事建模为实体-评价对并结合LLM推理与聚类细化，可提升叙事抽取的精确性与可解释性，为网络仇恨叙事的检测与理解提供更严谨的基础。

Abstract: Narrative extraction allows us to identify online hate narratives, supporting the construction of rigorous detection systems. Existing computational approaches, however, are limited in precision as they rely on semantic representations, which tend to capture only surface-level meaning. To detect more precise and interpretable narratives, we present an extraction pipeline that represents narratives as entity-evaluation pairs. Narratives are extracted using a Large Language Model (LLM) reasoning process that extends Aspect-Based Sentiment Analysis, identifying the aspect, classifying its judgement type as the basis for evaluation, and deriving the evaluation accordingly. Extracted narratives are then clustered using Leiden, following which clusters are resolved to an intended level of granularity through an LLM-guided refinement process. We illustrate this narrative pipeline with English Reddit comments from 2024 that criticize Taylor Swift, analyzing a representative cluster that exhibits hate speech patterns to demonstrate its interpretive value.

</details>


### [333] [Learning to Retrieve Missing Evidence for Long-Term Memory QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37443)
*Yi-Xuan Deng,Yi Zhang,Wei Liu,Chao Xue,Shuojin Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 MERA，将全局可搜索记忆与问题特定的证据状态分离，用强化学习训练轻量规划器，让检索决策基于已找到的证据，从而在长对话记忆任务上取得高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时记忆让语言模型能利用过去交互，但回答所需证据常分散在相隔很远的对话轮次中，且问题本身缺少定位证据的线索。已检索到的事实可以揭示这些线索，因此检索决策应当以已发现的证据为条件。

Method: MERA（Missing-Evidence Retrieval Augmentation）把全局可搜索记忆与问题特定的证据状态分离，已核实的证据引导后续检索，同时不限制对全局记忆的访问。通过强化学习训练一个轻量规划器，奖励那些能找回此前缺失证据的查询。

Result: 在 Qwen3-30B 和 GPT-4o-mini 骨干上均取得较强答案准确率。用 Qwen3-30B 做证据处理和答案生成时，训练后的 0.6B 规划器在 LoCoMo 上达 77.40%，在 LongMemEval-S 上达 71.29%，分别超出未做检索接地训练的 30B 规划器 4.10% 和 3.96%。在 LoCoMo 上，后续检索轮次将累计证据召回率从 55.5% 提升到 80.5%。

Conclusion: 以证据状态条件化的检索决策，配合强化学习训练的轻量规划器，能用小模型超越更大的无检索接地规划器，并通过多轮检索持续提升证据召回率。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables language models to use past interactions in future conversations. However, evidence needed to answer a question may be scattered across distant turns, while the question itself omits clues needed to locate it. Retrieved facts can reveal these clues, motivating retrieval decisions conditioned on evidence already found. We introduce MERA (Missing-Evidence Retrieval Augmentation), which separates globally searchable memory from a question-specific evidence state. Verified evidence guides subsequent retrieval without restricting access to the global memory. We train a lightweight planner through reinforcement learning, rewarding queries that recover previously missing evidence. MERA achieves strong answer accuracy across Qwen3-30B and GPT-4o-mini backbones. With Qwen3-30B for evidence processing and answer generation, the trained 0.6B planner achieves 77.40% accuracy on LoCoMo and 71.29% on LongMemEval-S, exceeding a 30B planner without retrieval-grounded training by 4.10% and 3.96%, respectively. On LoCoMo, later retrieval rounds increase cumulative evidence recall from 55.5% to 80.5%.

</details>


### [334] [Relevance Is Not Sufficient Evidence: Detecting Evidence Gaps Before Generation in RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37469)
*Suting Chen,Peichun Hua,Yunming Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出RINSE方法，通过三个信号组合在生成前判断检索证据是否充分，无需生成答案，在六个数据集上优于现有方法和前沿模型API。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG中检索到的段落常提及正确实体却缺乏回答所需事实，即使指令要求弃答，生成器仍会回答40.0-99.3%证据不足的问题。训练生成器弃答会将决策绑定到模型权重，可能奖励从参数化知识回忆的答案，且仍需完整生成器调用。因此希望在看答案之前，仅凭问题与证据判断充分性。

Method: 指出构建证据不足测试的陷阱（移除相关证据或将证据与无关问题配对可能通过词汇重叠或证据位置泄露标签），构建使用替换、删除和问题交换的配对基准，控制词使用等表面特征。提出RINSE，组合三个信号：问题各部分是否被覆盖、是否有段落提供答案、小型语言模型共同阅读段落判断是否充分。

Result: 在六个数据集上，RINSE将充分证据排在不足证据之上得分为0.837（随机为0.5），超过10种先前方法中最好的0.746以及通过API查询的前沿模型的0.784。其最弱数据集得分高于其他方法的最弱得分（0.684对0.676）。RINSE在生成前本地运行，单GPU每问题耗时36.5毫秒。

Conclusion: 证据充分性可在生成答案前判断，但无单一信号适用于所有数据集；RINSE通过多信号组合实现了优于现有方法和前沿模型API的性能，且计算开销低，可本地部署于生成之前。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) grounds large language models in external sources, but retrieved passages often name the right entities without providing the facts needed to answer. Even when instructed to abstain, 12 generators answer 40.0-99.3% of insufficient-evidence questions. Training generators to abstain ties the decision to model weights, may reward answers recalled from parametric knowledge, and still requires a full generator call. Can sufficiency be judged from the question and evidence alone, before any answer exists? We identify pitfalls in constructing insufficient-evidence tests: removing relevant evidence or pairing evidence with unrelated questions can reveal labels through lexical overlap or evidence position. We build a paired benchmark using substitution, deletion, and question-swap constructions that vary answer support while controlling selected surface features, such as word use. Sufficiency can be judged without generating an answer, but no single signal works across all datasets. We introduce RINSE (Relevance Is Not Sufficient Evidence), which combines three signals: whether every part of the question is covered, whether any passage offers an answer, and whether a small language model reading the passages together judges them sufficient. Across six datasets, RINSE ranks sufficient above insufficient evidence with a score of 0.837 (chance 0.5), exceeding the best of 10 prior methods (0.746) and a frontier model queried through an API (0.784). Its weakest dataset scores higher than any other method's weakest (0.684 vs. 0.676). RINSE runs locally before generation, taking 36.5 ms per question on a single GPU.

</details>


### [335] [Regime Boundary Alignment for Evidence-Gated Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37491)
*Zeyan Li,Qirong Guo,SIyuan Qiu,Hu Xu,Chun Li,Jianfeng Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Regime Boundary Alignment（RBA）训练方法，让检索增强语言模型在证据缺失时学会拒答，同时保持有支持证据时的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强语言模型本应依据检索到的证据作答，但实际中即使证据缺失仍常常强行作答。作者追溯该行为至训练信号：以答案为中心的微调对无支持上下文不设目标，无法区分"拒答的读者"与"猜测的读者"，导致无支持作答率在有支持准确率提升时仍接近100%。

Method: 提出Regime Boundary Alignment（RBA）：在同一问题与金答案的配对变体上训练单一reader。当上下文支持答案时（包括同时存在冲突证据的情形）输出金答案，当正确支持被移除时拒答。推理为标准解码，无需验证器、阈值或机制标签。

Result: 在三个多跳QA数据集、三个随机种子上，相较以冲突为中心的训练，RBA将无支持作答率降低超过60个百分点，同时匹配其有支持准确率。在留出的TriviaQA检索未命中切片上，同一reader将无支持作答率从100%降至1%以下，并同时提升有支持准确率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，证据门控式作答必须在支持边界的"两侧"同时学习，即需在有支持与无支持两种情形下都进行训练。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented language models are expected to answer from the retrieved evidence, but in practice they often keep answering when that evidence is missing. We trace this behavior to the training signal: answer-focused fine-tuning assigns no target to unsupported contexts, so it cannot distinguish a reader that abstains from one that guesses, and unsupported answering stays near 100% even as supported accuracy improves. We introduce Regime Boundary Alignment (RBA), which trains a single reader on matched variants of the same question and gold answer. The reader is trained to produce the gold answer when the context supports it, including when conflicting evidence is also present, and to abstain when the correct support is removed; inference is ordinary decoding, with no verifier, threshold, or regime label. On three multi-hop QA datasets across three seeds, RBA reduces the unsupported-answer rate by more than sixty percentage points relative to conflict-focused training while matching its supported accuracy. On a held-out TriviaQA retrieval-miss slice, the same reader reduces unsupported answering from 100% to below 1% while also improving supported accuracy. These results indicate that evidence-gated answering must be learned on both sides of the support boundary.

</details>


### [336] [Your Benchmark Is Not Saturated: Reviving Multiple-Choice Evaluation with Answer Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37494)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Abobaker Ahmed,Nawaf Barebood,Hussain Bu Subayt,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 AnswerPool：把同一语境下 N 道题的选项合并为一个选项池，让模型为每道题分配答案，无需新写题目或改标签，从而重新压榨已饱和的多选题基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多选题基准打分便宜但易饱和，而"写更难的新题"这一标准做法既慢又需对每个基准重复进行；同时每题的错误选项是单独编写的，模型可以通过排除少数选项来得分，即格式本身给予了"排除红利"。

Method: 将共享同一语境的 N 道题的所有选项汇集成一个选项池，要求模型为每道题分配其答案；不新写题目、不改变标签。通过从池中删除答案使部分问题不可回答且无精确真值，从而在同一次作答中对"弃答"进行评分。

Result: 五个四选项问题时，猜对整组的概率从 10^-3 降至 5×10^-7；在八个文本、图像、视频基准和十八个模型上，合并对所有模型都更难，弱模型的排除红利最大，且八个开放权重模型中有七个对 87% 至 100% 的不可回答问题给出了答案。

Conclusion: AnswerPool 无需新题即可恢复基准难度，并以"合并后损失的成绩"量化多选题格式曾提供的排除红利；弃答评分可与主任务同步完成；该方法揭示弱模型依赖排除、开放权重模型普遍不愿弃答。

Abstract: Multiple-choice benchmarks are cheap to grade and are running out of room, and the standard remedy, writing harder items, is slow and repeated for every benchmark. A saturated benchmark still holds a harder task. Each question's wrong options are written for that question alone, so a model can score by eliminating a few options. We propose AnswerPool: take $N$ questions that share a context, pool all their options into one list, and ask the model to assign every question its answer. No item is written and no label changes. The chance of guessing a group right falls from $10^{-3}$ to $5\times10^{-7}$ for five four-option questions, and a model that recognizes its answers keeps its multiple-choice score, so the accuracy lost to pooling measures the credit the format gave for elimination. Deleting answers from the pool makes questions unanswerable with exact ground truth, so abstention is scored in the same pass. Across eight text, image, and video benchmarks and eighteen models, pooling is harder for every model, the elimination credit is largest for the weakest models, and seven of eight open-weight models answer 87 to 100% of unanswerable questions.

</details>


### [337] [Larry Caused the Car to Stop, But the Model Didn't Notice: Transformer Blindness to the M-Heuristic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37497)
*Stefania Butnaru,Claudiu Creanga*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文检验编码器式Transformer（DeBERTa、RoBERTa、BART）是否在默认推理中运用M-Heuristic语用原则，结果发现它们未能捕捉词汇使役与迂说使役的语用差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代Transformer在语义关系上表现优异，但其语用推理能力研究不足；作者希望探究编码器式模型是否内化了新格赖斯M-Heuristic（标记形式蕴含标记意义）这一语用原则。

Method: 以自然语言推理框架对比词汇使役（如"Larry stopped the car"）与迂说使役（如"Larry caused the car to stop"），覆盖188种条件、15个双及物动词；对DeBERTa、RoBERTa、BART进行实验，辅以探针分析和控制实验，并对30组三元组做语义相似度分析，同时用显式元语言框架测试Gemini Flash-Lite。

Result: 三种编码器模型均未见捕捉该语用区分，DeBERTa在100%案例中预测为"Neutral"；探针最初疑似表示-使用分离，但控制实验表明其追踪的是句法复杂度而非使役语用；语义相似度显示29/30案例中迂说使役更接近无中介方式描述，与M-Heuristic预测相反；Gemini Flash-Lite在显式元语言框架下达到100%。

Conclusion: 该语用原则在指令条件下可用，但在默认NLI中未被使用，表明编码器式Transformer缺乏对M-Heuristic的默认语用推理能力。

Abstract: Modern transformer models excel at capturing semantic relationships through sentence embeddings, yet their ability to perform pragmatic reasoning remains understudied. This paper investigates whether encoder-based transformers such as DeBERTa employ the M-Heuristic (the neo-Gricean principle that marked linguistic forms implicate marked meanings). We test this hypothesis by contrasting lexical causatives (e.g., ``Larry stopped the car'') with periphrastic causatives (e.g., ``Larry caused the car to stop'') using a Natural Language Inference framework. Our experiments across 188 conditions with 15 ambitransitive verbs reveal that DeBERTa, RoBERTa, and BART show no evidence of capturing the pragmatic distinction between these forms, with DeBERTa predicting ``Neutral'' for 100% of cases. Probing analysis initially suggested a representation-use dissociation, but control experiments reveal the probe was tracking syntactic complexity, not causative pragmatics. Semantic similarity over 30 triplets places periphrastic causatives closer to unmediated manner descriptions in 29/30 cases, opposite to M-Heuristic predictions in the embedding space. Under explicit metalinguistic framing, Gemini Flash-Lite reaches 100% with item-specific traces, so the principle is available under instruction yet unused in default NLI.

</details>


### [338] [The Rashomon Wikipedia: A Data-Perspectivist Analysis of Divergent Historical Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37498)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu,Anca Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过五语种维基百科对罗马尼亚三个争议历史事件的分析，揭示跨语言叙事存在"引用隔离"，并提出"Peace-Maker"流程以实现冲突调和。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维基百科宣称提供统一中立的歷史记录，但其各语言版本常形成不同认知共同体，围绕争议事件产生分歧叙事，跨语言史学偏见的成因与演变尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 选取罗马尼亚语、匈牙利语、俄语、土耳其语、英语五个语种的维基百科条目，聚焦波萨达战役（1330）、苏联占领比萨拉比亚（1940）、特尔戈维什泰夜袭（1462）三个争议事件；结合人工标注者与大语言模型对引用立场进行分类，并量化2005至2024年的叙事演变，开展纵向分析；最后提出自动化的"Peace-Maker"调和流程。

Result: 发现"引用隔离"现象：波萨达战役119条引用中仅2条为各语言版本共享，罗马尼亚语版本呈91%的亲本国偏见，而匈牙利语版本较为平衡。纵向分析显示叙事具有波动性并受当代地缘政治影响，如2024年俄语对比萨拉比亚的表述出现显著转变。标准提示会使模型臆造共识，而"对抗式"提示（明确要求保留并归属分歧）可实现近乎完美的中立性得分。

Conclusion: 跨语言维基百科在争议历史事件上存在显著叙事分歧与引用隔离，且叙事随现实地缘政治动态变化；通过"对抗式"提示的"Peace-Maker"流程可有效保留分歧并提升中立性，为自动化冲突调和提供可行路径。

Abstract: Wikipedia aims to provide a unified, neutral record of history, yet its independent language editions often function as distinct epistemic communities, creating divergent narratives around contested events. This paper investigates cross-lingual historiographical bias by analyzing Wikipedia articles across five languages (Romanian, Hungarian, Russian, Turkish, and English) focusing on three contentious events in Romanian history: the Battle of Posada (1330), the Soviet occupation of Bessarabia (1940), and the Night Attack at Targoviste (1462). Using human annotators and Large Language Models (LLMs) to classify citation stance and quantify narrative evolution from 2005 to 2024, we identify a phenomenon of "citation isolation". In the case of the Battle of Posada, only 2 out of 119 citations were shared between language editions, with the Romanian edition exhibiting a 91% pro-national bias compared to the balanced Hungarian edition. Longitudinal analysis reveals that these narratives are volatile and responsive to contemporary geopolitics, evidenced by a significant shift in the Russian framing of Bessarabia in 2024. Finally, we propose a "Peace-Maker" pipeline to automate conflict reconciliation. We demonstrate that while standard prompting leads models to hallucinate consensus, "adversarial" prompting, which explicitly instructs the model to preserve and attribute disagreement, achieves near-perfect neutrality scores.

</details>


### [339] [Who Warmed the Archives? LLMs Overestimate Historical Warmth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37499)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 历史档案可延伸器测气候记录，LLM 有望自动提取气候学家手工推导的 Pfister 温度指数。但研究发现：词汇方法优于所有微调历史 Transformer，且六个 LLM 均存在随年代递增的暖偏差，仅靠相关系数不足以评估 LLM 能否用作历史气候指数工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 历史档案是延伸器测气候记录的未充分利用来源，气候学家手工从中推导指数成本高，LLM 提供了自动提取的可能。然而，仅凭提取精度或相关系数无法判断模型误差是否可用于跨世纪比较——高相关并不排除与年代相关的系统性偏差。

Method: 在跨越五个世纪的德语文本上，针对 Pfister 温度指数，比较三类方法：词汇基线、微调的历史 Transformer（包括一个从零在历史德语上预训练的模型），以及多种 LLM 提示（Gemini 2.5 Flash、GPT-5-mini、DeepSeek v4 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Qwen3.7-Plus、Kimi-K2.6-Fast）。此外做消融实验，从引文中剥离显式日期和年代标记，检验暖偏差是否源于对年代的推断。

Result: 词汇方法优于所有被测微调 Transformer（其中一个从零预训练的模型 r=-0.016）。六个 LLM 均表现出随日历年增长的暖偏差，方向一致（斜率 +0.13 至 +0.34/世纪，p<0.01）；效应量不大（r²约 0.01–0.05），但在六个独立开发的模型中一致。相关性最好的 Gemini 2.5 Flash 达到最佳词汇相关（r=0.32），但误差翻倍。剥离日期与年代标记后趋势基本不变，说明偏差来自模型“以今度古”的先验，而非正确推断引文年代。

Conclusion: 仅凭相关系数不足以将 LLM 认证为历史气候指数的可靠工具；评估时必须考虑与年代相关的系统性偏差，LLM 的误差不可直接用于跨世纪比较。

Abstract: Historical archives are an under-used source for extending the instrumental climate record backward in time, and LLMs offer a way to extract the indices climatologists derive by hand. Beyond measuring how well systems extract this signal, we check whether their errors are safe to use for cross-century comparison, since a good correlation score does not rule out systematic, era-linked bias. Comparing lexical baselines, fine-tuned historical transformers, and LLM prompting on the Pfister temperature index across five centuries of German text, lexical methods beat every fine-tuned transformer we test, including one pretrained from scratch on historical German (r=-0.016). All six LLMs we test (Gemini 2.5 Flash, GPT-5-mini, DeepSeek v4 Flash, Claude Sonnet 4.6, Qwen3.7-Plus, Kimi-K2.6-Fast) show a warm bias that grows with calendar year, with the same sign in every model (slopes +0.13 to +0.34/century, p<0.01). The effect is modest in size (r-squared approx equal to 0.01 to 0.05) but consistent across six independently developed models. The best-correlated of the six, Gemini 2.5 Flash, matches the best lexical correlation (r=0.32) at double the error. An ablation stripping explicit dates and calendar-era markers from the quotes leaves this trend essentially unchanged, favoring an anachronistic present-day prior over the model correctly inferring the quote's era. Correlation alone is thus insufficient for vetting an LLM as a historical-climate-index oracle.

</details>


### [340] [Evaluating Bounded Autonomy in Regulated Agentic AI: A Diagnostic Harness with Constitutional Rewards, Escalation Labels, and Runtime Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37501)
*Dipankar Sarkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 RegLLM 诊断测试框架，用于评估受监管智能体工作流中的"有限自主性"，通过六个可信度信号和确定性运行时监督器进行度量；小规模实验显示治理机制可提升升级召回率并降低不安全动作率，但配置方差可能掩盖微调效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受监管场景下的智能体需要在"行动"与"上报/延迟决策"之间做出可靠判断，但现有评测缺乏对有限自主性（bounded autonomy）的系统化、可测量的诊断手段，尤其缺少能将领域宪法同时用于评估、训练奖励与服务防护的统一框架。

Method: 提出 RegLLM 诊断框架，监测六类可信度信号：引用有效性、来源落地性、模式合规性、升级正确性、宪法对齐性和不安全动作率。信号按监督来源分为程序化验证器、任务级升级标签和 AI 评判分数。使用确定性运行时监督器拦截无依据回答并强制升级，并记录干预行为。同一领域宪法贯穿评估、训练奖励与服务防护；任务级"应升级"标签使"行动 vs 延迟"成为可测量的训练信号。以 smoke 规模进行验证：一个离线参考运行（n=12）和两个单 GPU Qwen2.5-3B LoRA/DPO 试点（n=8，相同随机种子与评估划分）。

Result: 离线参考运行（n=12）中，启用治理后升级召回率从 0 提升至 0.67，不安全动作率从 0.33 降至 0.08。两个名义上相同的 RL-base 配置试点差异显著：任务成功率 0.25 对 0.12，升级召回率 1.0 对 0.5。答案质量适配器在 Run A 中使召回从 1.0 变 0.5，而在 Run B 中从 0.5 变 1.0；升级感知变体在 Run B 中无可测变化。

Conclusion: 这些小规模试点无法确立可靠的适配器效果或生产可用性。其贡献在于诊断价值：配置方差可能压倒对有限自主性指标的表观调优效果。因此需要更大规模评估集与多次重复运行才能得出可靠结论。

Abstract: We propose RegLLM, a diagnostic harness for bounded autonomy in regulated agentic workflows. It instruments six trustworthiness signals: citation validity, source grounding, schema compliance, escalation correctness, constitutional alignment, and unsafe-action rate. Signals are distinguished by their source of supervision: programmatic verifiers, task-level escalation labels, or AI-judge scores. A deterministic runtime supervisor blocks ungrounded answers and forces escalation, logging interventions. The same domain constitution informs evaluation, training rewards, and serving guardrails. Task-level should-escalate labels make the act-versus-defer decision a measurable training signal. We demonstrate the harness at smoke scale. An offline reference run (n=12) lifts escalation recall from 0 to 0.67 and reduces unsafe-action rate from 0.33 to 0.08 when governance is enabled. Two single-GPU Qwen2.5-3B LoRA/DPO pilots (n=8, same seed and evaluation split) expose substantial variation: nominally identical RL-base configurations yield task success of 0.25 versus 0.12 and escalation recall of 1.0 versus 0.5. An answer-quality adapter changes recall from 1.0 to 0.5 in Run A, but from 0.5 to 1.0 in Run B. An escalation-aware variant produces no measurable change in Run B. These small pilots do not establish reliable adapter effects or production readiness. Their contribution is diagnostic: configuration variance can overwhelm apparent tuning effects on bounded-autonomy metrics, motivating larger evaluation sets and repeated runs.

</details>


### [341] [From Dissonance to Orchestration: Teacher Intervention in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37510)
*Yuhao Wang,Ruiyang Ren,Yinan Zhang,Ruiqing Zhang,Jing Liu,Chunyan Miao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究on-policy distillation中教师干预的深度与位置问题，发现单纯的rollout质量不足以指导干预分配，据此提出MAESTRO方法，利用局部策略分歧自适应决定教师何时接管与生成长度，在八个数学推理基准上取得最高宏平均准确率并大幅缩短训练回复长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在on-policy distillation中，学生在自身推理轨迹上接受更强教师的反馈。教师干预虽能改善轨迹质量，却也改变了学生学习所依赖的分布。作者通过受控实验发现：仅用rollout质量作为分配教师指导的标准是不完整的——更深的干预在提升rollout准确率上收益递减，却增加了off-policy负荷；在受限rollout horizon的训练探针中，峰值学生准确率与性能保持所偏好的干预强度并不一致；且偏好的干预深度与位置在不同基准间也存在差异。因此需要一个能自适应调整干预时机与长度的机制。

Method: 提出MAESTRO方法，利用局部策略分歧（local policy disagreement）联合自适应地决定教师何时接管以及生成多长时间。其策略分歧分数（policy disagreement score）结合了教师加权的候选覆盖度与局部分布相似性，并在推理段落（reasoning paragraphs）内进行聚合。

Result: 在八个数学推理基准上，MAESTRO在0.6B和1.7B两种Qwen3学生模型上均取得所比较方法中最高的宏平均准确率，其中1.7B学生在每个基准上都领先。此外，相对于标准OPD，MAESTRO将平均训练回复长度减少了67.3%。

Conclusion: 教师干预的深度与位置需要自适应分配而非仅依据rollout质量，基于局部策略分歧的MAESTRO能够有效提升学生准确率并显著压缩训练回复长度，代码已开源。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own reasoning trajectories using feedback from a stronger teacher. Teacher interventions can improve these trajectories, but also change the distribution on which the student learns. Our controlled studies show that rollout quality alone is an incomplete criterion for allocating teacher guidance. Deeper intervention yields diminishing gains in rollout accuracy while increasing off-policy load. In a training probe with a restricted rollout horizon, peak student accuracy and performance retention favor different intervention strengths. The preferred intervention depth and placement also vary across benchmarks. These findings motivate MAESTRO, which uses local policy disagreement to jointly adapt when the teacher takes over and how long it generates. Its {policy disagreement score} combines teacher-weighted candidate coverage with local distribution similarity and is aggregated within reasoning paragraphs. Across eight mathematical reasoning benchmarks, MAESTRO achieves the highest macro-average accuracy among the compared methods for both 0.6B and 1.7B Qwen3 students, with the 1.7B student leading on every benchmark. MAESTRO also reduces average training response length by 67.3\% relative to standard OPD. The code is available at https://github.com/yhao-wang/MAESTRO.

</details>


### [342] [E-MoE: Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts for Non-Factorized Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37533)
*Arseny Ivanov,Alexander Kolesov,Alexander Korotin,Ivan Oseledets,Mikhail Goncharov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出增强混合专家（E-MoE），通过在反向过程中引入离散共享潜变量（由MoE骨干的路由决策给出），构建因子化分布的混合，在不增加激活参数的前提下提升少步生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 掩码扩散模型（MDMs）的反向过程通常在位置维度上因子化，导致在少步采样（扩散速度优势最明显）时样本质量受限。现有引入连续高斯潜变量的变分自编码器方法易发生后验塌缩，潜变量被忽略。

Method: 提出E-MoE，将反向过程建模为因子化分布的混合，其混合成分由MoE骨干的专家路由决策构成的离散共享潜变量给出，且不增加活跃参数量。

Result: 在合成多模态基准、二值化MNIST和LM1B上，E-MoE在少步生成上优于因子化基线。

Conclusion: 利用MoE路由决策作为离散潜变量可有效捕捉跨位置相关性，避免后验塌缩，提升少步扩散生成质量且保持参数效率。

Abstract: Masked diffusion models (MDMs) generate sequences by progressively unmasking several tokens per denoising step, but their reverse process is typically factorized over positions, limiting sample quality in the few-step regime where diffusion's speed advantage over autoregressive decoding matters most. A recent line of work introduces a continuous Gaussian latent, trained as a variational autoencoder, to capture correlations across positions, but such approaches are prone to posterior collapse, where the latent is silently ignored. We propose Enhanced Mixture-of-Experts (E-MoE), which builds the reverse process as a mixture of factorized distributions over a discrete shared latent given by the expert-routing decisions of a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) backbone, without increasing active parameters over the factorized baseline. Across synthetic multi-modal benchmarks, binarized MNIST, and LM1B, E-MoE improves few-step generation over factorized baselines.

</details>


### [343] [RunyaNER: Auxiliary Language Selection for Runyankore NER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37543)
*Prosper Arineitwe Asiimwe,Francois Meyer,Jan Buys*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出首个东非语言Runyankore的NER基准RunyaNER（237k+标注词、30k句），并基于该基准系统研究跨语言零样本迁移和多语言微调中辅助语言选择策略，发现基于嵌入的度量比传统语言类型学特征更能预测迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源语言的NER常依赖跨语言零样本迁移或多语言微调，但在缺乏目标语言基准的情况下，何种辅助语言选择策略能带来最佳迁移效果尚不明确；同时Runyankore此前缺乏公开的NER评测资源。

Method: 以半自动流水线构建并完全人工校验的方式创建RunyaNER基准；在其上对预训练模型进行评测，验证数据集质量与规模；进而设计跨语言零样本迁移与多语言微调实验，比较不同辅助语言选择策略，并将基于标注训练片段计算的嵌入度量与传统基于元数据或类型学的语言学特征进行对照分析。

Result: RunyaNER包含逾237k标注词、30k句子；基准评测表明该数据集规模与质量足以训练出有效的Runyankore NER模型；迁移性能对辅助语言选择高度敏感，且基于嵌入的度量与下游迁移性能的相关性强于传统语言学特征。

Conclusion: 通过发布RunyaNER并系统分析辅助语言选择策略，本工作既提供了新的基准资源，也为低资源场景下的多语言迁移提供了实用洞见。

Abstract: Cross-lingual zero-shot transfer and multilingual fine-tuning are promising approaches for NLP tasks such as Named Entity Recognition (NER) in low-resource languages, but in the absence of target language benchmarks, it is unclear which auxiliary language selection strategy leads to the best transfer. We introduce RunyaNER, the first publicly available NER benchmark for the East African language Runyankore, and use it to investigate the choice of which languages to use for transfer. Created with a semi-automated pipeline and fully manually verified, RunyaNER contains over 237k annotated words across 30k sentences. We benchmark pretrained models on RunyaNER, establishing that our dataset is of sufficient quality and size to produce effective Runyankore NER models. We then use RunyaNER to investigate auxiliary language selection in cross-lingual zero-shot and multilingual fine-tuning settings. Our experiments show that while transfer performance is highly sensitive to auxiliary language selection, embedding-based measures computed from labelled training spans correlate more strongly with downstream transfer performance than traditional linguistic features based on metadata or typology. By releasing RunyaNER and providing a systematic analysis of auxiliary language selection strategies, this work contributes both a new benchmark resource and practical insights for multilingual transfer in low-resource settings.

</details>


### [344] [Orthogonal Yet Coupled: Decoupling Geometric Components for Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37564)
*Zijing Wang,Yongkang Liu,Mingyang Wang,Ercong Nie,Mengjie Zhao,Yunpu Ma,Kang Liu,Zihan Wang,Shi Feng,Daling Wang,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DiGA框架，将任务向量正交分解为不同几何属性的分量并分别合并，避免跨分量耦合，提升模型合并性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并方法把每个任务向量视为不可分割的合并单元，忽略了其中编码的异质几何变化，导致跨分量耦合：由完整任务向量统计导出的合并决策会让某分量的几何特征影响其他分量的选择、加权或组合，从而降低合并模型质量。

Method: DiGA以预训练权重作为共享几何参考，将每个任务向量正交分解为对应不同几何属性的分量；不在整体层面合并任务向量，而是在各自子空间内独立聚合对应分量，再重组为统一更新。该分量级公式保留每个分量的几何身份，防止分量间干扰，并可嵌入现有多种模型合并方法。

Result: 在多种模型、任务和合并方法上的大量实验表明，DiGA提升了合并模型的性能并减少了能力退化。代码仓库：https://github.com/wzj1718/DiGA。

Conclusion: 通过解耦几何感知的分量级合并，DiGA有效缓解跨分量耦合问题，是一种通用且可插拔的模型合并增强框架。

Abstract: Merging pretrained models has emerged as an effective approach for consolidating diverse capabilities into a single unified model. However, prevailing merging methods typically treat each task vector as an indivisible merging unit, overlooking the heterogeneous geometric changes encoded within it. This treatment can induce cross-component coupling: when merging decisions are derived from statistics of the complete task vector, the geometric characteristics of one component may influence how another is selected, weighted, or combined, potentially degrading the quality of the merged model. To address this issue, we propose DiGA, a
Disentangled Geometry-Aware model merging framework. Using the pretrained weights as a shared geometric reference, DiGA orthogonally decomposes each task vector into components corresponding to distinct geometric attributes. Rather than merging the task vectors as a whole, DiGA aggregates corresponding components independently within their respective subspaces and subsequently recombines them into a unified update. This component-wise formulation preserves the geometric identity of each component and prevents the characteristics of one component from interfering with the aggregation of another. Furthermore, DiGA can be incorporated into a broad range of existing model merging methods. Extensive experiments across diverse models, tasks, and merging methods demonstrate that DiGA improves merged-model performance and reduces capability degradation. Our repository is on https://github.com/wzj1718/DiGA.

</details>


### [345] [Devils in Question Relay: Source-Conditioned Relay Steering to Mitigate Hallucinations in Audio-visual Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37568)
*Yu Zhang,Pingrui Zhang,Xuefeng Bai,Pengfei Zhang,Yang Xiang,Kehai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了AVLLM中"源混淆接地幻觉"的成因，发现"问题中继"机制，并提出免训练方法SECRET来缓解跨模态干扰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AVLLM虽在多模态理解上进展显著，但存在"源混淆接地幻觉"问题：未使用模态的线索会诱导出所需模态并不支持的回复，损害实际应用可靠性。现有方法虽有缓解，但对这一失败源自内部跨模态交互的机制理解不足。

Method: 通过路径干预与表征分析，揭示"问题中继"机制：问题状态携带干扰线索与所需源证据。据此提出免训练方法SECRET（源条件中继引导），利用不同模态路径干预得到的对比问题表征，将原始问题状态引导向所需源证据。

Result: 在CMM和AVHBench两个基准、三种AVLLM上，SECRET持续优于先前免训练方法，显著缓解源混淆接地幻觉（相比基模型最高提升+18.0和+7.1个百分点）。模态特定描述任务进一步验证了其在开放式生成上的泛化性。

Conclusion: SECRET通过干预问题中继有效缓解跨模态干扰，为AVLLM的可靠接地提供了一种免训练且具泛化性的解决方案。

Abstract: Audio-visual large language models (AVLLMs) have made remarkable progress in multimodal understanding and reasoning through interactions among visual, auditory, and linguistic information. However, recent studies show that AVLLMs face a critical challenge: $\textbf{source-confused grounding hallucination}$, where cues from the unused modality induce responses that the required modality does not support, undermining reliability in real-world applications. Existing methods have made progress in mitigating this failure, yet how it arises from internal cross-modal interactions remains insufficiently understood. To address this gap, we conduct path-intervention and representation analyses, revealing a $\textbf{question-relay}$ mechanism: question states carry interfering cues alongside required-source evidence, undermining grounding in required-modality evidence. Cutting pathways from interfering modality to question states yields greater correct-answer logit recovery than cutting those to the generation position. Motivated by these findings, we propose $\textbf{SECRET}$ ($\textbf{S}$ourc$\textbf{E}$-$\textbf{C}$onditioned $\textbf{RE}$lay s$\textbf{T}$eering), a training-free method that mitigates cross-modal interference at the question relay. Using contrasting question representations elicited through different modality-pathway interventions, SECRET steers the original question states toward required-source evidence. Experiments on two widely adopted benchmarks CMM and AVHBench across three AVLLMs show that SECRET consistently outperforms prior training-free methods, substantially mitigating source-confused grounding hallucinations (e.g., up to +18.0 and +7.1 percentage points over base models). Modality-specific captioning further demonstrates its generalizability to open-ended generation.

</details>


### [346] [Pair Difficulty Matters: Rethinking Pairwise LLM-as-a-Judge Evaluation and Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37577)
*Bruno Brocai,Maria Becker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有用位置偏差、传递性、成对一致率三项代理指标评估LLM评审可靠性的做法具有误导性：这些指标被"排名差距小"的样本对主导，而这部分不一致在信息论上是预期的且对聚合排名贡献极小；真正承载排名信号的"大差距"样本对却几乎不影响代理指标。作者形式化论证并在模拟与两个人类标注语料上验证，建议改用排名差距条件化的指标并以人类排名为基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评审被广泛用于成对比较排序，其可靠性通常由位置偏差、传递性和一致率三项代理指标衡量，并据此进行评审模型选择与基准评测。论文指出，大量研究报告"LLM评审在这些指标上表现不佳"，可能误导实践者放弃本可用的评审模型，因此需要重新审视这套评估逻辑。

Method: 在Bradley-Terry成对聚合几何下，将三项代理指标的影响分解到不同排名差距的样本对上，形式化论证代理指标被close-rank-gap样本对主导、而排名信号集中在far-gap样本对。随后在受控模拟以及两个人类评分语料上进行验证，检验代理指标与排序准确率的相关性及其预测成分的分布。

Result: 模拟与人类语料实验均显示：三项代理指标与针对金标准的排序准确率仅呈弱相关，其预测能力主要集中于far-gap区域；close-gap区域的不一致虽拉低代理指标，但对聚合排名影响甚微。

Conclusion: 当前基于三项代理指标的评审可靠性评估存在系统性偏差，不应据此否定评审模型。应改用排名差距条件化的评估指标，并尽可能以人类排名作为参照基准。

Abstract: Large Language Model judges are widely used to rank texts and text-generating systems through pairwise comparison, and their reliability is typically assessed via three proxies: position bias, transitivity, and pairwise agreement (self- or human-labeled). Because these proxies drive judge selection and benchmarking, a substantial literature reporting that judges perform poorly on them risks steering practitioners away from otherwise capable evaluators. We argue this assessment is misleading. Under the Bradley--Terry geometry underlying pairwise aggregation, each proxy is dominated by close-rank-gap pairs, where inconsistency is information-theoretically expected and individual verdicts contribute little to the aggregate ranking; far-gap pairs carry the ranking signal but barely move the proxies.
